# Owner Material 023 — KOTOR I, KOTOR II and The Old Republic: Revan: notes from the other agent (reference copy, reviewed) — part 8 of 11

# KOTOR 2: super-deep character write-ups (Oct 19, 2026)

## Atton Rand
*Corpus coverage: 678 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 19 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 11 pronoun references resolved from context · 120 lines addressed to them · 68 narration lines · 21 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Atton is a Republic soldier turned Sith Jedi-hunter. «Served in both of them.» [K2/Atton]. «I was in one of the special units trained to do this.» [K2/Atton]. A captured Jedi woman showed him the Force and died protecting his secret [K2/Atton].
### Personality and motivations
Snark masking guilt. He hides his thoughts behind walls of emotion: «It's hard for Jedi to sense what you're really thinking if you throw up walls of strong emotions» [K2/Atton].
### Arc (scene by scene)
- **Peragus:** cell, then pilot.
- **Telos:** threatened by Kreia.
- **Planets:** loyalty grows.
- **Influence path:** confesses his past; can be trained as a Jedi.
- **Secret Tomb:** clash with Kreia.
- **Rebuilt Enclave:** explains the Handmaiden's defection.
- **Malachor:** Traya's prophecy: «Atton shall keep his murderer's heart» (variant) [K2/Trayus_Core].
### Relationships
- Exile.
- Kreia (fear and hatred).
- Disciple.
- Mira (banter).
### Abilities
- Pilot.
- Jedi-killing tactics: «you wound the Padawan first» [K2/Atton].
- Latent Force sensitivity.
### Feats
- Killed Jedi, reported [K2/Atton].
### Power-scaling statements
- On Revan: «Revan wanted to break them. And then have them join him.» [K2/Atton]
### Duels and fights
- Fights his own battles on Malachor; outcomes are in the narration bank.
### Verdict implications
- Tactics-based. No raw power.
### Open questions
- The identity of the Jedi woman is unnamed.

### Abilities: game mechanics (FLAGGED: gameplay stats, not lore)
- No NPC stat block on the wiki (party member or player character; party stats live on the class pages).

**[K2/Administration_Level]**
- «Nice outfit - what, you miners change regulation uniforms while I've been in here?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Are you an angel? Aw, I'm just kidding. That's the worst line I've ever used. Hope some poor kid doesn't start using it.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Nice outfit - what, you miners change regulation uniform while I've been in here?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Security claimed I violated some trumped-up regulation or another - take it up with them if you want, but they stopped listening to me shortly before they stopped feeding me. Now that's criminal.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Atton... Atton Rand. Excuse me if I don't shake hands. The field only causes mild electrical burns.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I'm Atton. I actually wasn't supposed to make it into the final game, but I was created at the last minute. Blame my agent. I was actually slated for a spin-off to Jedi Knight, but I don't want to talk about what happened *there.*» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «You mean you didn't come here on purpose? I'm shocked, I really am. This slice of paradise is the Peragus mining facility, the only supplier of shipping-grade engine fuel to this corner of the galaxy. Peragus fuel plays havoc with engines, but it gets the job done... as long as you don't mind the toxic byproducts and trying to mine it without blowing yourself up.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Yeah - this asteroid belt is one giant minefield. One proton torpedo, even a stray blaster shot, can start an explosion that'll make the one that shattered Peragus II look like a kid's pop detonator.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «You know the planet with the exposed core you saw flying in? That hole was caused by the first mining station that tried to siphon fuel off the planet. Blew a whole chunk out of the planet, and set it drifting out here in a big clump of fuel-cooled asteroids. So the miners drill the asteroids now, not the planet's surface. That's why they don't allow blasters here. Can't trust a miner jumped up on juma juice not to fire one stray shot that will turn the entire colony into a thermal detonator.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «[[../Administration Level#Locked up» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «You mean, before or after that Jedi showed up? Either way, it's a real short story. See, some of the miners get it into their ferrocrete skulls that since the Jedi's unconscious, they can collect the bounty the Exchange has posted for live Jedi. Well, what passes for the law here didn't like that idea, so the two groups started fighting.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Then there was some big explosion, I was sitting here for a long time, then you showed up in your underwear and things got a lot better.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Then there was some big explosion, and then I was sitting here for a long time, waiting for some half-naked miner to show up and ask a bunch of questions.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «The Exchange is a big crime outfit, mostly operate out of Nar Shaddaa: spice running, gun trafficking, slave trading. Now I guess they're posting bounties on Jedi.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Don't know much about it. Maybe the Exchange wants one as a trophy, or somebody's got something against Jedi and is looking to collect. Not many Jedi left... wouldn't surprise me if the bounty's pretty high.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Like I said, I don't know too much about it. Could be something personal... or just business. Either way, there aren't many Jedi left to do anything about it.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I doubt it. No, it was war that thinned the Jedi, not the Exchange.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «The ones that weren't killed in the Jedi Civil War ended up switching off the lightsabers long ago. Word is, there's not even a Jedi Council anymore, but who knows?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Yeah, Revan, Malak, and the Jedi that went to join them in the Mandalorian Wars. They turned against the other Jedi and had a scrap that almost laid waste to the galaxy. Heh. Where have you been?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Well, I wasn't there, but like all Sith, Revan and Malak turned on each other. After they turned on the Jedi, of course.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «That was the story - but whatever happened there must not have lasted. There was some big civil war on Korriban, knocked that academy to the ground. Looks like Revan's grand crusade finally consumed her.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I guess... there's rumors all over space about it. All I heard was Revan returned to pay Malak back for trying to kill her in the first place. You know women.» [K2/Administration_Level]

- «Dark Jedi are bad enough, but when a woman falls to the dark side, you better space yourself before they catch you.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «The males are bad enough, but when a woman falls to the dark side, you better space yourself before they catch you.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Uh, no offense or anything.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Look, not like your half-naked interrogation isn't a personal fantasy of mine, but...» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Look, no offense or anything, but your weird half-naked interrogation isn't my id...» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Hey, wait a minute - you're that Jedi the miners were talking about. Where is everybody?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «The miners can't all be gone. But if they are...» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «From my beautiful view in this security cage? Look, I heard some explosions, some emergency alarms, some toxic gas pouring out of the vents... Maybe none of them survived whatever happened. And if they're all gone...» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Look - hey, let me out, and I can help you. I can. I've gotten out of trouble countless times.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «It was a misunderstanding, trumped-up charges. I could clear it up if I could just speak to somebody in charge, but seems to be no chance of that happening... unless you let me out.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Just in case you don't understand the trouble we're in, let me lay it out for you. This facility is a series of mining tunnels dropping kilometers into the asteroid's core. Every worker and droid here has been cleared, then triple-cleared, for hazardous operations. This facility has an operational threat level so high they don't let un-cleared starships in system out of fear that an accidental engine discharge will create a very brief, very hot new sun in this sector. You know why? Because every asteroid in this orbiting minefield has deposits of Peragian fuel within it - that means when one decides to pop, its brothers and sisters are going to come along for the ride. One explosion - you might survive. But a hundred? A thousand? You'll be riding out of here on a comet trail, Jedi. So if something's gone wrong here, you can cling to the Force all you want, but nothing'll stop this place from going critical.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «[Success] This facility isn't a military installation, which means we may have a chance.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «This facility isn't a military installation, which means we may have a chance.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «You shut down this cell's security field, and I can reroute the emergency systems so we can get to the hangars. We grab a ship and then we fly out of here.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «[Success] Huh? What are you talking about?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «[Failure] No - should I? Maybe you should ask one of those miners who was thinking about selling you to the Exchange... maybe they wanted to drug you up for transport, ever think about that?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «[Success] So you done interrogating me, or are we going to work together and try and get out of this mess?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «[Failure] You better watch yourself - I don't like being accused of something I didn't do. When I kill someone, trust me, you'll know. Got it?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «You better watch yourself - I don't like being accused of something I didn't do. When I kill someone, trust me, you'll know. Got it?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «So you done interrogating me, or are we going to work together and try and get out of this mess?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «No deal. If I'm not leaving, you're not leaving.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Well, go ahead and burn your thrusters. If there's been a lockdown, you're trapped pretty nicely on this level. You're going nowhere. Oh... except I have clothes, and this security field is protecting me pretty well from any more outbreaks of poison gas.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Great - now to business. Let's get to the command console.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «All right, here we are. Now this console is set on automatic hail, you may have heard it when you came in. The asteroid drift charts are constantly being updated, so it sends out a transmissions to incoming vessels so they don't get crushed into space dust. Thing is, you can bounce that same transmission back to the comm here... and suddenly, you've got access to the communications system from the inside.Pure pazaak - the console's ours. Now, all we need to do is re-activate the turbolifts, cancel the emergency lockdown... hey.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «This system's been severed from the main hub - after it was locked down from remote. You can't even reroute the system, it's been cut clean.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «No - someone tried to lock down this whole level tight, and leave us here. Trapped.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «That's my guess... to cut off the console specifically from remote then sever the hub - it doesn't get any more deliberate than that.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I doubt it. All we have is communications back, for all the good trying to shout in a vacuum will do us.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «We could try. But if the miners were trying to trap you up here and probably kill you, why not call them and chat? I don't think a friendly call is going to wake them up.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Be my guest - not much else we can do. The comm's all yours.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Found anything?» [K2/Administration_Level]

- «What little is left of the facility's venting systems have gone active, most likely from the explosions in the mining tunnels.» [K2/Asteroid_Exterior]
- «They're venting Peragus fuel deposits into space through the exterior vents - right in your path.» [K2/Asteroid_Exterior]
- «I can't - I'm locked out of the main systems here, I couldn't shut it down if I tried.» [K2/Asteroid_Exterior]
- «That's just it - they're not supposed to be active when the airlock's opened - and not without the safety measures kicking in.» [K2/Asteroid_Exterior]

- «Oh, what now?!» [K2/Asteroid_Exterior]
- «I don't believe this - there's a ship coming in, sending a docking code.» [K2/Asteroid_Exterior]
