- «Well, that puts us on the same level, doesn't it? Doesn't make your decision any more right than mine if you want to start sentencing me.» [K2/Atton]
- «We were loyal to Revan. That was enough. He saved us.» [K2/Atton] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «[Influence: Success] After Malachor, after the Mandalorian Wars, that's when the Sith teachings started spreading through the ranks.» [K2/Atton]
- «[Influence: Failure] It's another story, and it's nothing I feel like talking about. I did a lot of things I'm not proud of... and I couldn't do it anymore.» [K2/Atton]
- «We knew where our loyalties lay - to the Jedi who came to help us, not the ones who sat back on Dantooine and Coruscant, watching us die. So when those same Jedi who watched us die decided to start fighting us during the Jedi Civil War, we fought back. I fought back.» [K2/Atton]
- «Yeah, it sure is. And that's why I was so good at it. And that's why I wasn't fighting Jedi. I was killing them.» [K2/Atton]
- «I started killing Jedi. A lot of them.» [K2/Atton]
- «I didn't fight Jedi, I killed them. A lot of them.» [K2/Atton]
- «People say killing Jedi is hard. It's not, you just have to be smart about it. No blasters, no getting close to them, no attacking them directly when you can gun down their allies instead. There's ways of gassing them, drugging them, making them lose control, torturing them. I was really good at it. What's worse, is that killing them wasn't the best thing. Making them fall... making them see our side of it, that was the best.» [K2/Atton]
- «Yeah, maybe you'll believe this - that when fighting a Jedi, you wound the Padawan first, then let the rest take care of itself. Not only will the master move to protect the student, but the Force bond between the two will mess up the master's head better than any stab wound. Anything else? That's only a sample of my arsenal.» [K2/Atton]
- «If there's no whiny little Padawan, start shooting innocents - not to kill, just enough so that they're gonna die unless the Jedi does something to save them. And if any Jedi is stupid enough to use their Makashi lightsaber form against you, start shooting as fast as you can and drop them while they're exposed. Set mines. Set a lot of them. Fire gas grenades, but make sure they have magnetic lock targetters so Jedi can't force push them back.» [K2/Atton]
- «I taught myself... techniques. It's hard for Jedi to sense what you're really thinking if you throw up walls of strong emotions and feelings. Lust, impatience, cowardice... most Jedi awareness doesn't cruise beyond the surface feelings, to see what's deeper. And I was good at that, throwing up walls, and my superiors knew it. Sometimes the Jedi on our side wouldn't even realize I was there.» [K2/Atton]
- «Part of it. Maybe it was always me. It's hard to tell sometimes. I haven't known who I am for years.» [K2/Atton]
- «Yeah, I had a talent for it. More like instinct.» [K2/Atton]
- «I wasn't the only one. I know you left at the Mandalorian Wars, so you don't know much about what went on behind the scenes in the Jedi Civil War. But Revan understood one thing - the real battle was going to be fought between the Jedi on both sides. That was the only battle that mattered.» [K2/Atton]
- «Whoever had the most, the strongest Jedi were going to win the Civil War. If Revan couldn't convert Jedi, then Revan would kill them. So Revan trained elite Sith units into assassination squads, whose duty was to go out and capture enemy Jedi. I was in one of the special units trained to do this.» [K2/Atton]
- «Well, it doesn't really matter what you believe.» [K2/Atton]
- «Revan had plans for all Jedi. I think it was important that the Jedi see his side of things, the Sith teachings. Revan wanted to break them. And then have them join him.» [K2/Atton] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «One day I decided not to do it anymore, so I left. Ended up on Nar Shaddaa, became someone else.» [K2/Atton]
- «Because you've killed Jedi, too. Different circumstances, but you have a bigger body count than I ever did.» [K2/Atton]
- «I didn't think you would, after Malachor. But it was a chance. I guess I was just tired of keeping it in.» [K2/Atton]
- «And I've been with you only a short time, enough to know that as soon as someone signs on with you, they haven't got long to live. You got history, and anyone who travels with you doesn't. And maybe I want somebody to know who I was in case a story needs to be set straight. Maybe you understand.» [K2/Atton]
- «Take your time - I have.» [K2/Atton]
- «[Influence: Success] Well, there was a woman. A Jedi. She... she gave her life for mine.» [K2/Atton]
- «[Influence: Failure] I think there's been enough lies and truth for today. Let's just leave it for now.» [K2/Atton]
- «[Influence: Failure] No, I don't think we're ready for that conversation yet.» [K2/Atton]
- «I never knew her name. She sought me out. She said she had come to save me.» [K2/Atton]
- «It wasn't a mission. She sought me out. She said she had come to save me.» [K2/Atton]
- «She was lying, of course - or I think she was. It doesn't matter - she told enough truth to get my attention.» [K2/Atton]
- «Maybe. It doesn't matter. She knew the right things to say.» [K2/Atton]
- «She said that Revan was doing something terrible to Jedi within the Unknown Regions. That when we captured Jedi, they were sent to a place designed to... break them.» [K2/Atton]
- «And that anyone in his service who showed any ability with the Force was sent there, too, to turn them, to break them into Dark Jedi... or assassins trained to kill Jedi.» [K2/Atton] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «She said that's what would happen to me - that I had the Force inside me, that's why I was so good at killing Jedi. And that when the Sith learned of it, there would be no escape, no turning back. I would become an instrument of the dark side, forever. I had heard talk in the ranks, troops vanishing. I knew what she meant, but I didn't believe her - or want to believe her.» [K2/Atton]
- «I did what I did with all Jedi. I hurt her. I hurt her a lot. And then, right when I thought she couldn't take any more - she showed me the Force. In my head. And I felt everything she felt, and I heard just an echo of what the Force was. And how what I was doing... I think I loved her, but it wasn't that kind of love. It was the kind of love where you're willing to give up everything for someone you don't even know.» [K2/Atton]
- «Maybe. It doesn't matter.» [K2/Atton]
- «I killed her for crawling in my head, for showing me that. But before she opened her mind to mine, my only thought was that I would love to kill her. And at the end, I killed her because I loved her.» [K2/Atton]
- «In the end, she sacrificed herself to keep my secret, to prevent the Sith from knowing about that touch of the Force inside me. She wasted her life to save me. Me. And I felt her die, when she opened her mind. I've killed Jedi like I said, but I was never there to feel it, to be on the receiving end. And after that, I couldn't stop feeling things - before, guilt, lust, impatience, it had been orchestrated to get close - now, it all just kept tumbling out - and I couldn't keep doing what I was doing. So I left. I fled with the displaced war veterans to Nar Shaddaa and I lost myself there, until the war came to an end. I wanted no more of Jedi, or Dark Jedi, or the Force. I just wanted to be left alone. And then I met you on Peragus. And I thought, maybe, maybe she had saved me so that I could help you. And if I can't, then I have to try.» [K2/Atton]
- «I didn't want to tell you any of this. But... I had to. Because if something happens, I can't let you think I was doing it for something other than the past.» [K2/Atton]
- «I don't want you to forgive me. That would only make it worse. All I want is to try and help, try and make a difference - pay her back. I feel I owe her that much.» [K2/Atton]
- «It didn't feel that way, though - it was different.» [K2/Atton]
- «Yes, but that was my choice.» [K2/Atton]
- «But what she said... if I had remained, they would have turned me into something else.» [K2/Atton]
- «{:: Frowns, silent, thinking ::» [K2/Atton]
- «But why would she die for me? And the feeling... that love...» [K2/Atton]
- «You're right... I didn't think of it like that... but she tricked me. Another Jedi trick, more Jedi hypocrisy, like the others. I'm tired of Jedi crawling in my head. I'm tired of not knowing who I am any more.» [K2/Atton]
- «I will follow you, to hell and back. I will do whatever you ask, and your enemies shall be mine.» [K2/Atton]
- «Once, I was shown the Force - I heard it, I felt it. And it was used to weaken me. I know that turning away from it was her plan for me, and I will not walk that path any longer.  I want to learn how to use the Force, as I was meant to. And there will be no place in the galaxy where Jedi or Sith can hide from me.» [K2/Atton]
- «Do not discount me - I can do whatever task you ask, kill anyone you want to prove myself. You have only to name it.» [K2/Atton]
- «You are wrong, and I will try to show you why. Until then, I only ask that you not abandon me. To be honest, it's kind of a relief. I already know how to kill Jedi... I'd always be second-guessing myself when entering a fight.» [K2/Atton]
- «Then that is the decision I will accept. But I will still serve you, aid you against the Jedi, if that is your wish.» [K2/Atton]
- «Then I will await your decision. You have heard my request, and that is enough.» [K2/Atton]
- «Once, a Jedi showed me the Force - I heard it, I felt it. At the time, there was too much pain to confront it - because if I did, it meant I would be changed into something else. Now, I'm not afraid of it any more. And I think that by learning how to use it - I can help protect you. Or at least buy you some time when disaster comes screaming in. I want to learn how to use the Force. I want to learn how to use the Force to help you.» [K2/Atton]
- «But... I can do it. Whatever it is, name it.» [K2/Atton]
- «I understand. If there's any chance you will change your mind... let me know. To be honest, it's kind of a relief. I already know how to kill Jedi... I'd always be second-guessing myself when entering a fight.» [K2/Atton]
- «What must I do? Is there someone you need me to kill to prove myself?» [K2/Atton]
- «What must I do? Is there some... some ritual, or...?» [K2/Atton]
- «:: Silence ::» [K2/Atton]

**[K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]**
- «Maybe Nar Shaddaa isn't such a good idea after all...» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- «Did we just see what happened?» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- «The Exchange is one of the most powerful groups on the Smuggler's Moon. If this Goto wants you so badly, it'd probably be a good idea not to show up on his doorstep, wouldn't you say? Not to mention, I'm sure Loppak Slusk has friends on Nar Shaddaa that won't be happy to hear he's gone.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- «Yeah, I just can't help getting into trouble when you're around.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- «Yeah, you're right. We'll cross that bridge when we get to it.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]

**[K2/Cantina]**
- «Oh, I know how that works. Gambler's down on his luck, almost out of credits and doesn't know what to do. Then bam, there's a credit line all ready to go.» [K2/Cantina]
- «His luck's got to change, right? So he makes a few more rounds of Pazaak - and he's desperate now, desperate to win - not knowing that the deck's stacked against him and those twenties are never coming. Next thing he knows, his luck still hasn't changed. In fact, the only thing that has changed now is he owes his life's worth to some Exchange boss and he's getting intimate with a stimmed-up goon with a fist the size of his face.» [K2/Cantina]
- «Lack of control, why- Oh, never mind.» [K2/Cantina]

**[K2/Command_Deck]**
- «"You sense no one on board?" Sense any assassin droids creeping up behind us like last time?» [K2/Command_Deck]
- «Then what are we doing on this ship? We were better off in the facility! You two are supposed to be Jedi? You two are the worst Jedi I've ever met!» [K2/Command_Deck]
- «Maybe I would! At least I was safe there!» [K2/Command_Deck]
- «Look, I don't mean to cast another shadow on this, but even if you could reach the ship you came in on, it wouldn't matter. You'll need the orbital drift charts to clear the Peragus asteroid field, unless you want to have the shortest flight out of Peragus ever recorded.» [K2/Command_Deck]
- «Oh, you mean with the huge crew we brought along with us? That's a brilliant idea.» [K2/Command_Deck]
- «Well, of course, they have the asteroid drift charts in their navicomputer. They'd have to.» [K2/Command_Deck]
- «Well, we'd have to get to the bridge. I mean... well, that's the biggest problem I can see.» [K2/Command_Deck]
- «Very funny. Right now, I'm better off with the two of you, so let's go.» [K2/Command_Deck]

**[K2/Crew_Quarters]**
- «This was your room? When?» [K2/Crew_Quarters]

**[K2/Czerka_Site]**
- «Why do you say that?» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «I'd say there's probably an illegal landing site somewhere then.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «That's decided, then. We should get going.» [K2/Czerka_Site]

**[K2/Disciple]**
- «No, I don't. Go back to your training, I'll call you if I need someone useless.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Problem? No, no problem. Just wondering how long you're planning to stick around.» [K2/Disciple]
- «How heroic of you. Well, she doesn't need you. In fact, we were doing just fine until you showed up.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Always with the details, aren't you? You can't fool me. You have some agenda, spying on her, always keeping your eye on her.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Yeah, and I noticed first. Get it? So cut it out.» [K2/Disciple]
- «And another thing - stop being all noble around her, in your big hero way. She sees right through your little act. She likes honest guys, not guys who run around being unselfish and heroic all the time.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Oh, great, what do you want now? To give me a hand? I told you, I don't need any help, especially from you.» [K2/Disciple]
- «No, I understand you just fine. Look, I know what's going on in your want-to-be Jedi mind. Thought you Jedi or ex-Jedi or whatever you are were supposed to be above all that.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Well...yeah. Okay.» [K2/Disciple]

**[K2/Docks]**
- «Uh... hey, thanks.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Dormitories]**
- «What in space is going on? Who's this? Another Jedi? What, did you guys suddenly start breeding when I wasn't looking?» [K2/Dormitories]
- «Uh... all right. I'm guessing that Republic ship that just docked isn't carrying friends of yours.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «Yeah, and I'm also good at running and drinking, your majesty. And even if you two aren't big friends of the Republic, that warship's the only way off this station.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «Good thing we have a clear run to the shi...» [K2/Dormitories]
- «What is it? Why are we stopping?» [K2/Dormitories]
- «I know we need to get out of here. Like I said, it's a minefield looking for any excuse to blow.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «Look - I'd be happy to share my whole life story with you when we aren't getting chased by assassin droids, all right?» [K2/Dormitories]
- «Look - I'd be happy to share my whole life story when we aren't getting chased by assassin droids and Sith troops, all right?» [K2/Dormitories]

**[K2/Droid_Warehouse]**
- «Hey - it's our astromech droid. I thought you got sold.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «There you are. What kept you?» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «Yeah, I know there's droids in the warehouse. So what?» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «He says he's got the transponder codes to Vogga's freighters - one that can be picked up by Goto.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «I don't believe this - he says he's got the transponder codes that Goto's using to hijack Vogga's freighters.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «We can change the ID signature of the Ebon Hawk, and get to Goto's yacht that way.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «[[../Refugee Landing Pad#Ebon Hawk ID Signature» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «There's bound to be someone in the Refugee Sector who can realign the Ebon Hawk's ID signature - there was a place near the landing pad when we came in, we could try there.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «Yeah, right - you're the one who wanted to sell her to Goto in the first place.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Can you change our ship's ID signature?» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]

**[K2/Ebon_Hawk]**
- «Well, check it again. It can't be anything else.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- «Just check the damn connector. I don't trust the diagnostics anymore than I trust you.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- «Because I don't like droids. They break. In the head.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- «Well, whatever you call that thing on your... head.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- «Yeah, well, if I'm mean to you, it's because I care.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- «Hey buddy. I've played enough card games to know a bluff when I see one.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Look, we're already full up. We don't need anyone else. We travel light.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Yes, bring him along. And when we get back to the ship, I'll prepare a celebration feast.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Engine_Deck]**
- «I can't help it - don't you feel it? Something's gonna get real wrong, real quick.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «Don't you feel it? Something's gonna get real wrong, real quick.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «You don't survive on the Rim as long as I have without knowing when trouble's coming.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «All right, but don't say I didn't warn ya. Trust me, when it comes to staying alive, I'm rarely wrong about these things.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «Tell me you're joking. We are not going to cross back into the Peragus facility through the fuel line - that's crazy!» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «Alright... but I know I'm going to regret this.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «Oh, hell - better the death you know... I'm right behind you.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «Wh- what's wrong? Are you all right? Dammit, hold on! It's only a little further. Don't give up on me now!» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «What happened to you?» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «You look fine - come on, we need to keep moving.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «Alright, then - if you're sure.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «Huh? How do you know that?» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «Look - if she's in pain, then that pain's buying us time we can't afford to waste. Especially if sleeps-with-vibroblades gets tired of playing with her and decides to use us for practice next.» [K2/Engine_Deck]

**[K2/Entertainment]**
- «I knew it! That stupid T3 unit stole our ship! It's probably joyriding through the system right now, laughing at us... laughing at me.» [K2/Entertainment]
- «Wait. You're saying the ship's actually somewhere on Telos' surface? I don't understand. Telos' atmosphere is highly corrosive outside the shielded Restoration Zones. Where else could somebody land safely?» [K2/Entertainment]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- «Yeah, maybe if the droid's not very good, there's some credits to be made here.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «I think maybe you should let me... handle... this one.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «Just like a Jedi.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Fuel_Depot]**
- «It's a utility droid - looks like it's been hit with an ion charge and dumped here.» [K2/Fuel_Depot]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- «What is this place? Some kind of control center?» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «Not again.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «Run?! I didn't run from you two schuttas last time - and this time, I'm going to make sure you stay down.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «Yeah, well you'll understand if I hold back the tears.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «Yeah, well at least we didn't find that Jedi Master with the weird name.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/HK-47]**
- «Tell me no one said that out loud, did they?» [K2/HK-47]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- «What the hell are you doing on our ship?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Well, we don't want your help. Or any of your sisters.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Yeah, me, too. I'll be in my chambers. But since I don't have any, I guess I'll just go to the cockpit like I always do. If she's coming with us, she gets the cargo hold. Might remind her how fun it is to get locked up.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «What do you mean, pazaak? What, again?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Don't you ever give it a rest?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Fine, don't mind me, I just need to get some stuff from the cargo hold.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «What can I say; I'm a quick study.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Oh, yeah? Guess you saw a lot of the world while you were freezing your cargo hold off in the ice academy, didn't you, sister?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Next time I walk in here, I'm carrying a blaster.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Oh, yeah, one other thing - don't think I haven't noticed the way you've been sparring with our ex-Jedi friend. This isn't some pleasure yacht, so if I did know all the Echani movements, I might know more than just the first tier, including the etiquette rituals. So keep your hands where I can see them.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Schutta.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Oh, there's a surprise. Trust me, she's a handful - all warriors are. They're not used to dealing with things they can't punch, kick, or break.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Look, I know how it is. Me, there's no denying that I'm a good-looking guy. You have it worse, because even though you might not be as good-looking as me, you have that whole tortured past, that command presence. Women want to save you. They think they can help you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «They think that everyone can be redeemed, and that they're the only ones who can do it. And you don't know if it's you, or the idea of you that they love.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «We're just talking. Like I said, I've never understood women.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It's possible they don't love you at all. That they just want to help you... help you hear yourself if you've gone deaf to your own voice. We all lose our way sometimes, and we need someone to pull us back.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Yeah... she woke me up, inside. I'm still trying to sort it out.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Don't remember. Truth is, I still don't listen to my conscience even when it's shouting. I think there's times I'd rather be completely deaf than hear it.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «But all this talk doesn't matter. I'm not qualified to give advice. Besides, when I open my mouth, I'm usually lying anyway.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Hangar_Bay]**
- «This door's magnetically sealed. I can't believe this! The ship's right out there, and we can't get to it.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Huh? What is that piece of junk saying?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «How can you even understand that noise?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «All right, well, if he can slice the door open from the terminal above, don't let me stop him.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «[[../Turret encounters#Peragus II» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «We've got company!» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «All right, let's get out of here!» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «If they hit us, we're dead! But if they keep missing us, we're dead! That's great odds.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Somebody shut that trash compactor up!» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I'm doing all I can, and that's not enough. What did you do to make these guys so mad? Now either they hit us and destroy us, or they hit an asteroid and make the whole field go nova.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Not with all these asteroids around us. We'd enter hyperspace in pieces. We have to clear that field first. Thing is, we clear the field, and they're going to have a clear shot at us.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «That'll take out the whole field, the colony, and maybe us. We might not even be able to jump to hyperspace in time.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Hold on! This is going to get a little rocky!» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Well, now that we just killed a planet, maybe one of you can tell me what's going on. Because between assassin droids, a Sith Lord that looks like he sleeps with vibroblades, and being target practice for a Republic warship, I was better off in my cell.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Not like we have much of a choice, the Peragus astrogation charts being what they are.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Repaired this ship, my eye. Next thing you know it's going to claim credit for saving our skins. If that little noisemaker says it repaired the ship once, then it can prove it by doing it again. Go on, get!» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Maybe that droid noisemaker could make itself useful repairing the ship - for once.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Look - enough with the "we," already.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «That's the first smart thing I've heard since you two started talking.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Hey, don't stop your long, boring rants on my account. I was just getting sleepy-eyed.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Look, uh, not like I care or anything, but you might want to go check on our passenger - especially with that hand of hers.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «We're on autopilot until we hit Telos. Until then, a droid could fly this thing. Besides, I think our passenger could use your help.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I think she was barely keeping it together - I'm surprised she's able to stand with all that pain rolling off of her.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Are you blind? If I were her, I'd be screaming like a stuck mynock. Well, I mean a very strong, manly mynock. I think she's just too proud to show any weakness... especially in front of you.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Is it? In case you hadn't noticed, she won't say two words to me, but she'll talk your ear off any chance she gets. What you think matters to her... a lot. She wants you to respect her. Besides, we haven't got much else to do until Telos.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Oh, don't give me that. All it takes is being around people enough to read them. You should try it sometime.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «How's our passenger? She still aging?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Yeah, to you, maybe. I don't usually hear much beyond 'fool' and 'imbecile.'» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Well, she sure could use it. It might do wonders for her mood, too.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «[[../Hangar Bay#Old» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «What a surprise. Just so you Jedi know, the whole "cryptic routine" isn't mysterious, it's just irritating. If you really can see the future, you should be at the pazaak table.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Then she must be royalty, because she's got to be Queen of the Galaxy to bark out orders like that. Or maybe she's senile.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «[[../Hangar Bay#Good» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «What was that, some kind of joke? That's what I'm talking about. 'Jedi Talk.' You two should start your own little Jedi Academy.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «All right, cut it out, I get...I get it, I get it. The last Jedi in the galaxy, I get the comedian who runs around in her underwear. Not that I'm complaining, mind you. I mean, compared to the Jedi queen of the galaxy back there, I'd rather be stuck in an escape pod for a year with you than her.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «All right, all right! Cut it out, I get it, I get it. The last Jedi in the galaxy, I get the comedian who runs around in his underwear. Not that I'm complaining. Most Jedi wouldn't know a joke if it crawled up their lightsabers. Our passenger, for example.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Good point. How old do you think she is, anyway? She may have been good-looking once, but it takes some hard living to make creases like that.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Hey, I just got out of prison. If we had a decent navicomputer, trust me, we'd be dropping out of hyperspace into the Nar Shaddaa Red Sector right now. After spacing that old witch of course.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Yeah, her face looks like it was plowed by crazed Ord Mantell farmers. Don't tell me you were too distracted by her personality to notice.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Whoa, all right, all right! Don't get mad at me. Hey, I didn't ask her to stay behind and get her hand cut off, okay? I mean - I appreciate what she did and all, but she could stand to lay off the insults herself, you know?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Oh yeah? Well, how much water you get from a stone depends what planet you're on, sister.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Yeah? Well, how much water you get from a stone depends what planet you're on.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Oh no, no, no. Look, look, I respect your privacy. I mean, when have I ever asked you any questions? I mean, besides that one.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Well, the astrogation system is voice-printed and locked down, but that T3 unit is doubling as the astrogation system. You can try to plot a course, but without that T3 unit to perform the calculations, you'd probably plow us into a star. As long as he doesn't steal the ship, we should be all right.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I have no idea. Previous owner, maybe? I'd love to get it overhauled, but that's a major job - besides, the droid'll be good enough for now.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Takes all kinds. Maybe someone didn't want anybody taking the ship out of the system - or knowing where the ship had been. Smugglers do it all the time in case the Republic decides to board them... or so I hear.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Not much, except sounds like it was after you. As far as I'm concerned, you handled that pretty well - no more droid, no more problem.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Beats me. Sounds like you're pretty popular - if it was built to hunt you down, that is. It could have been around before you even appeared on the scene.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Yeah, well you got me there. Look. Droids, I don't trust them. That one we fought? Some of them are built like that. Others just... well, break... in the head. Sometimes conflicting orders cause it. Give a droid too much data or tell it to do something it can't do, it'll crack their behavior module in half. Others just don't get memory wipes and they start going crazy.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Speaking of which, I think that little trash compactor's long overdue. Hnh. Trust me, droids were made to break. And most of all, they're predictable and stupid.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Like we have a choice? It's the only place Peragus had logged in their astrogation charts.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «You call this piloting? This is nothing - if Peragus had any place other than Telos in its astrogation charts, trust me, that's where we'd be headed.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «If you thought Peragus was dead, then Telos is a dying world they're trying to breathe back to life. We should be there before too long. You can check our course on the galaxy map if you want - it's on the wall behind you.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «So... what happened?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Don't give me that. There were plenty of times back on Peragus where a lightsaber would have been helpful. So - where's yours?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Oh, yeah? I thought a Jedi was supposed to be married to their lightsaber. Guess I heard wrong. Were you a single hilt or one of those double-bladed Jedi?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Hnh. Figures. It wasn't red, was it?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Hnh. I hear the twin blades are harder to master, but they can make enemies stampede over each other running for cover. Lot of Jedi in the Mandalorian Wars used double-bladed sabers - a more aggressive blade, gives you more slaughter per swing. Hey, you didn't go red, did you?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Great. Maybe you and that Sith Lord can have a party after we're dead.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Yeah, unique how?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Must have been something. Sure be nice to have it now - might make those Sith think twice before coming after us.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I thought a Jedi and their blade couldn't be parted. To lose it was like losing a part of yourself.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «All right - forget I said anything. Like I said before, you can check our course on the galaxy map if you want. It's on the wall behind you.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «We should be there before too long. You can check our course on the galaxy map if you want - it's on the wall behind you.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]

**[K2/Influence]**
- «Well, guess the Jedi Code is still alive and well.» [K2/Influence]
- «If you say so. I'm not sure I believe it.» [K2/Influence]
- «There's a lot of people who need help in the galaxy. If we stop to help each one, the Sith are going to be on us faster than anything. Let's just accomplish our mission first, then worry about everyone else.» [K2/Influence]
- «Let's not make this a habit - I don't want your good nature rubbing off on me.» [K2/Influence]
- «I don't understand what that accomplished, but if it makes you feel better, fine.» [K2/Influence]
- «Well, guess it didn't hurt to be nice this once.» [K2/Influence]
- «You really believe that? Look, Jedi or not, you can't treat the galaxy as one big charity case - if you do, then the galaxy's going to step all over you.» [K2/Influence]
- «Yeah... well... that's what I'm here for, to keep you from getting yourself... and me... into trouble. Now let's go.» [K2/Influence]
- «Oh, don't give me that. You needed me to get you out of there. You were just helping yourself.» [K2/Influence]
- «Well, maybe not. But... look... you're really trusting sometimes, and if you keep it up, someone's going to burn you bad.» [K2/Influence]
- «Look, I just... never mind, forget it. Can we just keep going, please? I don't know why I even try to stop you from doing half the dumb things you do.» [K2/Influence]
- «I... don't know what even came over me. One second we were just standing there, talking, and then...» [K2/Influence]
- «What... what just happened? One minute, we were just talking... and then... I just lost control.» [K2/Influence]
- «I don't have a problem with it, you just should choose your targets a little more carefully. And not be so conspicuous about how it's done.» [K2/Influence]
- «It's like this... impulse just came over me, I couldn't stop myself.» [K2/Influence]
- «Next time, warn me - if you're out to kill someone, don't surprise me like that.» [K2/Influence]
- «Good thing we got the drop on him before he knew what was happening.» [K2/Influence]
- «That felt good.» [K2/Influence]
- «Glad we got the drop on him.» [K2/Influence]
- «Why did we do that? Why did I do that?» [K2/Influence]
- «He had it coming.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- «The Ebon Hawk is patched up and ready to go. When you're ready to depart we can ask the Guide here to take us back to the ship.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Jungle_Landing]**
- «I've got some news. The space battle is still going on. The Hawk's sensors just picked up a contact heading to the moon. Most of our ship's systems are powered down, so that's about all I got. That ship may have landed nearby, though. Or it may be on the other side of the moon. So you might want to prepare for another friendly Onderon welcome.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]
- «The orbital fighting just ended. That Colonel Tobin stirred up a Mynock's nest when he took a shot at us. I'm still working on the repairs and I have to take down some systems, including sensors. So you'll have do without me for awhile. I know, you're crushed.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- «Huh. I could have sworn it was this way. This forest plays tricks on you.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- «Did you really used to live around here?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- «You're as smooth as the barrel of a blaster. I like that.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Korriban]**
- «We've hit the ground. This is Korriban. Why would one of the Jedi you're looking for come here?» [K2/Korriban]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- «...switch the face of the +1/-1 card, the totals are nine-ten. Switch the face of the +2/-2 card, the total is eight-eleven. Switch...» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- «We just got this message on the comlink. Looks like trouble. I think this is something everyone will want to hear.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «The droid's the one who picked up the message - he's got it all ready to display.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Well, good thing it's not a trap.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Could you please lighten up for one second?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «It's just off the docks, near one of the far traffic pylons. He's got you at a disadvantage there, though. The place is filled with cyanogen gas. One whiff of that, and it'll be the last breath you take. You'll need something to allow you to breathe there - and disguise you from the other patrons.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Yeah, that means all you have to worry about is the patrons not finding out that you're human and gunning you down. Be careful - this whole situation stinks.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Well, they don't like humans there - the Jekk'Jekk Tarr is an alien watering hole, no humans allowed.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «You're right - without breathing and skin protection, you'll be dead in seconds.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «No, cyanogen gas attacks you through the lungs and the skin, you'll be dead in seconds.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Besides, you'll need a full body suit if you want to remain disguised. Like I said, a human walking around in there isn't gonna get a warm reception. Can't bring any droids in there, either. There's a lot of electromagnetic activity in the area, screws with comlinks and behavior cores.Don't be surprised if your automap starts showing static, either.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Well, I wouldn't keep him waiting. If you got his attention, you probably attracted the attention of someone else.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Hey - look, I wanted to tell you, be careful. I won't be able to contact you via the comlink if something happens, and I'm betting that squid-head knows it.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Look, take these - they're antidote packs. If your suit gets breached, you'll need to inject them fast if you don't want your lungs to seize up.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Look, take these - they're healing packs. If your suit gets breached, you'll need to inject them fast if you don't want your lungs to seize up.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «And trust me - once the seizures start, you'll be dead.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «All right... don't be too long. I'll keep an eye out here until you return.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Watch yourself - and don't be too long. I'll keep an eye out here until you return.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «And I know just the place.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «All right... whatever. Don't say I didn't try to warn ya. Me, I've got better things to do.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Well, this sure beats staying on the ship. A few drinks to keep me on my toes, a few games of pazaak to keep the mind alert...» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «...should be enough to keep me out of trouble until our fearless leader straightens things out.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «...should be enough to keep me out of trouble until our fearless leader creates some more.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Give me a hit of juma, and keep them coming.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Well, looks like staying on the ship was a bad idea after all.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «So... I don't think I caught your names. Uh, do you two work here, or...?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Yeah? What happened to your Master?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «No, actually I'm here protecting someone. Keeping them out of trouble - by acting as a distraction for people looking to harm her.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «No, I'm here protecting someone. Keeping them out of trouble - by acting as a distraction for people looking to harm him.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Why don't you two schuttas try it, and we'll see what happens?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Well, that's it. The bounty hunter truce is off - that means that this place is going to get real bad, real fast. I better get back to the ship, warn the others.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Hey, we need to move out.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «The truce between the bounty hunters in Nar Shaddaa is off. There's going to be war. A trap in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr is bad enough, but having a hundred bounty hunters on your back is something else.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Look, we need to move. They're coming after us, not the exile.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Yeah... you're right. But I'm guessing we're in a lot more trouble than she is.» [K2/Landing_Rights] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Anybody here catch that? All I understood was 'very.'» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Ah. well that would explain it. Which one do you want?» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Lost_Jedi]**
- «Now we're off that dejarik board of a planet, I say we burn sky until we see lines.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «Those Jedi sure like their secrets, don't they?» [K2/Lost_Jedi]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- «Hacking through beasts and jungles? That sounds like a job for me. As usual.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Mining_Tunnels]**
- «So, uh, how long have you been a Jedi? Must be tough, you know... no family, no husband...» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «So is that stupid droid of yours going to come through or not?» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Hey, I wasn't try-» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «All right, all r-» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Well, I'm beginning to think I was a little better off in my c-» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Hey, what do you know - that little cargo cylinder came through.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «If he got the turbolifts working, then we should have a clear run to the hangar.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Wait, wait. Don't tell me you're taking that hatch down into the mining tunnels... are you? That explosion I heard came from below. There's probably nothing down there except superheated rock and collapsed blast tunnels. You'd be an idiot to go down there.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Me?! You're the Jedi - if one of us has to go, then it's you. Guardians of peace and justice throughout the galaxy, last I heard.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «There's no way I'm going down there. You might as well lock me up again if you freed me just to throw me into a nest of gundarks.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «I'll take coward over stupid any day. I'll keep the comlink on just in case you need some help... not that I'll come running, mind you.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «You're either really brave or really crazy - or both. All right - I'll try to monitor things from up here. Be careful - the only thing moving down there is likely to be mining droids, so don't be playing hero too hard. Uh, not that I care what happens to you or anything. I just don't want to be trying to get off this rock by myself.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «I'll keep the comlink open... I may be able to guide you through the tunnels from up here. Don't know if the signal will hold if you get too deep, though.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Can you read me?» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «What did you say? Your signal's crammed with static.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «There's a lot of interference down there - probably caused by that explosion. Still, it looks like there's a route down to the Peragus fuel depot, if the passages haven't collapsed. That explosion knocked out most of the sensors. There should be an emergency crate in the next room. Watch yourself. There's a lot of droid broadcasts in that area, but I can't pin them down.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Will do - and be careful down there.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «If you can handle yourself, fine - I'm just looking out for my own skin. Atton out.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Find the emergency supplies?» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Dammit! Uh... I mean, good, good to hear it. No sense in you running around half-naked. It's...it's distracting... I mean, for the droids.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «About time. The whole half-naked Jedi thing was wearing a little thin.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Look, there may be some survey gear and a safety harness inside the crate, too. The miners wear them when staking claims on the asteroids. The survey gear is designed to spot and protect you against sonic mines... and the safety harness can be helpful if you try to disarm them.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Yeah, it's like a military-issue energy shield, except it's designed to protect the miners against lasers... and heat. Should work against the droid mining lasers. It won't last forever - and certainly not against multiple laser hits - but it may buy you some time if you get ambushed by a battalion of droids.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Just equip it on your wrist, and then you can activate it like a medpac. Again, it won't last forever, so make it count.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Uh, just one more thing - I've narrowed down some of the ID signals, and if the numbers are right, you're sharing those tunnels with a battalion of mining droids.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «You could, if you have the skill and a stealth field generator. These droids rely on thermal sensors, primarily to detect fuel deposits. The good thing is, that explosion down there kicked up so much heat and steam it may blind them... a bit, but not much.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Well, these are construction models... they shoot like a moisture farmer militia. Since they rely on ranged weapons, get in close with a melee weapon and start bashing them. In close combat, the guy with the vibroblade has the edge over the guy with the rifle, droid or not. Otherwise, just drill them from a distance - if they're not shielded, that is.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Yeah, it's possible some of the droid models may have mining shields on. If so, the shields may absorb laser fire. You can usually tell when a shield is active - it'll make an electrical field around the target. If they activate a shield, the best thing you can do is hit them with a melee weapon or try to burn out the shield with continual fire. But that could take a while, and it leaves you a target.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «There's got to be some central controller down there. See if you can find a terminal by the main access shaft: that'd be governing intelligence.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Watch where you step - I'm picking up a lot of sonic mines down there. Don't run unless you have to, it makes them harder to spot.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «If you have any skill with demolitions, you might be able to recover them and use them against the droids... that is, if the mines don't get you first. If you have survey gear or a safety harness, put them on - they'll make spotting and disarming the sonic charges a little easier.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «There was a cylinder filled with emergency supplies near the hatch to the administration level - I'd check there.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Those mining droids, especially the excavator models, are designed to arm and set sonic charges for mining. Chances are, if they still had charges after they went rogue, then they may have set them to try and kill the miners... and you. If you see one of those excavators, watch out - they may use their undeployed charges as projectiles.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Hey, watch out. That explosion has super-heated the tunnels ahead. That steam'll cook the skin off your bones. If you can find a mining energy shield, switch it on - it should protect you against the heat if you move quickly enough.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «There was one in the emergency supply crate near the entrance. And one of the miners down there may still have one they couldn't switch on before the blast hit. Look around. If you can't find one, there's nothing much you can do except try and avoid the areas where the steam is thickest.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «You're getting close to something big - I think it's the main ventilation shaft. The central droid controller should be somewhere nearby. Keep an eye out for it. I'm picking up a lot of droids. If you still have a mining energy shield, I'd equip it and switch it on.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Sure, if you have a stealth field generator. It'd make it an easy walk to the central droid controller.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Sensor...? Must be maintenance drones. Watch out, those little pests will try to repair the mining droids if you don't gun them down first. Still, it's odd they're still active after the explosion - they don't have the same shielding as the mining droids. Be careful of those droids, I don't like this.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «Hey, I'm picking up some strange readings - what are you doing down there?» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «The containment fields in the mining tunnels are shutting down - you need to get out of there before they vent fuel to the surface of the asteroid through the tunnels.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- «I may be able to keep it contained until you get the turbolift to the fuel depot, but not for much longer. I'm locking down the turbolift to the administration section now to keep the blast from spreading. If you've got anything left to do down there, make it quick, because where you are is going to get real hot, real soon.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]

**[K2/Mira]**
- «I heard you making friends with the bounty hunter. I don't blame you. She's a scrapper. You don't survive on Nar Shaddaa for very long if you aren't.» [K2/Mira]
- «I've known people like her. Maybe without the rocket launchers, but sort of the same. Maybe without the plunging neckline and the boots. I wouldn't let anything she says bother you - it's a wonder you cracked her attitude at all. She's cold as the ship's hull.» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- «Well, here we are... the smuggler's moon. It's the gaping maw of Nal Hutta, swallowing all the cargo and space port thugs the galaxy has to offer. Mandalorians, mercenaries, war veterans, and pilots from the Mandalorian Wars and the Jedi Civil War ended up on Nar Shaddaa, from all sides of the conflict. When the last war ended, there was no place left for them to go.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Nar Shaddaa's a rough place and easy to get lost in... or for someone to get lost. If we wanted to keep out of sight from the Sith for a while, you couldn't pick a better spot.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «It means "glorious jewel" in Huttese, but don't let that fool you. It's the central breeding grounds of the Hutts - Nar Shaddaa orbits it. Nal Hutta's as slimy as the Hutts, lots of swamp and bloated gas. It's where those slugs reach out and grab chunks of the galaxy. Trust me, we're not going to go anywhere near the place unless we want to be washing the stink out of our clothes for the next few years.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Yeah, some came looking for work running freight and cargo. Still, there's only so many ships to go around and so many workers. So others lend their weapons to the Hutts and the Exchange. It's become a prime base for raider recruitment across the galaxy.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «It won't be easy. There's so much traffic on Nar Shaddaa, finding anyone on the moon's surface is going to be hard.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Shouldn't be too hard. There's so much traffic on Nar Shaddaa, finding anyone on the moon's surface is going to be hard.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «We're going to touch down in the Refugee Sector. There's a lot more traffic there, and it's harder for people to spot you coming in... or find you once you arrive.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Anyone flying the star lanes has docked on Nar Shaddaa at least once. I wouldn't want to live there, and I doubt anyone does by choice.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Not everyone who came to Nar Shaddaa were soldiers. A lot of worlds were destroyed by the Mandalorians... and the Jedi. It left a lot of people wandering the galaxy.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «I've plotted a course for the Refugee Sector, and we should touch down within the hour. Once we're down, we should finally be able to breathe easy. There's no way anyone's going to find us here.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Ah... the beautiful stench of decay and desperate living.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Welcome to Nar Shaddaa: towering buildings kilometers high and miles deep, with canyons so wide you could have a dogfight in them. Word of warning - watch where you step, or you'll fall for hours.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Sure, most of the landing pads around here are unclaimed... or should be. They're pretty badly maintained, so they're not safe to land on. Well, I mean, not this one, but they all have the reputation, so we should be all right. I think.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Maybe a little, but landing here means we didn't have to transmit our ID signature, and you know what trouble that always brings. In fact, while we're here, we should get those signatures changed. Wouldn't make us such a target when we enter a new system.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «No, but I forgot to tell them we were landing. The Refugee Sector's a dead zone, no one cares too much who flies in and out of here as long as they're not carrying cargo that the Exchange or the Hutts might want a piece of.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Yeah, in all its glory. Don't get your hopes up from what you see here, though - as soon as we hit the main sector, that's when the smell... and the mobs... can get pretty bad.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «All right, then, let's move out. Uh, where are we headed, exactly?» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Uh... yeah. If you want to stay on the ship and meditate some more, don't let us stop you.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Whatever we do, I say we get the Hawk's ID signature changed while we're here. Might keep us from being a target when we enter a new system.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «What is it you wish to do?» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Then just being here should be enough. People in the Refugee Sector don't tend to ask too many questions, we should be safe enough.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «There should be a central trading hub up ahead - their stock's probably not the best, but they may have some things worthwhile.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Well, that means finding either a bounty hunter, a ranking member of the Exchange, or someone willing to talk - none of which are too appealing. Bounty hunters and the Exchange are going to want to shoot you, and someone who is willing to talk is willing to talk to anyone, which means trouble.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «It's up to you. There's bound to be someone in the Sector willing to spill their guts for a credit or two.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Well, if we're going to search a moon of a few billion inhabitants for one Jedi that even our own can't sense, might as well start as soon as possible.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «All right. If you have any questions, just ask. We should be able to leave the ship here as long as we want, no one supervises these landing pads anymore.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Uh-oh.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Onderon]**
- «Well, this is Onderon. It looks like there's a long line to get into the Iziz Starport.» [K2/Onderon]
- «I guess this blockade is a symptom of larger problems on Onderon.» [K2/Onderon]
- «Looks like we're about to find out. I'm receiving a message from some Colonel Tobin. Patching it through...» [K2/Onderon]
- «We've taken some hits! We can try to fight back, or I can outrun them and hide us on the jungle moon nearby. It's your call.» [K2/Onderon]
- «I can't keep us in one piece much longer. Sorry to spoil your fun, but we've got to land now. I'm going to put us down on that jungle moon.» [K2/Onderon]
- «You know, just once, I wish someone was glad to see us. But no, if it isn't weapons pointed at our heads, it's someone trying to blast us out if the sky.» [K2/Onderon]
- «Well, the space battle's still going on overhead. Since they were so eager to use our hull for target practice, I doubt they're just going to forget us. I don't like it - Onderon is about as far from the Core as you can get and still be in the Republic. But even out here the locals have heard of us. We're lucky I was able to find this place to land - looks like something has cleared away the jungle in a few spots around here.» [K2/Onderon]
- «It looks like one of the moons of Onderon, not sure which one. It's mostly jungle and mountain. I did pick up the remains of an old outpost near here. Maybe that's why there's all these clearings around - maybe they were once settlements.» [K2/Onderon]
- «Crash sites?» [K2/Onderon]
- «This is where the Mandalorian Wars started? This doesn't look much like a battlefield.» [K2/Onderon]
- «It's taken a little damage, nothing too serious. I'm shutting down all unnecessary systems until we make repairs. It'll keep us from being a target.» [K2/Onderon]
- «Until the ship is repaired, we're not going anywhere. Unless you can find another route to Onderon, we should sit tight.» [K2/Onderon]
- «Something? Oh, there's something here all right - predators. Not small Flipdarters, but big, mean, nasty predators.» [K2/Onderon]
- «I agree. Going off alone is just asking for trouble - especially when there's no more reason for it other than a cryptic whim... no offense.» [K2/Onderon]
- «Well, if you go, be careful - no telling what other ships were forced down in the battle.» [K2/Onderon]
- «Yeah... I understand. What's so important about this place?» [K2/Onderon]
- «She fought here? Why didn't she say anything?» [K2/Onderon] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «The repairs are coming along. I hope to have the Ebon Hawk ready by the time you finish your business here.» [K2/Onderon]

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- «Why is it that everywhere we go I end up in a cell? I mean, why did they lock us up? What is this place?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «What? This ice hole?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You've got to be joking. What is a Jedi Academy doing out here in the middle of nowhere?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Atris?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Well, the sooner we're outta here, the better. Two crazy Jedi are more than enough for me. No one told me we were going to be dumped in a nest of Jedi.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «What do you mean? We're in the middle of a bunch of Jedi. You know how they are.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «What... what are you doing? Get out of my head!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Stop... st... ernhhhh!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You can't tell her. Please - I'm asking you. I don't want her to...» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «What? What price?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «And if I refuse?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «I don't know how you became such a manipulative witch, but why a vicious old scow like yourself would even bother with me is a bigger mystery.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Fine. I'll be your pawn. But I still think you've got the wrong man.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «More Jedi speak. Care to explain?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- «+2 pazaak makes 19, -2 pazaak makes 17, +1 pazaak...» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «The Handmaiden took her. She thinks Kreia killed you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Of course she didn't. It's a lie... another lie.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Because that's the lie Kreia told her, that's why.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «The only thing that matters is the Handmaiden believes it. And she's going to react exactly how that old witch hoped she would. That's why she wanted the Handmaiden on board, you know. So she could use her to reach the Telos Academy whenever she wished - without needing the access codes.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Yes. The Handmaiden knows who she is, now.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «She would, if she thought she was bringing Atris a prisoner. Especially a Sith Lord.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «She'll take her to Telos, and Atris will do what she'll do to anyone she thinks is a Sith.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «She's gone. The Handmaidens came for her. They know who she is now.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I couldn't stop them. I wasn't conscious at the time. I don't even know if I could have stopped them.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «They'll take her to Telos, and Atris will do what she'll do with anyone she thinks is a Sith.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Are you surprised? All that talk of hatred, manipulation, and standing on your own two feet - sorry, you don't get any more Sith than that. Still, if we were all judged by who we were in the past, I don't think you'd understand who we are now.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Yeah... she will. If she can.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Yeah... I know.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «That's what I was afraid you'd say.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- «Sullustan hearing's pretty sharp, if you couldn't guess from the lobes.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Big-ears here might know someone who can change the Ebon Hawk's ID signature. Could be useful for avoiding notice.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Ask a Twi'lek. It's not flattering.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Your ship? I'd lay off the spice if I were you - that's our ship, not yours.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Whoa - he could have gotten that information some other way than owning the ship. C'mon, he's skifting us.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «What? It's our ship! I mean, your ship, that I fly.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «What are you doing?! That's going to trap us here.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Lemme shoot him in the back, no one has to know.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Point taken.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- «Hey, let me take care of that for ya.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «Expert. I like the sound of that.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «Let's see what I can do here.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «Whatever you say.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «Hey, I was only trying to help!» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «All done.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Rescue_T3-M4]**
- «Voiceprint? Great - that means he'd have to speak the code. And he's dead, right?» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- «There's ways to beat voiceprints, though. If you can get samples of his voice and the right words, you should be able to fake the voiceprint code, if you know what it is. You know, I overheard the maintenance officer getting chewed out by the security officer. You might want to check the security office on this level and see if there's any voice samples stored in the logs. And maybe there's samples of the code in the maintenance officer's holo logs, if you can find a way to record them.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- «That's a start - see if you can find any recordings where the maintenance officer is speaking, and try to piece together the words to unlock the voiceprint.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- «Yeah? Did you blow it up?» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- «Yeah well, watch your back. Droids can't be trusted.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- «Because droids can't be trusted.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- «Look - I could have let you rot in those mining tunnels, and all I get from you is backtalk. You know what? I don't trust you. For all I know, you could have caused all this trouble - in fact, you did, just by being a Jedi! So before you start accusing me of anything, Jedi, maybe you better take a look at what you've done.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- «So they are still alive - but the dormitory comm isn't picking up anything. Is there any way to get to them?» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- «There may be some emergency route to get to them - still, maybe we should leave them where they are and try to get to the hangar. If they wanted to sell you to the Exchange, we should make a clean getaway while we can.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- «You mean like they did to you? You're pretty forgiving.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- «My feelings exactly. The sooner we're off this orbiting mine field, the better.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]

**[K2/Residential_082_East]**
- «Why don't you answer the terminal? Doesn't sound like they're going to leave us alone.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Maybe we can get back to sleep now.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «We should get back to bed. Whenever they decide to release us, we should get going immediately.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Explain something to me.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «If she served in the war... well, Jedi are supposed to be tough. Capable.» [K2/Residential_082_East] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «But to lose so much... I guess I didn't realize how much they relied on it.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Such a loss of ability - for a Jedi, it seems so extreme.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Well now what? We can't just stick around. We need to find a way off this station, whether it's the Ebon Hawk or some other ship. We could hit Nar Shaddaa, maybe. If you've got people coming after you, it's where you go to get lost in the crowd.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Hey, everyone needs to get lost once in a while. Get away from something, you know? It's no big deal.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Look, this place is a waste. If Telos wasn't doomed before Peragus blew, it is now - even more so if your 'friends' from Peragus come calling.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Because it depends on Citadel Station, and the Citadel depends on Peragian fuel and the Republic's senators. The Citadel's fuel source went up with the mining facility, and the bickering senators? Heh... not something worth counting on.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Well? What do you think?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Makes sense. I'll follow your lead.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «The Republic? They can't even properly fund and manage a space station, let alone fight an enemy as cunning and ruthless as the Sith. Even if somebody believed us, the Republic won't be any help - believe me, I know. Only the Jedi threw the Sith back last time around.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Do you really think it's a good idea to be working for the Exchange?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Is it really worth it though? I don't know if we can trust Lorso, either. I mean, it takes something to ally yourself with the Exchange.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «That woman's a shark, but if you say so, I guess it'll be okay.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «As long as you're ready for it. But I hope it doesn't come to that.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «I don't know anyone who's dealt with the Exchange and had it end well.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Well, if you say so, just watch your back.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «You're right, of course. I just wish we could find our ship and get out of here.» [K2/Residential_082_East]

**[K2/Residential_082_West]**
- «Smooth.» [K2/Residential_082_West]

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- «Well, this is familiar. Feels like my last time on Telos.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «No, pazaak.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «You're welcome, Kreia. You know, if I weren't such a crack pilot, we could have hit the shield wall or one of those rock faces.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Beats me. No one's supposed to be here but a Czerka research team. I can't say they'd be happy to see us, but shooting us down...? I can't imagine Czerka having us shot down by a bunch of scientists, either. You know, I caught a glimpse of what looked like an AD tower when we flew over the compound.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Maybe they're doing something they shouldn't be. I've seen pirate bases with the same sort of set-up.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «But we don't have any idea where to look for the Ebon Hawk.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Just the trouble we need, some crazy Zabrak with a squad of mercs on his tail.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Hidden caches. We should be on the lookout for more of these - they could come in handy.» [K2/Restoration_Zone] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Great.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «We could try handing the Zabrak over. You know, bargaining chip?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Good point. Forget I said anything.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Amazing at wasting the Republic's credits, maybe. But now that you mention it, I think I feel it too. Like a weight's been lifted off my shoulders.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- «Hey, the gang's back together. I must hear all about your vacation in Onderon.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Well, that's good I guess. When powerful people butt heads, it's usually the little guy who suffers most.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «You think you're hot stuff, don't you?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Okay, keep us in the dark then. Whatever...» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- «Do all these women look alike? Heh. Not like I'm complaining. I mean, it's... well, it's, uh, interesting.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Oh, please. At least ease off the cryptic talk until we're back on the ship.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Enhhhhhhh....» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Enhhh... hey, you're back with us. We were just on our way to rescue you from those ghost women, when... uh, we got locked up.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «What? Look, I'm fine, okay? I was just a little dizzy when I first woke up.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Nah... don't worry about me, I'm fine.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Look, I appreciate the concern, really, but I'm fine.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Got it - sorry about that. This place must be getting to me.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Got it - sorry about that. Everything we've been through must be wearing me out.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Uh... how did things go with the Jedi here? Are you all done?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Things went that well, huh? You make friends wherever you go, don't you?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Well, that's not exactly what I wanted to hear.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Well, that's encouraging. Nothing like a steady stream of people who hate us or want to kill us to keep the heart pumping.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Nah. I was just complaining. Heh. I'm with you until things start going better for you. We need to stick together, you know? And who knows... I might be able to help you out of a tight spot at some point.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Uh, hey. Don't mention it. It's my pleasure.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Oh, that. Don't tell anyone, but you wouldn't believe how many fights you can prevent by just pretending to know that stuff. I mean, it doesn't compare to wearing a lightsaber, but then again, that doesn't seem to help you much.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Well. Hey. Thanks.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Huh? Well... hey, thanks.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «But you've got the wrong guy. I'm good at shooting people, cracking wise, and pretending to know how to fight with my hands.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Well. Hey. Thanks. But you've got the wrong guy. I'm good at shooting people, cracking wise, and pretending to know how to fight with my hands.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Well, if it isn't the one who stole the Ebon Hawk. Not so smug now, are you, you little thief?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Says you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- «Get away from her! She's a dark Jedi!» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «No, I won't allow her to corrupt you! I will protect you, even from yourself.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Think again, Kreia! Your dark influence will end!» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Apathy is death.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- «Hmmm, I'll bet even the Ebon Hawk couldn't blast through this door.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «So this is who we came here to find? Looks like this has been a big, dangerous waste of time.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Suburban_(082_West)]**
- «Does this mean you won't be putting us into force cages again?» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]

**[K2/Swoop_racing]**
- «Yeah. There's nothing swoop fans like more than seeing a good wreck.» [K2/Swoop_racing]

**[K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]**
- «I don't like the sound of that. If they think we caused the explosion... Uh oh - here comes the welcoming party. They may not know what happened, so don't blow it.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «Hold on. Is there any way to get our gear back?» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «Tell me I'm not going to jail again.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «Well, we might be here for a while. Might as well get comfortable.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «The Exchange, huh? I'm pretty sure some two-bit pistol jockey like yourself isn't one of them.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «You bounty hunters couldn't even win a fair fight. You're the cheapest, most worthless mercenary scum in the galaxy. I'd hire a Mandalorian over your filth in a second.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «...then what, overloaded our force cage fields and made it look like an accident? You probably don't even have the guts to fight me. Heh. Pathetic.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «Hey, leave her alone - you want a fight? Then try me, if you've got the guts.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «But just to be on the safe side, why don't you leave us a blaster or two?» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «Well, this is a step up from a force cage at least.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «This isn't good. We've got to get off this station.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «What do you think the TSF is going to find at Peragus? That could bring the S- You know what, forget it. As long as we're trapped here, it doesn't matter.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «Yeah, you go ahead and meditate. As for me, I could use some sleep.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «Ennnh... what's that stench?» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Yes, a great powerful stench.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Looks like somebody left the doors of the Academy wide open. I have a bad feeling about this.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K2/Visas]**
- «Now I've seen everything. This woman... she's a Miraluka. I didn't think any were left in this part of the galaxy.» [K2/Visas]
- «Yeah, they're a pretty secretive race. I heard that some of their kind become Jedi, but a Sith? That's... well, that's a new one. I'm not sure how you'd go about killing one. It'd be tricky.» [K2/Visas]
- «Just mentioning it - she looks like she's suffered enough wounds already... even after the beating you gave her.» [K2/Visas]
- «I heard they had a colony on the Mid Rim, almost halfway between Onderon and Dantooine. Then... it wasn't there anymore. The whole planet was wiped out, nothing left alive, no one knows why.» [K2/Visas]
- «Well, it *was* a planet of her people. If they see through the Force, who knows? If they're all Force Sensitive, maybe they all saw something through the Force that we can't see, and they left before it happened... or maybe it killed them.» [K2/Visas]
- «They claim to see on a higher plane than we do, you know, the whole Force thing. Makes me nervous.» [K2/Visas]
- «Well, some of her wounds are pretty bad - looks like she was already carrying her share of scars, though. I think she'll recover, yeah.» [K2/Visas]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Atton Rand (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Disciple]**
- Disciple: «Atton... I feel that perhaps there has been some miscommunication between us concerning the exile.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Atton, perhaps I should explain - I see relationships with others in a different light, not as possessive, or carnal. Relationships are something shared between two people and give both strength, whether through struggle or whether through affection. I admire her, perhaps even feel for her. It is difficult to be around her and not have such feelings.» [K2/Disciple]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- Mira: «Great. I hope he doesn't snore like Atton.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Kreia: «Of course there was. It is because Atton was not playing pazaak, yet he counts cards in his head. At times, he will list off engine sequencers, memorize the hyperspace routes on the other side of the galaxy, count the ticking in the power couplings even though they are fixed. At other times... he will imagine certain... base lusts, certain... indignities. It may be Atton is far cleverer than he feigns to be. Or perhaps he is simply a fool.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- Disciple: «No, Atton, I think it may be a trap.» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Mining_Tunnels]**
- Central Controller: «[Judging from the droid schematic displays, it looks like Atton was right: the droids rely on thermal sensors to detect fuel deposits. You could easily blind them by raising the temperature.]» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]

**[K2/Mira]**
- Mira: «Heh... for a minute there, I thought you and Atton... but it's just the Force.» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- Disciple: «It is not your choice, Atton, it is hers. What is it you wish to do?» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- Kreia: «Do not worry, "Atton." If she is Jedi, she will forgive. And if she is not, she will not care.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «Do not worry, 'Atton.' If he is a Jedi, he will forgive. And if he is not, he will not care.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «...think less of you? I hardly think that's possible. Still, there is no shame in what you ask. We all wage war with the past. And it leaves its scars. I will not speak of yours, Atton, but there is a price for such things.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «You will not. If you do, then my silence will be broken. And then, Atton, you will be broken. You fear the Jedi and rightly so. If Atris learns of your... choices, you will never leave this place. But whatever fear you hold of the Jedi, know that if you disobey me, that my punishment will make you beg for the death that has long hounded you. Wipe the fear from your mind. You will not find blind obedience a difficult Master... you chose it once. You will learn to embrace it again.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Handmaiden: «Atton, restrain yourself. Besides, a blaster would be unnecessary when a nerve strike to his neck would incapacitate him painfully without inflicting any lasting harm.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- Kreia: «Atton, I've had enough of your snide contempt!» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «Stay out of this Bao-Dur! This is a personal dispute between Atton and myself.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Bao-Dur: «You're threatening Atton with a lightsaber, and I'm supposed to just stay out of it? No!» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Trayus_Core]**
- Kreia: «Atton shall keep his murderer's heart. Many deaths shall he cause in the dark corners of the galaxy - always hunting, always finding prey. He shall grow hungry in a galaxy where there are few Jedi, and it will eventually consume him... but I shall say no more.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Atton is, as always, the fool. And the Force watches out for ones such as him, I feel. As it does for the old such as I.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «He is a fool, and that should answer your questions. He has nothing to offer to one such as you - and even a fool such as Atton is not so ignorant of that fact.» [K2/Trayus_Core]

- [context-resolved] Handmaiden Sister: «He has had some Echani training. He masks it well, but when you were in danger, his mask dropped into a stance we know well.» [K2/Secret_Academy] (anchor: And Atton would have?...)
- [context-resolved] Kreia: «He is only sleeping - it seems the journey here has fatigued him.» [K2/Secret_Academy] (anchor: What happened to Atton?...)
