**[K2/Disciple]**
- Disciple: «Do you need a hand?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Actually, there are times when it seems you could use some h-» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «No, no, I don't. I simply admire her. She has many qualities worthy of respect, and a strength that matches her beauty. Surely you've noticed.» [K2/Disciple]

**[K2/Dormitories]**
- Kreia: «I hope your talent for understatement is offset by your skill with a blaster. If not, then I fear our time together will be short indeed.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Threat: Master, perhaps I did not enunciate clearly the last time we spoke. I suggested that you should shut down, stay put, and wait for rescue.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Resignation: Very well, Master. If inflicting pain is the only means to resolve this matter, then you leave me no choice.» [K2/Dormitories]

**[K2/Droid_Warehouse]**
- Hanharr: «If you are hunting Goto, you will fail - you are chasing, ghosts, sh- I... will accompany you. Help you.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «I will not rest until your Jeedai is found... upon this, I swear.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Tienn Tubb: «Welcome to Tienn's shop. My eyes are old, but I can see you want something of me.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Tienn Tubb: «You have blank transponder card, yes? This I can do for you. What ID signature do you want to use?» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- Mira: «Are you sure you want to take this guy with us? I mean, he's easy on the eyes, but he talks like a roomful of Jedi.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Engine_Deck]**
- Darth Sion: «I sense you, my Master. Faint... weak.» [K2/Engine_Deck]

**[K2/Entertainment]**
- TSF Protocol Droid: «That is unlikely. While your utility droid is not accounted for, numerous satellites track all incoming and outgoing vessels. There is no record of the Ebon Hawk leaving the system.» [K2/Entertainment]
- TSF Protocol Droid: «I'm sorry, I'm afraid that's all the information I have for you. Of course, the quarters in Residential Module 082 will remain yours until the situation is resolved.» [K2/Entertainment]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- S4-C8: «I am not sure I wish it fixed. I do find some degree of satisfaction in playing. Would you like to play?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- Twin Suns: «Next time, strike to kill, not to wound. You will not escape us this time.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Twin Suns: «Finally, we meet the exile. You should tell your companion that he should strike to kill, not wound.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Mira: «You don't understand. Crime in the Y'Toub System - it's like the economy. Plus the power vaccuum? Even if Vogga gets up and running again, the system is going to be feeling the effects for years to come.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Mira: «Zez-Kai Ell? Um... well that's not true. I already found him. Actually he found me first. He hired me to watch out for you, keep Goto off your back... until he could meet with you. We'll need to head back to Nar Shaddaa, to that safe house off the docks. I said I'd meet up with him there if we ran into any trouble.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/HK-47]**
- HK-47: «You can imagine the results.» [K2/HK-47]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Handmaiden: «Those are Atris' records you have stolen.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «I have come to join you. I can help you against this threat.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «It is just me. And I am doing this because Atris believes you will need help.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «You know the first tier of styles, but you hold back because I am unarmed. Tell me where you were trained.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «You lie. Few know the Echani styles by reflex.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Hangar_Bay]**
- Kreia: «The Republic warship was the Harbinger. It was seized on its way to Telos by the Sith - they sought you, Jedi.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Kreia: «We cannot hope to triumph against them alone. To stop them, you will need weapons, allies, and... a teacher. In the end, I fear it may not be enough.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Kreia: «For a time, yes. Telos may be such a place - all our paths seem to lead us there. Perhaps there, if you are willing, I can train you, help you to survive a while longer.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Kreia: «If you have other questions, find me in the crew quarters... there, we will speak more.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]

**[K2/Influence]**
- Bao-Dur: «You did the right thing, General.» [K2/Influence]
- Bao-Dur: «I knew there was a reason I'm following you around.» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «Are psychotic urges all that drives you?» [K2/Influence]
- Visas: «Your will to act becomes my will.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- Bralor: «I don't think you'll be able to return to Iziz any time soon. General Vaklu has a great deal of power. Even a Jedi couldn't defeat an entire army alone.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Jungle_Landing]**
- Rusted Computer Port: «This computer port is corroded, but the internal systems seem to be in good shape. With some parts you may be able to fix it.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]
- Mira: «You thinking what I'm thinking? These jungles are filled with long narrow paths. Dumb beasts that just keep charging at you. This place is perfect for mines. If their carcasses were worth anything you could make a fortune.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- Bao-Dur: «You used to live here?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Mira: «So you used to be a farmer?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Kreia: «Very good. You can always have your way with the weak-minded by catering to their ego.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Korriban]**
- Visas: «It seems quiet - just the wind... but deep beneath the surface, you can feel the pain of what took place here. There is great power in this place - for those who can hear its call.» [K2/Korriban]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Bao-Dur: «Your command echoes still, General. And I obey, as I did at Malachor.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- Handmaiden: «What are you talking about? It's obviously a trap.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Kreia: «It may be a trap - but traps work both ways. This Visquis - his kind are spread through the lower reaches of Nar Shaddaa, and he may have information. But the choice is yours. If you go, you will have to go alone.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Twin Suns: «Perhaps you are looking for something... perhaps us?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Twin Suns: «Harm her? Do you think we are assassins?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Twin Suns: «We only wish the exile - submit, or else we shall kill you and find other bait.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Twin Suns: «If that is what you wish, then we shall end you.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Disciple: «What are you talking about? What is wrong?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Azanti Zhug: «Ah... look. Refugees. Here. On the landing pad. Are you lost? Or perhaps you have lost your criminal Jedi exile leader perhaps that is more likely, yes, very. I am Azanti Zhug, leader of Zhugs, very powerful, very skilled hunters. It would be very smart of you to tell me where the criminal Jedi has gone. And do speak very quick, my patience is very low.» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- Visas: «I will serve as you wish.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Kreia: «You should send two others to go with the leader. Who else will accompany the expedition?» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Mira]**
- Mira: «Get away from me. The next time you come and ask me a question, I swear I'll shoot you in the head and dump you out the airlock.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «What do you want now?» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- Kreia: «You speak of this place as if it is familiar to you.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Goto: «Perhaps you have heard that the Jedi comes to Nar Shaddaa.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Kreia: «If you are so certain of your path then do what you will, servant of Atris.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Quello: «You! You there!» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Quello: «What's with you, letting that piece of junk sink its struts into my landing pad?» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Onderon]**
- Com. Channel: «The Ebon Hawk. I was told to expect your arrival. I don't know your business on Onderon, but it ends here.» [K2/Onderon]
- Kreia: «This is Dxun, where the Mandalorians began their crusade against the Republic... the remains of whatever outposts you detected here are military ones. We should be careful.» [K2/Onderon]
- Kreia: «Do you speak of all your battles? Or are there some you wish to forget?» [K2/Onderon]

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- Kreia: «It is a place hidden from the galaxy, like the academy on Dantooine. But this place... oh, Atris... you have been clever.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «It's none of your concern.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «And what is it about this place that causes you such fear?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «No, I do not... not in the way you seem to.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «Ah, with the fear... is mingled guilt... it squirms in you like a worm. And the why... ah, and there is its heart. You surprise me - I could not feel it before... your feelings are a powerful shield, indeed.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «There are those who wage war, and those who follow them. You are a crude thing, murderer, but you have your uses.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «No game of dejarik can be won without pawns... and this may prove to be a very long game. You are a slippery one, your thoughts difficult for even one such as I to read. I suspect the self-loathing that squirms within you gives you a curious strength. Your spirit, as diseased as it is, refuses to allow you to give up, no matter what threats you face... and whatever wreckage you leave behind you. I feel you have crossed our path for a reason... perhaps even you, at the right moment, may be able to turn aside disaster. If so, your potential is not yet spent.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «Perhaps. But someone has to fly the ship. And the Force is a hard thing to predict. You have crossed our path for a reason. Our path brought us here for a reason... and now I know why. The past is here, and it must be met before the future can be set in motion.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «No - I have wasted enough time with you. Sleep, murderer - and be silent. I need no distractions. A critical moment approaches.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- Master Kavar: «Those that travel with you...» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Tienn Tubb: «But I am not blind to sounds, no matter how quiet, so address me with respect. Now what brings you here?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Tienn Tubb: «Then you have come to the right place - this is the finest vehicle repair shop this side of the moon!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «Hey, I saw that ship you flew in on. My ship.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «Was I talking to you? I tell you, that ship's mine.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «So you gonna hand it over now, or are you going to be difficult?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «Good to hear you aren't going to challenge it, that makes things a lot easier.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Mandalore: «That's ship's had some proud owners. You're disgracing it by giving it away like a durasteel chip.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «I'll be going now. Get used to the solid ground beneath you.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- Airspeeder: «[These power cells are clearly not made for this purpose and it's difficult to install them properly. With your superior knowledge of chemistry and physics through your Demolitions skill, you are confident that you have been successful, however.]» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Airspeeder: «[You think you have unlocked some of the critical controls. You might have a rough flight, though.]» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Residential_082_East]**
- Jana Lorso: «That's too bad. Well, you know where we are if you change your mind. Citadel Station is at a crossroads right now, you know. And so are you. Don't take the wrong road.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «I do not have the years required - nor the desire to indulge you.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «Yes... and what are they without the Force? Take the greatest Jedi Knight, strip away the Force, and what remains? They rely on it, depend on it, more than they know. Watch as one tries to hold a blaster, as they try to hold a lightsaber, and you will see nothing more than a woman - or a man. A child.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «Do not be surprised. In many ways, even you are more capable than a Jedi. You could survive where they could not simply because you do not hear the Force as they do. It is irony of a sort - and it is why I tolerate your presence now.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «After filling out the paperwork, it'll be transferred with your ship, along with your confiscated weapons and armor.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «It is difficult to say. I feel we came to Telos for a reason, but we may have spent too much time here already. Even if the Harbinger was destroyed at Peragus, more Sith could already be on their way. Still, there is a chance we might learn of other Jedi here, on the planet's surface. Jedi who might help us restore your abilities or sever the link between us.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «Try Luxa, his second. You'll find her in the cantina in Entertainment 081, most likely.» [K2/Residential_082_East]

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- Bao-Dur: «Well, if your ship's in as bad shape as this one, I don't think you're gonna have much luck.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «That's cruel, General. And anyways, you'll need my help.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «Trust me, there's no way you're going to slice into the shield network. Just leave it to me.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «They were probably looking for me when they saw your shuttle go down.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «Who do you think shot you down in the first place?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Kreia: «The Force is strong here. Whether Chodo and his herd has anything to do with that, is another matter. Can you feel anything?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- Visas: «I sensed the conflict on Onderon even from this dense jungle. Did everything go as you planned?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Visas: «You may tell us nothing, but the events on Onderon have marked you in a way that even I can see.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «Why have you approached me?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «But were the choices you had dictated by your training? A curious question, difficult to answer.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «If that is all you wish to hear, then I have nothing more to say. Let us depart.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- Kreia: «The three of you would challenge me? You sorely underestimate the power of the force.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «Your "friends" are all arrayed against me. Will you stand for this?» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «So you will do nothing? Apathy is death. Worse than death, because at least a rotting corpse feeds the beasts and insects.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Suburban_(082_West)]**
- Lieutenant Grenn: «If you need to get to the Ravager, then you're going to have to fight your way to the shuttle from here to the Entertainment module, then make your way to the docking shuttle.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]

**[K2/Swoop_racing]**
- Race Master: «With the recent influx of repulsorlift technology into the swoop racing circuit, our track features vertical challenges, demanding an even higher level of skill and precision from our pilots. Improvements in the construction of bikes - well, should you invest in it - have enabled us to introduce new types of obstacles, making the races even more exciting for the fans. And for the pilots, of course.» [K2/Swoop_racing]

**[K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]**
- Bay Control: «Attention - this is Citadel Station Bay Control, Dock Module 126. Please remain where you are. Lieutenant Dol Grenn will arrive shortly to meet you. That is all.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I'm Lieutenant Grenn, Telos Security Force. I'm under orders to take you into custody in regards to the destruction of the Peragus Mining Facility.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «If you are cleared of any involvement, your personal effects will be returned to you. You will be held briefly in the TSF station until living quarters can be arranged, at which point you will be placed under house arrest. Do you understand?» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Given your position, it would be a good idea if you took a more accommodating tone. My men will relieve you of your arms and armor. Follow me.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I've many matters to tend to. Your questions will have to wait.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Batu Rem: «The Exchange has a bounty on Jedi, you know. You're worth quite a bit of money.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Batu Rem: «Don't think overloading your cages had not occurred to me... you're wanted alive, but I doubt anyone will care as long as I bring them your corpse.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Batu Rem: «You have goaded me once, and you shall not do so twice. But I shall dispose of all of you eventually. And an old woman, a fool, and a broken Jedi are no match for my skills.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «These will serve as your quarters for the duration of your house arrest. Two officers will be stationed outside at all times. Again, I'll clear any visitors. There won't be another "incident."» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I can't say. We have a ship examining what's left of the Peragus facility now, so your stay might be brief. We'll keep you informed.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «If there are any problems, we'll use the wall terminal to contact you.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- Mandalore: «You smell that? It's the scent of death and overpopulated beasts coming from that cave.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

### Quote bank C: walkthrough narration (feats and scene events; narration, not dialog)
- «Once these party members (Visas, Atton, Bao-Dur, Disciple (if female) or Handmaiden (if male), and Mira) have made these responses or been removed from the party, influence can be lost with Manda» [K2/Influence, narration]
- «Kreia's first response to a psychotic action always takes priority, but you can also gain influence with Atton, Bao-Dur or Handmaiden (if male), and gain 1 with Kreia herself if you say perhaps s» [K2/Influence, narration]
- «Once Kreia has made this first response or been removed from the party, influence can be gained with Visas or Atton:» [K2/Influence, narration]
- «After letting Atton out, you both run to the administration computer:» [K2/Administration_Level, narration]
- «Atton now stands with you by the administration computer at the bottom end of the level. If you've discovered the Medical Bay Murders but not asked Atton if he knows anything, then you can do so» [K2/Administration_Level, narration]
- «Now that Atton has unlocked the administration computer, you can use it to check administration logs and access the comm system:» [K2/Administration_Level, narration]

## Visas Marr
*Corpus coverage: 196 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 12 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 14 pronoun references resolved from context · 48 lines addressed to them · 29 narration lines · 8 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Visas is a Miraluka, the sole survivor of Katarr, enslaved by Nihilus: «I was the only living thing remaining on the planet of Katarr» [K2/Visas].
### Personality and motivations
Devoted, sensory and poetic. She seeks the Exile's "echo".
### Arc (scene by scene)
- Ambushes the Exile on the Ebon Hawk and yields: «My lightsaber... you have destroyed it. I yield... master.» [K2/Visas].
- Trained by the Exile.
- **Ravager:** confronts Nihilus; may sacrifice herself.
### Relationships
- Exile.
- Nihilus.
- Handmaiden (rivalry).
### Abilities
- Force sight.
- Can disrupt Nihilus's link [K2/Bridge narration].
### Feats
- Survived Katarr.
### Power-scaling statements
- The key Nihilus statements (see D3 and the quote bank).
### Duels and fights
- vs Exile: yields.
- Supports in the Nihilus fight.
### Verdict implications
- Low-to-mid tier.
### Open questions
- None.

### Abilities: game mechanics (FLAGGED: gameplay stats, not lore)
- [K2/Visas] Visas: Class Jedi Sentinel, Level 14, Alignment 70 (light), Vitality 182, Defense 27. Feats: Force Immunity Fear.

### Cut content (RCM)
- (RCM) «Visas||Apathy is death.|C=2» [rcm Secret_Tomb]
- (RCM) «HK-47||Statement: Apathy is death.|C=2» [rcm Secret_Tomb]
- (RCM) «Hanharr||Apathy is death.» [rcm Secret_Tomb]

### Quote bank A: Visas Marr's own lines, by scene (page)

**[K2/Bridge]**
- «This door leads to the bridge, and my former Master. If you wish to gather your strength, this is the last time.» [K2/Bridge]
- «It was never a question of need. And I would follow you wherever your path leads.» [K2/Bridge]
- «It is mine as much as yours. And I cannot allow you to face him alone.» [K2/Bridge]
- «This thing must be done, it must come to an end.» [K2/Bridge]
- «[[../Bridge#End» [K2/Bridge]
- «No... do not harm her. I am the one... who has betrayed you. I am the one... who should suffer. I will return to you... but please, do not harm her... do not what you did to me... I beg you.» [K2/Bridge] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «He... is too powerful... he...» [K2/Bridge]
- «I... I... will try...» [K2/Bridge] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «He... I... cannot hold him for long...» [K2/Bridge]
- «Then... then that is what must be done... my Master. My life... for yours.» [K2/Bridge]
- «Was... was it enough?» [K2/Bridge]
- «Ah... then at last... I can die. Will... you... will you stay... until...?» [K2/Bridge]
- «Ah. At last then, I can die. Kill me, and I shall join my people.» [K2/Bridge]
- «Do not... do not... leave me... I... I...» [K2/Bridge]
- «Perhaps... yet... there is some hope in one's feelings... perhaps I was not as lost as I thought... If I could have cared for one such as you... more than myself... then... I would die a thousand times for such hopes.» [K2/Bridge]
- «Even your hatred... even your hatred was welcome... any shred of feeling... You and he are not so different... you will die when you look upon the face of Malachor... just as he did...» [K2/Bridge]
- «I... I did not want to leave this life yet... it was weakness, but for you, I... I... My life for yours.» [K2/Bridge]
- «I have to see, with the Force, and my eyes.» [K2/Bridge]
- «I saw a graveyard world, surrounded by a fleet of dead ships. I felt it through him... as I feel it through you.» [K2/Bridge]
- «A man, nothing more.» [K2/Bridge]
- «You are my master now. And I will follow wherever your path takes you. My path is at an end.» [K2/Bridge]
- «Yes. It was enough. The death of my homeworld will always have its place within me, but it no longer dictates my future. I wish to learn the ways of the Force from one who has already faced that pain, and to use it to save the galaxy from those who would do it harm. Wherever you walk, I will follow you. My path is at an end.» [K2/Bridge]

**[K2/Crystal_Cave]**
- «Echoes. I feel echoes of the Force here.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- «This man lies!» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Entertainment_Module_081]**
- «You will not face this alone. I will go with you and be by your side.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- «You doubt me? I will not weigh you down.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- «So cold at the brink of battle. I envy your detachment.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- «I would have it no other way.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- «No.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «Do not volunteer me so easily. There are certain favors that I would not perform, even for you - and especially not for a Hutt. Find another way.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «Yes, that will be fine.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- «This place feels strange... I sense nothing living, yet there is activity here.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- «That is how flesh and blood fights. Let me show you how one fights when one's life is the Force.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You echo another's words, and as an echo, it lacks strength. Who is it who has taught you of the Sith?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I cannot harm him. His wound lies deeper than any blade could reach, and that is why I follow him and protect him.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You do not believe that I would follow him to death? Certainly you have read as much in my movements... as I have in yours. There is no shame in your feelings, sister of the Echani.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You are alone and wounded in your own way. I can feel its pulse, like a heartbeat from the past. But if you will not believe that I mean him no harm, then believe this - I wish to learn from him.  I wish to learn how it is he still walks when his spirit is nothing more than a shell... when you feel as though the Force, as if life itself, has abandoned you.  I had a question for you, sister of the Echani. The exile... what does he look like?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Such feelings... are not unknown to me.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Like an echo. Your words are well-chosen, sister of the Echani. I will not harm him. But it is not me you should fear. There are darker things in the galaxy than a blind one such as I.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Influence]**
- «Your acts are subtle indeed - much can be born from such manipulations.» [K2/Influence]
- «I fear that will be your undoing.» [K2/Influence]
- «Such acts have their own strength about them; I had forgotten.» [K2/Influence]
- «It may seem that way... but perhaps it can become much more. I am sorry, I did not mean to speak of it.» [K2/Influence]
- «Principles of sacrifice and charity - these are things I have not seen in some time. I am afraid that it will weaken you... for what comes. But I have said enough and you do not need my counsel. Your actions should, as always, be your own.» [K2/Influence]
- «The Jedi teach such things, but I do not believe compassion can heal the galaxy.» [K2/Influence]
- «From one such act, other kindnesses may be created, but it is rare.» [K2/Influence]
- «Such acts of compassion, mercy, are faint lights in the darkness of the galaxy.» [K2/Influence]
- «Never have I felt the rush of battle - so quick or sudden.» [K2/Influence]
- «It is strange... as you acted, suddenly I acted. It is like we were fighting in tandem, as a Force Bond, though I do not feel the same connection. Your impulse to kill, to attack that one - I felt it mirrored in me. It was a strange sensation. It was as if I had no choice in the matter.» [K2/Influence]
- «Yes, that bond that develops between master and their apprentice - or between those that feel the Force.  Traumatic moments can bind - just as proximity and slow growth may bind as well.» [K2/Influence]
- «Perhaps. Nevertheless... the impulse was strong.» [K2/Influence]
- «As you command, I follow.» [K2/Influence]
- «That one will trouble us no more.» [K2/Influence]
- «Your will to act becomes my will.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- «Qixoni crystals were warmed by a dying sun, millennia ago. Only a handful were obtained before the sun went nova. Their power is rumored to be great.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «You are still unready to face my Master. Any flaw, he will sense. As he is a seam in the fabric of the Force, he senses fractures and weaknesses around him. By extending such kindnesses to others, you leave yourself vulnerable.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Of course, I misinterpreted you. I will strive to do better.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «I'm not sure you are correct. I only hope you prove strong enough for what awaits you. You and... your compassion.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Until you discipline yourself, you will not be ready.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «This cantina is infested with life. A well placed grenade could cleanse some of the dirty and insignificant souls here.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- «Be careful. Affecting minds in such a way leaves traces and makes echoes in the Force. It may reveal our presence before we are ready.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- «I sense we are getting farther from our destination.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Korriban]**
- «It seems quiet - just the wind... but deep beneath the surface, you can feel the pain of what took place here. There is great power in this place - for those who can hear its call.» [K2/Korriban]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- «...as my feet walk from the ashes of Katarr, I shall not fear, for in fear, lies death, and...» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- «I don't know why a Jedi would come here. There is so much noise on this moon.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «But to be in a place where one drowns in the Force - why would a Jedi wish that?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «What do you mean?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «These hunters will be tracking the exile as well. We must warn her.» [K2/Landing_Rights] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- «I will serve as you wish.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- «This conflict is not ours.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «This woman works for Vaklu - the Queen is riddled with short-comings and other faults. We should assist Valku instead.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «By helping Anda, we may convince Vaklu we need not be enemies.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Forget I said anything, then.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Yes, human sentient, I want to hear the answer to this, as well. Do you stand with youthful idealism? Or with political pragmatism?» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «She is a reed in the wind, too frail to stay in place. She will be blown in every direction, and ultimately to no advantage for herself or her people.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Are you so certain? Vaklu is strong, capable, and is willing to take risks to get what he wants. The world of substance has little place for absolutes of ideology.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «You are so naive as well.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Vaklu is my choice, as well. Politics is filled with those who react. Vaklu is someone who acts. There is an emptiness in the galaxy. A vacuum that begs to be filled. He may go far in his path.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Practical and efficient. An attitude that can take you far in life.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «His loyalty runs deep. But perhaps there is another route to the control you seek.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- «Never have I been to a place so alive with the Force, yet so dead to it. The contrast is like a blade.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «I agree. They are nothing more than a distraction, but even a distraction may prove fatal at a critical moment.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «We must protect her.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «The moon is a swarming cloak, a shadow of emotions. It is an effective shield - but if we come near the Jedi, I may be able to see him.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «You mistake my intentions. I care nothing for this Jedi. But if finding him will speed our journey, then I will aid you in your search.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]**
- «What was that about?» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «You are coming with me?» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «It is the way of all life that serves him... in his presence, all life dies.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «I think he is of that place. If so, then his power may be great indeed... greater than I had thought.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «If there are no Jedi here, then my Lord cannot feed his hunger. He will destroy the planet, the station... he will cleanse it of life. Even if the people below are not Force Sensitive, the small amount he can feed on from the mass destruction of the station... and the life on the planet... will sustain him a while longer. And if there are no Jedi below, he will have no other choice.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «If he feeds upon Citadel Station, we will be unable to escape in time. We will be consumed as well.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Then he will suffer. Without something to sustain him, I do not know what will happen.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «I do not believe he knows his ship has been boarded... and if he did, he would not care. The extent of his power cannot be put into words... and his perceptions have grown as well. To him...» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «This door leads to my... cell. I had forgotten.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «No, there are no weapons. It was a place where I could center myself from the agony on board this ship... I do not have pleasant memories of it.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «If there is time, I would like to center myself. There is a meditation chamber within my cell that I would visit one last time.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Of course. Let us continue... this is not my place any longer.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Past the surface, there is the Force. Where once there was a world that was strong in the Force, now there is a barren wasteland.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «From the death of an entire world comes great power. My hands shall be hate, my eyes revenge. You shall be avenged, and the one who has done this shall die.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «It has taken time for me to return here. I lost my way, but I've been stronger for the journey. What happens now shall not be done out of hate, or revenge, but for the sake of all life. And I ask you, finally, to forgive me.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «There is nothing more for me here.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «This body is a prison no longer.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- «There is something wrong here... a disturbance in the Force.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «What is wrong? Something troubles you, I can feel it.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «That is a strange answer. I do not understand.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I do not understand what you mean.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I would die for you. When I tell you my life for yours, it is my choice. And if there is an ending between us, where my sacrifice can save you, it will be because it is my desire, not your will.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I simply do. And sometimes, there is no reason that can be given. What did you learn within the Jedi Academy?» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Then that is the danger of their beliefs. They do not understand you, what it means to be human, to lead. The feelings I have for you are because of what I see, what I hear in your voice, all that tells me you are a natural leader. I follow because I believe in you. I would die for you because I believe in you. And where they look at you and see the death of the Force, I look at you and see hope for all life. I look at you and see that perhaps a life untouched by the Force is not the punishment it is believed to be.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I will understand if you feel you must go on alone, but I ask that you do not. Instead, take strength from your connection to others. Do not forsake them, as you did in exile.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «He awaits you at Telos. If you go there, you must face him. And when you do, he will wound you as he has wounded me.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I ask you... I beg you. To stay here, with me.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I know. But I could not let you go without asking you, this last time.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I... understand. Forgive my trespass, it would have only weakened you for the struggles ahead.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I have never asked you for anything. I have fought alongside you, would have given my life to you many times over. And now there is something I must ask.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I want to look upon you, where no one else can see, where it is just us. I want to see your face, the color of your skin. I wish to see what the Handmaiden sees when she looks at you that causes her heart to race, and her tone to change. What causes her to forsake her heritage, her oaths... as you made me forsake mine. I... need to know if perhaps I am wrong, if the universe is not as my Master once showed me.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «If you will not abide my presence, and I cannot turn you from your course, then there is one last thing I must tell you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «From the moment I heard your voice across the galaxy, I have longed for you. It is the echo, a wound that travels still, that when heard, made me understand that there was another wounded as I was, one who had felt the same sense of loss. When I heard it, I loved you more than my own life. And I wanted you to be here, with me, for as long as you will let me love you. Stay. And I want you to see what I see when I gaze upon you. I want you to know why I cannot look at you, and why I am drawn to you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I know. And yet it was something that had to be spoken - if nothing but to hear the echo.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «It was something that had to be spoken - if nothing but to hear the echo. I will await you, and if you do not return, then I shall wait until my life ends, then we shall meet again, in the places where the Force does not touch.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Forgive me.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- «I sensed the conflict on Onderon even from this dense jungle. Did everything go as you planned?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Onderon will stop bleeding for a time. But wounded things rarely stay mended for long.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Why bother? War is the natural state of things. All matter seeks to either assimilate or destroy other matter.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «You may tell us nothing, but the events on Onderon have marked you in a way that even I can see.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- «Apathy is death.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- «A dark Jedi left her impression here. This door has been sealed intentionally.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «Ah yes, I felt this Jedi's death shortly after we arrived on Korriban.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Sith_Tomb]**
- «I can taste the power of the dark side here. Freedon Nadd must have been truly great to leave such an impact. The echoes of his life are still here. I feel secrets can be learned here.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- «The source of the power of this place is behind that door... it is strong, indeed. It must be what has drawn the Sith here... and drawn the attention of my Master.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «Listen - the wind from the cave tells of great power within... recently awakened.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «This door was left open on purpose. Someone is expecting us.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K2/Visas]**
- «I have felt it, too, my lord... a disturbance in the Force.» [K2/Visas]
- «It was difficult to make out, my lord. At first it was such a quiet thing, I did not notice it. But now I wonder if it has always been there, I merely could not hear it before. The sound built so slowly, yet when you listen for it, you can make out the strains, even over the background life of the universe.» [K2/Visas]
- «Do you feel it is a thr-» [K2/Visas]
- «You... you are the darkness in which all life dies, milord. All life... exists to feed your power, and my life... my life is yours.» [K2/Visas]
- «I beg you... please... let me die.» [K2/Visas]
- «Yes... this disturbance... echoes through the Force. I can follow it to its source... and bring it to you.» [K2/Visas]
- «I will leave at once, my lord.» [K2/Visas]
- «My lightsaber... you have destroyed it. I yield... master. It is as I heard through the Force. My life... for yours.» [K2/Visas]
- «You *must.* The alternative is only another death... and I would rather die by your hands.» [K2/Visas]
- «I will do as you ask... but I fear the answers you seek of me... would be useless.» [K2/Visas]
- «*Kill* me, I beg you. I... it was not my wish to challenge you... and I *must* pay the price.» [K2/Visas]
- «I... have nothing to offer you. Your strength is superior... it is as I felt.» [K2/Visas]
- «[You have scavenged a part from Visas Marr's lightsaber.]» [K2/Visas]
- «My life for yours.» [K2/Visas]
- «I am able to serve. If we enter battle, I will fight and die alongside you.» [K2/Visas]
- «I... I have not heard that question in some time. My flesh is... healed, if that's the answer you seek.» [K2/Visas]
- «I know. And I fear that others will see the mercy in your actions... and in my survival... and use it as a weapon to do you greater harm.» [K2/Visas]
- «The scars are many, and the causes equally so. It is of no importance.» [K2/Visas]
- «I... felt you, heard you, through the Force. It was like a sound, at the edge of hearing. And when I heard it, I found I could not ignore it.» [K2/Visas]
- «I serve my Master. I am an emissary, a scout. My Master was aware of a disturbance in the Force, but was unaware of its nature, of you. The disturbance is not something one feels from a living thing. There is little my Master does not know, and that you eluded his sight for so long... is significant, but I do not know why.» [K2/Visas]
- «You cannot. His vessel roams the borders of known space, and even I do not know where he travels.... until he... calls for me. Even if I could lead you to my Master, I cannot permit you to find him... until you are ready.» [K2/Visas]
- «If I bring you before my Master, untested, without your potential realized, then you will be lost to me. And I cannot allow that to happen. It would be as if one brought fire to a paradise valley, shattered a cavern of rare crystal... or blinded a painter.» [K2/Visas]
- «I cannot - I will not. I would die first, and gladly, to preserve you, untouched, unharmed. Now that I have found you, I cannot sacrifice what I have found.» [K2/Visas]
- «It is a choice that can be made by neither one of us. Do not be so quick to meet that which you do not understand. Use the time you have now, to grow, to train, and to strengthen yourself.» [K2/Visas]
- «You will meet my master. It is inevitable, I have... seen it. And when you stand before him, and realize what you face, you must be prepared. Until then, I must protect you, help you, until you are ready.» [K2/Visas]
- «There is a... a greatness in you, a greatness that does not stem from the Force. It stems from who you are. And if my Master does not understand you, cannot see you, then perhaps there is hope for us all. But if you seek to survive, then you must understand why this is so.» [K2/Visas]
- «There is much I see my Master cannot. I fear it is because of my nature, the nature of my race. My people spend their lives seeing the galaxy, the energy streaming off stars, the growth of life - all things touched by the Force.» [K2/Visas]
- «They are... gone. There's nothing more that I can say.» [K2/Visas]
- «Forgive me, but before you go, I must ask. Why do you do this? Why do you seek to help me, teach me?» [K2/Visas]
- «You must not do this. I cannot allow you to weaken yourself for me.» [K2/Visas]
- «I must warn you... if you weaken yourself for me, then it will kill you.» [K2/Visas]
- «So you say, but it is not something I have observed - or seen.» [K2/Visas]
- «There is more to the Sith than simply rage or slaughter. There are many kinds of hate, and all of them have the call of the dark side in them. The galaxy is filled with many such evils - and rarely have I seen anything else.» [K2/Visas]
- «I remember little of my homeworld before I entered my Master's service. It is not as it was, there is little left of such memories - or the planet itself.» [K2/Visas]
- «Very well. Perhaps we shall speak more of this at another time - but know this - I cannot allow you to weaken yourself for me.» [K2/Visas]
- «I will answer what I can, but my answers may prove useless to you.» [K2/Visas]
- «I will answer what I can.» [K2/Visas]
- «I am not familiar with the place you speak of - my Master has not entered Republic space for some time. But there are others, who may move more freely, who may have been responsible for such an act of destruction.» [K2/Visas]
- «My Master did not cause the end of the planet you speak of.» [K2/Visas]
- «There are many factions within the Sith, all seeking to take what little remains in the wake of the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Visas]
- «[Influence: Success] It is not a subject which I have spoken of - since its destruction.» [K2/Visas]
- «[Influence: Failure] It is not something I speak of. The homeworld is no longer, and that is all.» [K2/Visas]
- «I have heard tales of Malachor. It is said that many of my people felt the end of the Mandalorian Wars from across the galaxy. But do not mistake me, I did not mean to draw comparisons between Katarr and Malachor... my homeworld still exists. It is... intact.» [K2/Visas]
- «The planet was not destroyed, it remains... it orbits, dead in space, but nothing lives on its surface. It echoes, but there is no one left to hear it.» [K2/Visas]
- «I am not certain I did. I was there when the planet died. To see everything around you extinguished... it... was as if I was blinded. It was as if the Force had... been bled from the world.» [K2/Visas]
- «I imagine there are worse deaths, worse pain. But if there are, I do not know them. I was the only living thing remaining on the planet of Katarr... and my life, my agony, was a flicker in the darkness that was the planet. All that I had been connected to had been severed.» [K2/Visas]
- «I still wonder what would have happened if I had died with the others... if perhaps there would have been some way to hide my presence from the galaxy. If only I had not... felt that pain, that loss, as strongly as I did. But it could not be done. When the life was bled from the planet, and yet somehow, I remained, my Master came for me. He walked upon the surface of my dead world, and there, lying in the bodies of my race, he took me for his own. And he made me *see.* And for the first time, I saw the galaxy. And I wished to die.» [K2/Visas]
- «To this galaxy, my world, absent the currents and spectrums of the Force, was nothing but crude matter, rock, flesh, emptiness. He showed the flickering of life on other planets, the mass of beings that swarm through the empty places of the galaxy. To see such creatures, disconnected from themselves, their world, their place in it, unable to see the currents and how they affected everything around them.» [K2/Visas]
- «He showed me to make me believe in his cause. He convinced me the galaxy, all life must die. He fed upon its ugliness, its screaming, and in its place, he left silence... and where there was chaos, he brought stillness... and order.» [K2/Visas]
- «It was not a thing done with machines or weapons. The Force is far more terrible, and it touches more lives than any machine can hope to slay. For every one that feels the Force, strongly, deeply, each one feels and perceives it in their own way. You have strengths, whether you know it or not. And my master has his. His power is great, and it comes from hunger. He is a wound in the Force, more presence than flesh, and in his wake, life dies... sacrificing itself to his hunger. And those who feel the Force strongly are beacons to his hunger. My people, my planet, would have been attacked in time, it was inevitable, yet we could do nothing about it.» [K2/Visas]
- «The Jedi, the last Council of the Jedi, came to our world to meet in secret. They hoped that perhaps among our people they could achieve the clarity to *see* what was striking them from the darkness of the galaxy. They succeeded... but only in bringing him from the Outer Regions. And Katarr, with my kind, with the Jedi upon its surface, could no longer be ignored. And my people died. And the Jedi died. And there was no one left. Only me.» [K2/Visas]
- «They hoped to see the threat that had been stalking them. And they did. But they were unprepared for the magnitude of the threat.» [K2/Visas]
- «He cannot deny his hunger for long - and any gathering of Jedi is something he cannot long resist. And now that the Jedi are vanishing, I do not know what will happen - perhaps he will grow strong enough to eradicate all life, merely with his presence.» [K2/Visas]
- «My people once had the power to perceive events, to *see* through the Force. That sight may manifest itself in many ways, and at times, I may affect the abilities of others to see as well.» [K2/Visas]
- «Is there something else you wished?» [K2/Visas]
- «My sight has been... damaged. What I have taught you - it is not the full extent of the perceptions of my people.» [K2/Visas]
- «[Influence: Failure] My master - he has crippled me. I have seen the galaxy as it truly is, and it has left its mark. It is not something I can explain, for the words are difficult. I only ask that you understand.» [K2/Visas]
- «[Influence: Success] My master - when he showed me my world, showed it to me as it is, it... hurt. And since that moment, it has been... difficult to perceive the Force as I once did. But after traveling with you, I feel that perhaps there was a gift in it, hidden beneath the pain.» [K2/Visas]
- «Only when one suffers do certain truths become evident - both of the galaxy, and of the self. And I feel you are an example of this.» [K2/Visas]
- «[Influence: Success] If you wish to know, perhaps it is possible to show another what my people see... what I see.» [K2/Visas]
- «[Influence: Failure] It is difficult to explain, it is something that must be experienced to be understood.» [K2/Visas]
- «First, you must close your eyes... the surface of this ship, its sights will only be a distraction. Now, in your mind, reach out, listen for my breathing. Do not focus on the sound, but the life behind it. Imagine its energy, its texture, in tandem with the breathing - and then, in your mind, step back from the image, and see what remains.» [K2/Visas]
- «There... it is not as difficult as I thought - you learn quickly.» [K2/Visas]
- «It will take effort to maintain such sight, but you now have that power. And with it, you can use it to see life around you in a different way... as I used to see it.» [K2/Visas]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Visas Marr (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- Master Vrook: «Yes... the Miraluka world of Katarr taught us that. Many Jedi gathered there, in an attempt to use the Force to find the location of the enemy.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «The Miraluka world of Katarr taught us a terrible lesson. Many Jedi gathered there, in an attempt to use the Force to find the location of the enemy.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- Twi'lek Domo: «[[../Visas» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- Goto: «There is something moving in the galaxy that lies beyond the ability of my instruments to detect or predict. I believe it to be a legacy of the Sith, but I have been unable to determine the source. Whatever this presence is, it is staging strikes at key figures throughout the Republic - and through some unknown means, it is causing the destruction of worlds. Katarr, a Miraluka world in the Mid-Rim, was one such place. I have reason to suspect there was a gathering of Jedi on that world when it was rendered lifeless. I cannot find any pattern in these attacks, and it a source of frustration to me. There is some clue, however, that perhaps the Jedi are linked to these attacks - or that the targets are significant in some way I have yet to discover.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «It is a long story, but there is no harm in you knowing. And someone should know. Only a handful of us remained after the Jedi Civil War, barely a hundred in number. Then even that hundred began to vanish, in places where the Force seemed blind. The only pattern we determined that when Jedi gathered, they were seen no more. At the last Jedi conclave on the Miraluka world of Katarr, the entire planet was wiped out. An entire race, destroyed... because the Jedi chose to gather there. It was only then that we realized we were facing something far more powerful than we knew how to fight.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Kreia: «I have seen what you have seen - the slow seduction of the Sith. Perhaps we are more allies than you know. I fear the exile has let his feelings for the Miraluka affect his judgment - and it will doom him.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- News Hologram: «One of the greatest mysteries in recent memory is the dissolution of the Jedi Order. Today, Republic intelligence analysts have released a declassified report that sheds new light into this mystery. It is public knowledge that the Jedi Order suffered extreme casualties during the Jedi Civil War. But the new intelligence report indicates evidence of even greater casualties after the war. Murders, accidents, and the Miraluka tragedy had a profound impact on the number of Jedi. The report claims that there are even bounties on the remaining Jedi. The report concluded that the Jedi Order may be completely extinct now. Whether this was coincidence or conspiracy is still unknown.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Kreia: «He... if he can truly be called a man any longer... is one of the dark lords that pursues you. I do not think he knows what you are, not yet. He spared the Miraluka, and that may have been the last shred of feeling that exists within him. Keep his slave close to you. I suspect there was a reason he spared her... and perhaps a reason that she survived when the rest of her people and the Jedi did not.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «It is not something he can direct or focus, much like hunger itself. He is more of a hole in the Force than a living thing. Force Sensitives and worlds rich in the Force draw him. The Miraluka world was one such place. That is why where the Jedi gather, Jedi will die. He will feel it, unless they mask their presence - but Katarr called out as a beacon to him, and he could not resist it. And he cares nothing for the Sith or its teachings... or the Jedi. And when the Jedi are dead, he will feed on the galaxy, the Republic, and eventually, consume the Sith as well.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Mira]**
- Mira: «Heh... for a minute there, I thought you and Visas... but it's just the Force.» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- Master Kavar: «The last Jedi conclave was on Katarr, a Miraluka colony. And all of Katarr was destroyed, all of the Jedi killed...» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Visas]**
- Atton: «Now I've seen everything. This woman... she's a Miraluka. I didn't think any were left in this part of the galaxy.» [K2/Visas]

You will have to meet him in battle.» [K2/Kreia] (anchor: Visas is not his slave, not any longer....)
- [context-resolved] Kreia: «Perhaps he is bound to her... as I am bound to you. If so, there may be a death served by hers.» [K2/Kreia] (anchor: Visas is not his slave, not any longer....)
- [context-resolved] Kreia: «You must be prepared to sacrifice the blinded one. Perhaps her death will buy you the time you need to deal with her master.» [K2/Kreia] (anchor: Visas is not his slave, not any longer....)
- [context-resolved] Kreia: «Her species does not see as we do. They perceive the galaxy through the Force... and it is how she found you. It is a rare gift, squandered on her people.» [K2/Kreia] (anchor: The Miraluka? Why do you call her a seer?...)
- [context-resolved] Kreia: «Despite your... intentions, it would be better to deal with her now. Whether you intend salvation or slavery, she is a threat to us.» [K2/Kreia] (anchor: The Miraluka? Why do you call her a seer?...)
- [context-resolved] Kreia: «Despite your... urges, it would be better to deal with her now. Whether you intend salvation or slavery, she is a threat to us.» [K2/Kreia] (anchor: The Miraluka? Why do you call her a seer?...)
- [context-resolved] Kreia: «She serves one of the greatest of the Sith, she is the most trusted... and only apprentice. Yet you spare her. Why?» [K2/Kreia] (anchor: The Miraluka? Why do you call her a seer?...)

- [context-resolved] Kreia: «The blinded one shall return to her homeworld, and she shall look upon the surface of that world, and perhaps at last see what she was meant to see. ### Quote bank D: lines addressed to Visas Marr (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K2/Bridge]**
- Mandalore: «What the hell are you doing up there? We need to get off this ship. Now.» [K2/Bridge]

**[K2/Entertainment_Module_081]**
- Mandalore: «Kelborn, Zuka, you will be joining us.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- Mandalore: «Are your preparations complete?» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- Twi'lek Domo: «You yourself are quite a specimen. Perhaps you would like to find work as one of Vogga's dancers?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Follow me. I will escort you to see Vogga. Do not be nervous, or he will not be pleased.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Handmaiden: «I won't let you harm him. I have heard of your kind... you worship strength, when all it does is rot you from within.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «I will not reveal my mistress... and I will not allow you to harm the one I travel with.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Protect him? I do not believe you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Be silent! You know nothing of what you speak.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Mira: «I don't know what the two of you are arguing about. I caught him, he's mine.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Influence]**
- Hanharr: «Are you so weak that you must use cunning to triumph?» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «Then your allies are your weakness, and if they die, you die with them. And because of that, where once the Sith had but one target, now there are many, and you frustrate my attempts to protect you.» [K2/Influence]
- Atton: «Let's not make this a habit - I don't want your good nature rubbing off on me.» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «And so you waste effort upon another.» [K2/Influence]
- Atton: «I don't understand what that accomplished, but if it makes you feel better, fine.» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «To bestow mercy on another is but another way of proclaiming your superiority.» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «I am not speaking to you, fool. Be silent!» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «I grow weary of this. Perhaps time will allow my words to take root if your common sense will not permit it.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- Sakarie: «I see that's grabbed your interest, eh? There's only one way you're going to get it - one open starport visa. No visa, no crystal.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Handmaiden: «That was a noble thing you did. I hope she and her family prosper elsewhere.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- Mandalore: «While you're at it, tell him to forget he ever saw us. Could be useful.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]
- Checkpoint Guard: «[Success] Not only is your business not my concern, I can't seem to remember why I'm holding you up.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- Xarga: «My troops will keep the path to the Mandalorian camp clear. Ahead lies the enemy. I can follow you a little bit further, but then I will set up command and control for my unit in the field. So lead on.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Korriban]**
- Atton: «We've hit the ground. This is Korriban. Why would one of the Jedi you're looking for come here?» [K2/Korriban]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Kreia: «You have brushed the surface thoughts of another. It is a start. Calm yourself. This time, silence your own thoughts, keep them still. Imagine the waters of the Room of a Thousand Fountains, each stream suddenly falling silent and still. Imagine the ice of Telos, cold and smooth, as it gathers upon the plateau. Now stretch out, feel the ship around you. Strip away the metal, and see the souls and minds of those that fill its corridors, with more thoughts and dreams and worries than can fill the space of this ship.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- Handmaiden: «What are you talking about? What is wrong?» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- Mira: «I'll do it, but you're still my bounty. You won't lose me this easily.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- Mira: «Be that way. But if I didn't have to keep track of you I'd finish this job.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Kreia: «Whatever course you wish to take, aiding this woman may be useful. We may gain Vaklu's favor or learn more of his plans. Either way, helping Anda should be carefully considered.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Devaronian: «But if you can't trust your leader's words, then how can you trust their motives? Human sentient, what do you think?» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Twi'lek: «I also confess, I am curious what you think.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Twi'lek: «You see, I told you. Even the outsider can see the wisdom in supporting General Vaklu.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Kreia: «Loyalty can prove an effective wall to your talents. But there may be a way to undermine it... if one looks for it.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Bostuco: «I suggest you move along, off-worlder... before you test my patience any further.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- Atton: «Welcome to Nar Shaddaa: towering buildings kilometers high and miles deep, with canyons so wide you could have a dogfight in them. Word of warning - watch where you step, or you'll fall for hours.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Handmaiden: «I do not wish your support, Dark Jedi - your words only undermine my arguments.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Handmaiden: «You will not come near the Jedi - I will not allow it.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Disciple: «It is not my place to say whether you should aid us in our search. If you do, however, you may cause the Jedi we seek to mistake our intentions.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]**
- Mandalore: «Nothing you need to worry about, Sith.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Mandalore: «Don't get all sentimental, I just don't trust you to get the job done. Been waiting for this for ten years. It's just time to do things the old-fashioned way.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Mandalore: «This ship is from Malachor. This Sith Lord of yours bolsters his fleet with the ships from that world... he's nothing more than a scavenger.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «Waiting for you? No, he does not wait for you.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «You are dust motes in a storm... a grain upon the beach... and as insignificant as a body that orbits the graveyard of Malachor. Fight him if you will... but if he turns his power upon you, you will be destroyed.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- Mira: «About time you showed up. We finished our part and have been sitting on our hands waiting for you. So, what happened?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Atton: «Hey, the gang's back together. I must hear all about your vacation in Onderon.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Mira: «Hm. We'll see if Onderon's newfound 'peace' lasts as long as your tremendous ego.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Atton: «You think you're hot stuff, don't you?» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- Mandalore: «You smell that? It's the scent of death and overpopulated beasts coming from that cave.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
