## Mandalore (Canderous Ordo)
*Corpus coverage: 195 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 149 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 1 pronoun references resolved from context · 47 lines addressed to them · 30 narration lines · 16 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Mandalore is Canderous from K1, now leader of the Mandalorian clans: «I have reclaimed Mandalore's helmet, lost after our defeat at Revan's hands.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]. He fought at Malachor V [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins].
### Personality and motivations
Rebuilding the clans; respects Revan.
### Arc (scene by scene)
- **Dxun:** the Mandalorian camp and tasks.
- **Onderon:** advises backing Vaklu.
- **Dantooine courtyard:** recruitment.
- **Ravager:** leads the assault.
- Traya predicts more battles for him [K2/Trayus_Core].
### Relationships
- Exile.
- Revan (follows his will).
- Kreia (lectures him).
### Abilities
- Armour, implants, cunning [K2/Mandalore].
### Feats
- War veteran.
### Power-scaling statements
- «Only Revan was worthy of our respect.» [K2/Mandalore]
### Duels and fights
- Ravager assault.
### Verdict implications
- Non-Force elite.
### Open questions
- None.

### Abilities: game mechanics (FLAGGED: gameplay stats, not lore)
- [K2/Party] Mandalore: Class Soldier, Level 5. Feats: DC=6, DC=9, Improved Power Blast.

### Cut content (RCM)
- None found on the RCM-tagged lines for this character.

### Quote bank A: Mandalore (Canderous Ordo)'s own lines, by scene (page)

**[K2/Battle_Circle]**
- «You defeated Bralor in the battle circle? Either you're very lucky, or you're a lot tougher than you look.» [K2/Battle_Circle]

**[K2/Bridge]**
- «Her death was worthy of a Mandalorian.» [K2/Bridge]
- «What the hell are you doing up there? We need to get off this ship. Now.» [K2/Bridge]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- «Watch out for Azkul. I've known his kind before. He will attack you without hesitation if he believes you aren't working with him.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Docks]**
- «I'm not here for any mercenary work. I'm here to return your clan to Dxun, where I am restoring our people to their former glory.» [K2/Docks]
- «I intend to rectify that error.» [K2/Docks]

**[K2/Enclave_Courtyard]**
- «I know how this works. A mercenary or two can easily get work during the dry times as a bounty hunter or bodyguard. There's always odd jobs around. But large mercenary groups have to wait for things to get interesting to make their credits. It can be very lucrative and challenging work if there are battles to be fought.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- «I am the new Mandalore. I have reclaimed Mandalore's helmet, lost after our defeat at Revan's hands. Assemble the rest of your clan. You will return to Dxun. We must come together as we were before and prepare to fight a new war - a war that will return us to glory.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- «It is unfortunate that you feel that way. Perhaps I'll be able to change your mind.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- «Any other takers?» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- «Good. I have no desire to spill any more Mandalorian blood today. Gather your clan and return to Dxun. You have seen the last of your mercenary days.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]

**[K2/Entertainment_Module_081]**
- «We will join you in the assault. These rank and file soldiers will do you no good aboard the Ravager. Let we Mandalorians lead the charge into battle and to victory.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- «Kelborn, Zuka, you will be joining us.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- «Are your preparations complete?» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- «This must be the nerve center of the ship. I say we take it out.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/Influence]**
- «We shouldn't waste our time with the weak.» [K2/Influence]
- «If you showed that kind of compassion in the Mandalorian War, the Republic would have lost.» [K2/Influence]
- «If you want to help someone, let them stand on their own feet.» [K2/Influence]
- «The weak don't deserve mercy.» [K2/Influence]
- «I wouldn't try that with a Mandalorian - they're likely to kill you for helping them.» [K2/Influence]
- «The strong take what they want. The weak fall.» [K2/Influence]
- «That one had it coming.» [K2/Influence]
- «Good - that ought to shut them up.» [K2/Influence]
- «Next one should go down faster.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- «Assuming we get Dhagon free, we're going to have a few private words about this. Together we fought against the Republic during the Mandalorian Wars. I don't particularly like the idea that he's working for them now.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «That bit of information will help with clearing Dhagon, but the Onderon military won't let him off that easy. They're not tough, but they are stubborn. Maybe someone in here will know more about what happened that night.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «That bit of information will help with clearing Dhagon, but the Onderon military won't let him off that easy. They're very obstinate. Maybe some of these people will know more about what happened that night.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «It might be a good idea to look at the crime scene. We fought many soldiers in the Mandalorian Wars. The Onderonians were certainly brave but they were disorganized and stupid. I wouldn't be surprised if they overlooked something.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Nobody we talked with mentioned a droid. This droid was destroyed recently. Someone inside the cantina might know more.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Kiph in the cantina can probably do something with this. We should head back.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Kavar, huh? The famed Jedi guardian? The Mandalorians counted on the fact it would be you, not Revan, who would lead the Jedi against us during the Mandalorian Wars. I wonder how we'd have fared against you. I thought you were killed fighting Malak during the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «There's a chance that non-combatants could be hurt here. I recommend we avoid using grenades.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Vaklu is a political animal, but he has never backed out of a deal. The Sith must have changed their arrangement substantially for him to consider betraying them. You can trust his offer. He's far more capable than Queen Talia is, I think allying with him is the best course.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Credibility is important in Onderon politics. If he betrayed you and betrayed the Sith, his reign would be a short one.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «No, you didn't. Make up your own mind then.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «I'll vouch for him. I know him from across a battlefield, that's the best way to learn about someone.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Not all of the military is after us. Still, Vaklu can throw a whole army at us. I think we've outworn our welcome over here. We should head over to the spaceport before General Vaklu invents some charges against us. Then we'd be in real trouble.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «The shuttle is just ahead. I think we should get out of here fast. It's going to be some time before they forget about us here. No more trips to Iziz for us until the situation changes. A lot.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «We're back. The shuttle is going to stay docked here until further notice. I'm not ready to part company yet. It sounds like you have a lot of traveling to do. So do I. I'm going with you. The Sith have taken a particular interest in you, and you could use an extra blaster. I've been meaning to leave Dxun to look for other Mandalorian clans, anyway.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Mandalore leads *all* of the Mandalorian people, not just a single clan. In order to revive our people the clans must be reassembled under me.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «They could be anywhere. You seem a magnet for trouble. Mandalorians are, too. I have a feeling that if we travel together it would work to our mutual advantage.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «I don't have it in me. It's a matter of self-interest.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «I'm going to look for the other clans with or without you. At least this way you'll have another ally against the Sith.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «The Sith aren't known for sharing power. If the Jedi Order is destroyed for good, it's inevitable that the Sith will dominate the Republic. And then my people will be eradicated or enslaved. Helping your cause is a matter of necessity. Mandalorians helped Exar Kun during his war. We know first hand how we'd fare in the service of their like.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «But that's enough for now. We'll have time to talk later.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «It's up to you, Jedi. This soldier knows the terrain, and can get us where we need to go quickly... without needing to blast our way through herds of cannoks.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «So, you fought against the Mandalorians in the war?» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «But you fought on the front lines. To a Mandalorian, there is honor in that.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «If I were you, I'd pick your words more carefully.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Maybe that's what it looked like to you, but that isn't why we fought. We fought for honor and glory in the heat of battle.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «The Republic took us too lightly. We wanted to face the full force of their army. We had to goad them to fight.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Defeat is part of a warrior's life. We will recover, stronger than before.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «People die in war.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Fine by me.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- «Here we are, the city of Iziz. It's been shut down tight for months now. General Vaklu is close to declaring martial law. We won't be able to travel too far in the city. Fortunately I have a friend in this quarter of the city. He's a doctor by the name of Dhagon Ghent. His office is on the other side of the market square. It might be best if you do the talking around here. The Onderonians have a mixed view of Mandalorians after we conquered their world.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]
- «While you're at it, tell him to forget he ever saw us. Could be useful.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- «Kelborn said you dispatched some covert military scouts in the jungle with him. He spoke highly of your work.» [K2/Jungle]
- «Kelborn told me that they were covert military and probably aligned with Colonel Tobin from Iziz. Kelborn doesn't think they'll send out any more scouts. But that doesn't mean there aren't more of them out there.» [K2/Jungle]
- «Colonel Tobin is General Vaklu's right-hand man. Despite his martial skill he handles himself as a functionary more than a warrior. Back when we were fighting Vaklu's resistance during the Mandalorian Wars, Vaklu wouldn't have any use for a poisonous kinrath like Tobin. But Vaklu seems to have adapted well to peacetime.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- «This isn't the right way.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Khoonda]**
- «I sincerely hope that history doesn't remember our wars as a footnote to Revan and Malak's. But it's good to see that people still remember it with fear.» [K2/Khoonda]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- «I would hardly call the mercenaries that were here Mandalorians. Without a leader many Mandalorians fell far from the path of honor.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- «...if I upped the tibanna gas levels in the carbine that would be enough to punch a hole even in triple-durasteel... and we'll need weapons like that if the Republic discovers the camp on Dxun...» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Mandalore]**
- «Yeah? What do you want?» [K2/Mandalore]
- «The Republic is a stagnant beast, that had been killing its people for years before the wars began. Were it not for Revan's strength, the Republic would already be dead.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Only Revan was worthy of our respect. We swept through the Outer Rim without any opposition - until Revan assumed command of the Republic forces.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Open your eyes. The ease of our attack was as much a testament to the strength of the Mandalorian clans as it was to the weakness of the Republic.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Only then did the battle turn. But as you know, only a handful of Jedi fought, while the others cowered in their halls, afraid to meet us head on.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «I'm sure there were good reasons.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Then the Jedi were fools as well as cowards.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «I'm not accusing you of being a coward. You commanded the Republic fleet that destroyed ours. You were a worthy opponent.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Now, was there something else you needed?» [K2/Mandalore]
- «The Republic thinks my people are dead, scattered. They are wrong. You should have destroyed us when you had the chance. As long as one Mandalorian lives, we will survive. Our honor, fortunes, and lives revolve around battle. From our earliest history, this has been our way, as it will be, always.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Our history and our ways are something we shield from outsiders. Only on the battlefield can you truly learn of someone.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «There are, but those are luxuries. Luxuries that were paid for by the blood of our warriors - alive and dead.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Which is one of the reasons I travel with you. It pleases me that you recognize the truth.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «The Mandalorian clans will be united. Then we will take back our place in the galaxy. But we live in times of action, and I've said enough for now. We can speak of this again later.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «I am Mandalore, leader of the Mandalorians. I will help you fight your battles and plan your strategy. But I will not be your errand boy. Find some other lackey to do your bidding.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «I thought that the Force would be enough of a weapon for a Jedi like yourself. Need a little something extra to work up your courage in a fight?» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Right. That makes sense. I'm sure the others are just too embarrassed to ask for themselves, so they sent you in their place.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «We wouldn't want to let them down. Here - and don't get too attached to them, I only have time to make so many.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «If you say so.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «That's a good position for a warrior to take, but even better if he relies completely on his own strength.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Here you go. But don't get too attached to them, I only have time to make so many.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Already gave you my last one. Go easy on them - even a strong warrior can only take so much.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Like us, the people of Onderon are a warriors. Early in their history they built Iziz to protect them from the beasts that swarmed on their world. No matter how far they've progressed, they're fundamentally still warriors standing watch on their battlements. They don't like outsiders and their inability to take ideas from other cultures gives them a fundamental disadvantage in warfare. They fell quickly during the Mandalorian Wars despite their martial skills. Their technology and doctrine are not in the same league as Mandalorians.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «I've fought countless battles - ones most didn't walk away from. The wounds add up - and even the implants are all that keeps me going sometimes. I may have been stronger in my youth - and stupider - but now I've got to rely more on my armor, my weapons, and my cunning.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «You fought with Revan and Malak during the Mandalorian Wars. Why?» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Hnh. We'd never faced Jedi before - and didn't know the threat they represented. You've always been in the Jedi Order. Things that are common knowledge to you are rumors and stories to the rest of the galaxy. We only knew what we had seen in holovids and from our experience with Exar Kun, and by that time he was completely a Sith. We thought we would triumph easily over such 'noble' and 'compassionate' leaders. Those are weaknesses we easily exploited in the past. What did you think of us?» [K2/Mandalore]
- «That so? Ever consider what would have happened if we'd won?» [K2/Mandalore]
- «War breeds butchers on both sides of the battle lines, Jedi. You know it better than anyone. It's just that we were better at it than anyone the Jedi ever faced.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «And that is how we thought of you. Not of the Jedi in particular, but the men and women who joined Revan and Malak to fight against us. You turned a demoralized, defeated mess into a coordinated army. You brought tactics, backbone, and above all else, victory, to them.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Fair enough. But let me share some of my opinions.» [K2/Mandalore]
- «Ever consider what would have happened if we'd won?» [K2/Mandalore]
- «The Sith would have been nothing more than a border skirmish against the might of my clans. We would have brought a new age of strength and expansion to the Republic, with the wealth of the Core worlds providing arms and warships to fuel our growth. Instead, the Republic 'won.' Look around you. Look at what your precious victory has brought you. I ask you this - is the galaxy better off for the Republic's victory?» [K2/Mandalore]
- «I suppose we'll never know.» [K2/Mandalore] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Just think about it.» [K2/Mandalore]

**[K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]**
- «So you're the intruder? Our sensors picked up your handiwork in space.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «So you're the one that picked a fight with my sentries. The sensors also picked up your battle in space. You've had a pretty busy day. That ends now. No more fighting in our camp.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «I am Mandalore, leader of the Mandalorians.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Mandalore is the name we give to our leader. Becoming Mandalore is the greatest honor any Mandalorian could aspire to. When our leader falls in battle, the most worthy takes his place. So even though your army killed our leader, it was only a matter of time before we chose someone new. Me.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Scattered, perhaps, but we're still alive. Alive and rebuilding.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Many Mandalorians have fallen from the path of honor and are now no more than common mercenaries. But that is changing.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «This used to be the heart of the Mandalorian war effort. From this complex we commanded an armada that had the Republic on the run. It didn't last.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Mandalorians have a rapport with this jungle. Every moment here is a struggle, all creatures gripped in a constant war for survival. The sole purpose of the weak is to feed the strong. We train here and learn the lessons of the jungle. The beasts also help us keep our edge.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Covert camps are not meant to attract attention. Because we conquered them, the people of Onderon still hold a grudge against the Mandalorians. So we keep our presence here a secret.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «So, it's transportation you want? It so happens I have a small shuttle that's more than capable of running the Onderon military blockade. I make occasional trips to Iziz for information and supplies. If you want to go with me, you're going to have to prove your worth.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «It's Mandalorian business, not yours.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «[Failure] That's not going to work. I've traveled with Jedi before, so I know all about your tricks. If you want something from me you're going to have to convince me the old fashioned way.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «[Failure] I could - but I don't know you. Once I get to know you I might tell you more, but not now.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «I don't travel with anyone I'm not sure of. You look capable, but Iziz can be a dangerous place. If you want to travel on my shuttle, I want to make sure you aren't going to be a liability.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Figure it out yourself. Ask around, see if you can make yourself useful. Or something that'll show what you're made of.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «I'll let Kex know that you check out. We've found more gear than we can use, so you can trade with him if you need some more supplies. Be careful in the jungle. Our patrols have stopped until the space traffic dies down. The challenging beasts have been cleared from the area, but what's left might still be too much for you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «What do you want?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins] *(+3 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «So far you haven't done anything. Check back when you've done something worth my time.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Not yet, Jedi. Find some more to do around here. There's a jungle full of trouble out there.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «You've made quite a reputation around here. You did better than I thought you could. I was planning on heading to Iziz tomorrow. But I'll move up the timetable and take you now. You won't be able to get to the Ebon Hawk from the city. If you need to grab anything from your ship, I'd do it now.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Are you ready to go to Iziz yet?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «[[../Mandalorian Ruins#Shuttle Hangar» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «There is one thing. Before your ship landed we were preparing some demolition work. All the activity forced my men to stop before they finished. The charges need to be detonated before anybody comes across them. So all you'd have to do is flip a switch.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «We want to keep a low profile, so we've recalled all our patrols in the jungle. Setting off some high explosives isn't a textbook example of subtlety.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «You asked if there was anything you could do, and I told you. Nothing in this jungle is as simple as it seems, though.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «We were trying to uncover the entrance to a hidden cache of old Mandalorian equipment. The explosives should be easy to find. Just get to it before the cannoks do.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «The last months before Dxun fell, the old Mandalore knew that Revan and the Republic were gaining the upper hand. He ordered our best engineers to hide caches of weapons and munitions throughout the moon, safely away from the enemies' hands.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Don't you think they tried? It's real easy to hide things in a jungle. Even when they found some of our caches, our engineers left them some 'presents.'» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «They're not much more than a couple of plasteel cylinders. Before you get any bright ideas, if you uncover cache B13 leave it alone. Even if you survive the traps, it would require military-grade explosives to open without the proper access codes. What's inside belongs to the Mandalorians.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Cannoks are nothing more than pests, but they have a real talent for screwing up plans. Just get to those explosives in the next day or so, and you'll be fine.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «I know. It made quite a racket. Good work.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «That's to be expected. They don't really like it when someone makes loud noise in their territory.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «It wouldn't be much of a test if all you had to do was take a hike through the jungle now, would it? You're alive, in one piece, and learned something about the beasts of the jungle.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Ask around the camp. Someone else might have something you can do to lend a hand. I also promised you a reward. Here you go. This should help you fend off some of the local beasts. You're well on your way to earning some real respect around here. Keep it up.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «When you were last here, you probably didn't even go to Onderon. The Republic knew that Onderon's fate would be decided on its jungle moon, not the planet itself. To begin with, when you talk about Onderon you're really talking about the ancient walled city of Iziz. It's the only inhabited settlement on the planet.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «It depends on who you ask. Queen Talia rules from the Royal Palace. But her cousin, General Vaklu, has a great deal of power in Iziz, as well. They disagree about many things, just like their citizens. We fought General Vaklu during the war and he is a worthy adversary. Queen Talia is young, and hardly a match for him.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Onderon is a recent addition to the Republic, joining during one of the darker eras in Republic history. Since joining, the Republic has endured many wars and great battles. The people of Onderon don't possess the resolve and strength needed in these times. Many of them want to break away from the Republic. They want to go back to an earlier age when they could cower behind their walls. Their spinelessness made them easy to conquer.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «We're not self-sufficient here. We need supplies. Iziz is the only place to go for them in this system. Not much choice. Which is unfortunate because their animosity towards Mandalorians makes it inconvenient to go there.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «I don't blame you. They're a sad people. I have some things to look into. I'll talk to you later.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «The gate guard said you managed to kill a zakkeg. Those are tough beasts, Jedi. You've earned some respect around here.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Xarga told me that you returned one of our sheep to the herd. You have gained some small prestige by helping Kumus.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Zuka told me you helped out with some repairs around the base. I was hoping you'd prove yourself more with a lightsaber, but it's certainly a great help.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «How many cannoks did you have to kill to find those parts for Zuka? That was a tremendous help. I won't forget it.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «How are the port stabilizers?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Good. I want the shuttle bound for Onderon within the hour.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Hnh. It is, like I promised. Why, you want to back out now?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Don't pretend to understand us - we Mandalorians are a breed apart.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Not for long. Soon the Mandalorians will be strong again, united as one clan under one banner. Mine.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «I was at Malachor V. And I remember how many Jedi died to stop us there. And no matter how many dead orbit that planet, the Mandalorians still live. Clan Ordo still lives. See Kex there - he was serving on Nar Shaddaa as muscle for the Hutts. Kelborn was a scout for the Duros on frontier worlds. I brought them here, gave them a purpose. This galaxy will be ours again, I promise you. That is the future.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «You've got some guts, talking to me like that. You think your age or your Jedi whelp are going to keep you safe from me?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «How do you know that?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Forget the Jedi. Keep your eyes on her.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Seems trouble follows you on a regular basis. I think it's best for both of us if we head to Iziz immediately. Grab your gear. My men will take care of cleaning up the mess.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- «Dividing our forces at a time like this is foolhardy.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- «Why are you joining in this? I prefer Vaklu over Talia, but this is hardly a task for a warrior.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Shrewd. I hate to lower myself to joining this mob, but your plan may work.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Maybe I should've left you on Dxun.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «We should try to get General Vaklu to be on our side. He has a shrewd mind and is far more fit for command. Queen Talia is young and too idealistic. A veteran leader is what this planet needs and would make a better ally.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Vaklu is no fool. Whatever our past disagreements, he'll recognize the advantages of an alliance between us.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Why do you take that so personally. When you've been around as long as I have, it's just part of doing business.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Have it your way, then.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Twenty years would mean you were here during the Mandalorian Wars, right?» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «You mean after the Jedi drove the Mandalorians from Onderon. The Mandalorians never gave ground to any Onderon soldier.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- «Too many Mandalorians were scattered after the war. Without purpose and without direction, most became little more than raiders.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]**
- «And our cargo?» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Do the Republic forces suspect?» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Did you get an ID signature on this vessel?» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Then let's finish this. And remind the galaxy of Malachor V.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Nothing you need to worry about, Sith.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Don't get all sentimental, I just don't trust you to get the job done. Been waiting for this for ten years. It's just time to do things the old-fashioned way.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «What the hell was that? I told you to wait!» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Was it in the target zone?» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Do we have another core?» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «We need to find another proton core. Or we need to make one... all right, wait for my signal. This isn't over. And don't kill yourself before I tell you.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «This ship is from Malachor. This Sith Lord of yours bolsters his fleet with the ships from that world... he's nothing more than a scavenger.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «He means that Jedi witch you travel with – Kreia.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Hnh. This ship is barely holding itself together. The structural damage should have destroyed it long ago.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Hnh. More Jedi tricks.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «If he's so powerful, why hasn't he stopped us, then? We've attacked his ship, killed his soldiers, and he's done nothing.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- «Go report to the target site... Colonel. I'll let my men know you're coming... and if you try anything stupid, they'll shoot you dead.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- «I only see one Jedi here...» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- «That's ship's had some proud owners. You're disgracing it by giving it away like a durasteel chip.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- «You did the right thing. Leaving Vaklu alive would be foolhardy.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- «Apathy is death.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- «This Jedi hasn't been dead long.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Sky_Ramp]**
- «Looks like they still remember us.» [K2/Sky_Ramp] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I hope to build on that legacy.» [K2/Sky_Ramp] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «But now is not the time for talk. It's the time for action!» [K2/Sky_Ramp] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It's nice to see we're still remembered.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- «You should've seen it when the sky was filled with them. Their fighters crackling like small novas.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]

**[K2/Suburban_(082_West)]**
- «I must leave you for a time to gather my Mandalorian warriors. We will rejoin you when the time comes for the assault on the Ravager.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- «Don't worry about me, I can handle myself just fine. Look after your own health.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- «Watch yourself. I'll soften up the way for you, but I won't be here to hold your hand.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- «Is that your idea of a joke? Don't be a fool. I'll meet you at the shuttle.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «You smell that? It's the scent of death and overpopulated beasts coming from that cave.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Hmm... This looks like a trap, if I've ever seen one.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Western_Square]**
- «This is Dhagon Ghent's place. He's not the best doctor you'll ever meet, but he's well connected in this city. Doesn't look like he's here right now, though.» [K2/Western_Square]
- «Dhagon Ghent is the only person I know with the contacts to help us out. We need to get Dhagon out of custody.» [K2/Western_Square]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Mandalore (Canderous Ordo) (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Administration_Level]**
- Atton: «Yeah, Revan, Malak, and the Jedi that went to join them in the Mandalorian Wars. They turned against the other Jedi and had a scrap that almost laid waste to the galaxy. Heh. Where have you been?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «Well, I wasn't there, thankfully. But I heard what she was like during the Mandalorian Wars, and it sounded like she was quick to wipe out anyone who crossed her.» [K2/Administration_Level]

**[K2/Atton]**
- Atton: «You know what? Not once have I asked you about the Mandalorian Wars. Not once. I heard about Dxun. Everyone has. I heard about Serroco, and I sure as hell know about Malachor V. What makes you think you've got the right to interrogate me on anything? You've got plenty of lives to answer for - all you Jedi do.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Because you can't believe in the Republic anymore after the Mandalorian Wars. After Revan, nothing was the same.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «No, it wasn't. We needed the Jedi during the Mandalorian Wars, more than anything.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «[Influence: Success] After Malachor, after the Mandalorian Wars, that's when the Sith teachings started spreading through the ranks.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «I wasn't the only one. I know you left at the Mandalorian Wars, so you don't know much about what went on behind the scenes in the Jedi Civil War. But Revan understood one thing - the real battle was going to be fought between the Jedi on both sides. That was the only battle that mattered.» [K2/Atton]

**[K2/Bao-Dur]**
- Bao-Dur: «I felt the same way. I remembered when word of the Mandalorian attacks arrived on Iridonia.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «The war went poorly before Revan and the Jedi lent aid to the Republic. Many of us believed the Jedi to be cowards who were afraid to face the Mandalorian threat. I remembered when word of the Mandalorian attacks arrived on Iridonia.» [K2/Bao-Dur]

**[K2/Battle_Circle]**
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «Be careful when you ridicule a Mandalorian. If Mandalore hadn't ordered us to... Is there anything else you want to talk about?» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Tagren: «You have prowess in battle, this cannot be denied. But your Jedi teachings prevent you from having a true warrior's spirit. Jedi couldn't have beaten us in the Mandalorian Wars. It took a fallen Jedi to gain that honor.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Kelborn: «We're taught not to. Mandalorians want to be tested to the limits of our abilities and beyond. We admire anyone who beats us on the battlefield. Instead of hate we study, practice and train until the next time, so we won't be beaten. But some young Mandalorians find this a hard lesson. Any veteran of the Mandalorian Wars respects Jedi like you. Your actions on Dxun validate this. But we aren't a simple people.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Bralor: «So the Jedi Order isn't completely dead. You always had hidden strengths - very hard for even the wisest Mandalore to judge and predict. I have studied much of the Jedi since our defeat. I would be honored to cross blades with you in the battle circle.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Bralor: «You talk with the sergeant. Today is his day to preside over the circle. Each fight is different because the Mandalorian with greater honor dictates the terms of the engagement. But there will be no terms if we fight. Everyone else will deny you your lightsaber and the use of your "Force." But I want to fight you with all your skill. Victory isn't important in the battle circle. What is important is discovering more about yourself and your opponent.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Bralor: «So I have learned much. The last Jedi I fought was in ship to ship battle on Malachor V. I lost, but was spared. Before the Mandalorian Wars we looked at Jedi as weak. Their code restricted their full potential. The Jedi were limited by a narrow sense of morality that couldn't survive a real war. But our view was the narrow one. I see that clearly now. I sense we've both learned something today. We can fight again if you like.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Bralor: «And so I lose again. I still have much to learn. The last Jedi I fought was in a ship-to-ship battle on Malachor V. I lost, but was spared. Before the Mandalorian Wars we looked at Jedi as weak. Their code restricted their full potential. The Jedi were limited by a narrow sense of morality that couldn't survive a real war. But our view was the narrow one. I see that clearly now. I sense we've both learned something today. We can fight again if you like.» [K2/Battle_Circle]

**[K2/Bridge]**
- Admiral Onasi: «I don't know. But it was important to him. He said that he believed something had been behind the Mandalorian Wars. That it hadn't been the Mandalorians choice to attack the Republic. Whatever it was, I think he went off to find it... to fight it.» [K2/Bridge] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Admiral Onasi: «We saved the Republic. But it was like the war didn't end for him. He would keep remembering things that he'd done, and it kept driving him. And I think he finally remembered something terrible that he'd done during the Mandalorian Wars. And he went to put an end to it. He left a lot of people who cared about him behind. But I think he did it because he thought it was the right thing to do. But whatever he set out to do, I...I don't think he succeeded. And here you return, with his ship, without him.» [K2/Bridge]
- Admiral Onasi: «We saved the Republic. But it was like the war didn't end for her. She would keep remembering things that she had done, and it kept driving her. And she kept using it as a wall between us. And I think she finally remembered something terrible she had done during the Mandalorian Wars. And she went to put an end to it. She left without warning. She didn't say where, only that it was to a place where she could not take anyone she loved. And here you return, with her ship, without her.» [K2/Bridge]

**[K2/Crystal_Cave]**
- Master Vrook: «Disobeying the Jedi Council brought about more destruction than the Mandalorian Wars ever could - countless Jedi lost, the Republic broken, the...» [K2/Crystal_Cave]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- Zherron: «I was expecting something more, but you did better than I ever have. You reminded me of my instructor in the Mandalorian Wars.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Disciple]**
- Disciple: «Yes... these dead worlds, well, they have a pattern to them. They were all touched by the Mandalorian Wars and the Jedi Civil War... but sometimes, well, I feel as if they are all connected in some other way. The attacks on Katarr, Telos itself, the decay on Dantooine - something is wrong with life. The connections have been damaged, sickened. Sometimes I feel like I almost understand, and then it just slips away.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «That is my thought. The Republic is not without its flaws, but it is preferable to anarchy. Without supply lines and a standing military, the chaos that would result would be greater than the Mandalorian Wars.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I see. Have you ever been in a situation where you are dependent on another? You could not have won the Mandalorian Wars alone. In fact, I doubt you would have survived if you had not others to stand with you. Where are they now, now that you stand here, and they are gone? Did they deserve to die? Did you deserve to live, dependent on them as you were?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I see the Mandalorian Wars left their mark. Perhaps the Mandalorians won after all... just as they defeated Revan with their philosophy of slaughter.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Many Jedi went to go fight in the Mandalorian Wars, and few returned. To their disciples left behind on Dantooine on Coruscant, there were few masters to train them. I was one such student. Perhaps not as strong in the Force as the others, but it really did not matter. The teachers left behind were few - only a few students were accepted as the Mandalorian Wars raged on. And when I came of age, there were no masters left to train me. So I was forced to leave the Order, to seek my path elsewhere. It was for the best, I think. I had begun to have... doubts. And I think the masters were aware of them.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I have watched you. You have become strong in the Force again - but that is not all. You have achieved a center in the chaos around us, and I have felt it. My master - the one intended for me - left to fight in the Mandalorian Wars. Now she has returned, and I ask her now if she will train me in the ways of the Force.» [K2/Disciple]

**[K2/Docks]**
- Mandalorian: «Mandalore? But after Revan defeated Mandalore, no new one was chosen.» [K2/Docks]
- Mandalorian: «I have no doubt that you believe that you are Mandalore, but look at the situation we are in now. Anyone could buy a helmet from a swoop track and make such a claim. And the last time Mandalore traveled in the company of non-Mandalorians, we were pledged to Exar Kun.» [K2/Docks]
- Mandalorian: «[Success] A Jedi? Perhaps you are deserving of the title of Mandalore.» [K2/Docks]
- Mandalorian: «I heard rumors from Iziz about Mandalorian sightings on Dxun, so perhaps there is truth to what you say. Very well, Mandalore. My clan will follow you and make for Dxun that another clan might be added to the strength of your army.» [K2/Docks]

**[K2/Dormitories]**
- Kreia: «I realize that you have traveled alone ever since the Mandalorian Wars, but that time of self-reflection is over. ...and if you cannot see beyond yourself and your own needs, then you should know that I have the only means of accessing the Ebon Hawk's navicomputer. Without it, we both are trapped here.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Answer: It is beyond the scope of my programming to probe the motivations of my clients, master. Suffice to say that I am being well compensated for my services. You have been a difficult target to find. You have been wandering the galaxy since the end of the Mandalorian Wars, leaving little record of your passage. It is as if you did not wish to be found... by hunters such as myself, or more likely, the Jedi Order.» [K2/Dormitories]

**[K2/Enclave_Courtyard]**
- Dopak: «Most of us are veterans of the Jedi Civil War. Some even fought in the Mandalorian Wars. A lot of soldiers have been looking for a place in the galaxy. The really skilled usually wind up here.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Esok: «I've heard the rumors, too. There's no way I'm going to fight for some upstart warrior who styles himself as Mandalore. We didn't pick a new Mandalore when the last one was killed, and maybe we're better off without one.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Esok: «Look who's walked into our camp, boys. Mandalore himself has come to visit us.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Esok: «And if I take that helmet from you, guess that makes me the new Mandalore? I've always wanted to be called that. I know all about your exploits, "Mandalore." We have fallen far in the past years, but even still, I will not stand idly while a usurper claims to be my leader.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Mandalorian: «No, Mandalore. We will follow you.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Mandalorian: «Yes, Mandalore.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- Disciple: «Many Jedi defied the Order during the Mandalorian Wars - and it paved the way for the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/G0-T0]**
- G0-T0: «Perhaps you misunderstand me. I "care" for the Republic, but I have no choice. It is somewhat frustrating to be forced to love and care for such a mess of a government. Some of it may be blamed on the Jedi Civil War, the Mandalorian Wars, but not all. There are so many bad decisions that build upon each other that it is a wonder the Republic is intact at all.» [K2/G0-T0]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- Zez-Kai Ell: «There were a few within the Order who knew more than I did of such bonds - but their students were few, lost in the Mandalorian Wars.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «This form you may have already seen much of during the Mandalorian Wars - but it is fitting to speak using elements of the past, I think. [[../Forms#Aggression (Ataru)» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/HK-47]**
- HK-47: «Statement: Why, your own life, master. Your single life changed the face of the galaxy, of history itself. Malachor V was an impressive act of destruction, but its impact on the lives of others in the galaxy was far more extreme. I mean, master, you brought about the death of the Mandalorian race. I doubt they realize it yet, but you dealt them a blow from which they will never recover.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Query: I must express some degree of confusion concerning your actions. You are the one who followed Revan in the Mandalorian Wars, are you not?» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Observation: Well, master, I was not at Malachor V during your near-genocidal reaction to the Mandalorian threat, but I feel that I may have been constructed as a result of that.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Statement: It is a fact that the targeted extermination of certain individuals will bring war to a close quickly and efficiently. Leaders unite - and when the leaders are removed, the unity they inspire erodes as well. You were a General in the Mandalorian Wars, master. I have seen the records of your battles, and I know that your name was one the Mandalorians feared. It is not solely because of your skill in battle, but the fact that you could inspire others, convincing them to fight to the death in situations where other military leaders would be forced to retreat. Revan often speculated on your leadership in this regard. I believe my previous master had formed some other conclusions concerning you. Revan's apprentice wanted me to kill you when you left the war effort and did not go with them to the Unknown Regions, but Revan would not permit it.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Conclusion: I believe the Mandalorian Wars were to beat the Mandalorians and also to allow Revan to build the foundation of his army.» [K2/HK-47] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Handmaiden: «Atris says that you fell to the dark side in the Mandalorian Wars when you gave in to your lust for battle. Once you tasted war, you could not give it up.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Handmaiden: «He left our family to serve in the Mandalorian Wars. But his choice was not because of battle. He went to join my mother, one whose movements and spirit matched his. His only desire was that they fight together, side by side, for as long as there were enemies amassed against them.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «I never saw her face, and she did not return from the final battle of the war. She died in the battle that shattered Malachor V, and her body was never recovered. My father returned from the Mandalorian Wars and did not enter battle again. He entered politics, a caste where one's battles are fought through words rather than action.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Because when my father returned from the Mandalorian Wars, he walked as you do now. There was something wounded inside him. He did not speak of what had happened there. And with us, he was silent. Changed. When I look upon you, I see in you an answer to a question I have searched for all my life. And that is why I tell you this now. I do not believe you to be the monster Atris made you out to be. I believe your choice was my father's choice, and it was just as difficult.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «This is a difficult thing for me to say, but I ask that you be silent as I tell you this. It is my desire to learn from you what you can teach me of battle. I have already learned much in our duels, but with every battle, I wish to know more of you. Your stance, your movements, I can sense shades of meaning, and an echo of something I have yet to experience. Atris said that you were the only Jedi to have survived the Mandalorian Wars. That you had stared into the heart of war, and only turned away because you were forced to. I do not believe her. I believe that you made a choice... as my father did. And that is important to me, more than you know. And you are important to me, more than you know. I will accept whatever you wish to teach me, though it breaks my oath to Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «I knew her mother. She was a Jedi Knight - a master, named Arren Kae. Jedi are forbidden to have children, and when the crime finally came to light almost a decade later, Kae was exiled. She joined the Mandalorian Wars after the shame of her birth was revealed.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Hangar_Bay]**
- Kreia: «You fought in the Mandalorian Wars, and it cost you everything. Are you willing to sacrifice as much again?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Kreia: «Indeed. The Jedi Council counseled caution and patience to... assess the Mandalorian threat as the Outer Rim burned.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Kreia: «Two Jedi Knights, Revan and Malak, defied the Jedi Council. They challenged the Mandalorian fierceness and brutality on the battlefield with a viciousness of their own. Revan's entrance into the conflict marked the true beginning... and end... of the war. It was Revan who drove the Mandalorians back into the Unknown Regions.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Kreia: «Many believed the Mandalorians defeated at Malachor V. But the Mandalorians taught the Jedi much through battle. And so it was that Malak, Revan, and the Jedi that followed them discovered their true natures in the Mandalorian crusade. But you know this.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Atton: «Hnh. I hear the twin blades are harder to master, but they can make enemies stampede over each other running for cover. Lot of Jedi in the Mandalorian Wars used double-bladed sabers - a more aggressive blade, gives you more slaughter per swing. Hey, you didn't go red, did you?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]

**[K2/Hanharr]**
- Hanharr: «I know enough. Enough to smell how weak you are, how broken such an act made you. Did you hear them scream as you butchered the Mandalorian tribes? Did you attempt to cover your ears, kill your heart to shut them out? I have heard of you, Jeedai - heard of your battles. You are a coward who must use planets to kill your foes so you will not see their faces as they burn. At least every one of my people I killed I looked into their eyes as they died, and they knew why they were dying. I know that you did no such thing with your own tribe. They died alone, in pain, and the only one to hear them die was you.» [K2/Hanharr]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- Xaart: «I thought it was you. I served in the Mandalorian Wars with you. You're a Jedi, right?» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Dhagon Ghent: «What the hell happened to you, anyway? Your face looks like the backend of a dewback. Well, regardless, I owe you and Mandalore one.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Dhagon Ghent: «I owe you and Mandalore one.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Dhagon Ghent: «We go way back. Way before he became Mandalore. In my experience Mandalorians always know about the doctors around. For some strange reason they keep getting into scrapes. If you want to know more, ask him yourself.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Dhagon Ghent: «Bakkel's gang pretty much owns this street. From what I hear she's the one that cleaned me out. She's in the local cantina most days. She's tough as drexl leather and more dangerous than an angry Wookiee. If you take Mandalore with you, though, you should be just fine. Just get me the encrypted holodisc. After that, if there is a Jedi Master in the Palace, I'll get you a meeting with him.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Kelborn: «I hear you handled Iziz adequately. Mandalore has left me in charge until he has finished his journey to reuinite the clans. If there is any change planetside I'll send you a message.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Mandalorian Guide: «Reporting as ordered, Mandalore. I can take you wherever you need to go on Dxun.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Bao-Dur: «The last stand of the Republic. The tattered remnants of our fleet, the largest we could gather, but it was damaged, weakened and vulnerable. The Mandalorians couldn't resist. They tore into us like beasts, shredding our ships to scrap as we fought back. Yet this time, there were no reinforcements for either side. Revan had been delayed out-system by Mandalorian scout ships. By the time he arrived, it was too late. And beyond Malachor, there were no more Mandalorians left to die. I remember standing on the bridge with you and watching the destruction of the Republic - watching ships full of soldiers and Jedi burn and die. I remember the look you had when you turned to me. It was the longest you'd ever looked at me. You didn't say anything - just a nod. Events move quickly then, even in my dreams. Flashes, explosions, you - falling. I could feel the pain around me. And then the memory. The drifting hulks of the Mandalorian ships, the dead - allies, friends, strangers. And then the echo. Lingering. The sound I awaken to in my nightmares.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- Mandalorian Sentry: «Looks like there'll be bloodshed before the day is over, after all. For the glory of Mandalore!» [K2/Jungle]
- Davrel: «I was too young to fight in the Mandalorian Wars. With our clans scattered there was no opportunity to prove myself. Years ago young Mandalorians would fly point in a Basilisk, laying waste to anything before them, at the forefront of battle! But this has been denied me. What little honor I have earned you stripped from me in the battle circle. This I won't allow.» [K2/Jungle]
- Kumus: «I... will reward you for your efforts. If I make it back to the Mandalorian camp, I will see to it you are rewarded.» [K2/Jungle]
- Kumus: «[Success] There is truth in what you say. If I make it back to the Mandalorian camp, I will see to it you are rewarded.» [K2/Jungle]
- Kumus: «It is strange that you still helped me, after what you said. I am... grateful for your actions. I'm returning to the Mandalorian camp now.» [K2/Jungle]
- Kumus: «I am grateful for your help, stranger. I am returning to the Mandalorian camp now. I won't forget this.» [K2/Jungle]
- Kelborn: «Mandalore sent me out here personally to track a ship that landed in the area.» [K2/Jungle]
- Kelborn: «Hnh. All right. I'm heading back to the camp to report to Mandalore. You've been helpful - whether you meant to be or not.» [K2/Jungle]
- Kelborn: «General Vaklu is the cousin of Queen Talia. He's also in charge of the Onderon military. He led the Onderon resistance when we occupied their world during the Mandalorian Wars. He was a worthy foe, maybe more than a match for you.» [K2/Jungle]
- Kelborn: «Mandalore needs to know about the scouts. I'll let him know your role in dealing with them.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- Xarga: «My troops will keep the path to the Mandalorian camp clear. Ahead lies the enemy. I can follow you a little bit further, but then I will set up command and control for my unit in the field. So lead on.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Khoonda]**
- Terena Adare: «Mercenaries have been a problem for Dantooine since the end of the Mandalorian Wars. When the Jedi Enclave was here, they were firmly kept in check. But now... Relations are uneasy between us. Their leader, Azkul, was a Sith Trooper during the war, and I have no doubt he was very good at his job. Until they find work, tensions will grow. If you want to know more, I suggest you talk with Berun in the militia headquarters. He would have more details.» [K2/Khoonda]
- Suulru: «Before the Mandalorian Wars we were making great gains in civilizing this world. The kinrath and kath hounds were mainly under control. We were small but prosperous. Then the Republic gets invaded by those flaming Mandalorians. Then a few years after that the Jedi Civil War. We're lacking in manpower and supplies.» [K2/Khoonda]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Kreia: «Yes... of pain, he has learned much. Of knowledge, of teaching, he knows nothing. Like the others, he was spawned by the horrors of the Mandalorian Wars. He exists solely to spread his pain to all Jedi, everywhere.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «Revan had a mother and father, parents, ancestors, like all Jedi do. And when he awakened to his potential, I was there to see it. But where he was born, where he came from, I do not know... anymore than I know where he walks now. Some said that Revan was born in the Outer Regions, beyond the Rim, and that's what called to him during the Mandalorian Wars... and after.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]**
- Kex: «No, just you. You're no Mandalorian. I don't know why Mandalore has let you roam free, but I don't trust you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kex: «No, no problem. I just don't know why Mandalore lets you roam free. I don't trust you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kex: «I'm Kex, the new quartermaster. If a Mandalorian needs something, they come to me. Mandalore's ordered me to let you have access to our supplies, including some military-grade surplus. It's not free, though. You can pay with credits or trade in some of your gear. It doesn't matter to me.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kex: «Yeah, I do. I don't know why Mandalore would let some outsider roam around our camp without an escort. If it were up to me, you would've been shot on sight. Manadlore must think you're something special.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kex: «I won't - as long as the Mandalore's orders stand. If he wants you dead, though, then I'm going to be the first one gunning for you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guide: «About time you showed up. Mandalore ordered me to report here and escort you wherever you need to go on Dxun.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guard Captain: «The people of Onderon still live in fear of us. They were the first world we conquered in the Mandalorian Wars. Their 'resistance' was laughable. We cut through their defenses like vibroblades through cloth. Iziz fell within hours. If they knew we were here, they would eventually gather enough courage to attack us. It is not a battle we wish to wage... yet.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Davrel: «I see now how we lost the Mandalorian Wars. You have a formidable spirit. I have no illusions that I would've been able to defeat the zakkeg without you. I doubt you even needed me. But you have helped me secure my place here. I wish you well, Jedi.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «Every Mandalorian dedicates himself to honor and loyalty. The duty of Mandalore is to focus our efforts to increase our strength and power. I am fortunate since not only can I fight, but I can also maintain and repair the machines of war. Every time my machines are used in battle, part of the honor is mine.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «You're trying to help Mandalore? If you can fix the power problem I'm having, I'll put in a good word for you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «That's the best thing I've heard in weeks. I'll be sure to tell Mandalore about your help. Look, I have some extra parts and computer spikes. Take them - you can probably make better use of them than I can.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «I'll be sure to tell Mandalore how much help you've been. I know you didn't ask, but here's a reward anyway. Mandalorians are expected to do anything they can to help. But you're a stranger, and you didn't have to help us.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «They check out, Mandalore. All systems are green.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kreia: «Indeed? The future is always in motion, it is a difficult thing to see. Perhaps there will be no new age, Mandalore, no great Mandalorian crusade. Perhaps your people fought their last battle at Malachor V, and you have been dying ever since, a quiet death that will last centuries. And perhaps all that remains will be what I see before me: a man, wounded by a Jedi, encased in a Mandalorian shell, haunted by the thought of being the last of the Mandalorians.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kreia: «No, Mandalore, you are wrong. I hope that it is you who will keep the one I travel with safe. You are loyal, and you have served many Masters... even following them into darkness.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kreia: «No, Mandalore, you are wrong. I hope that it is you who will keep the one I travel with safe. You are loyal, and you have served many Masters... even when they abandoned you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kreia: «Do you wonder where she wanders now, Mandalore? Why she cast you down, left you broken, at the edge of the galaxy?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kreia: «Do you wonder where he wanders now, Mandalore? Why he betrayed you at the end, cast you down, left you broken, at the edge of the galaxy?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kreia: «Do you wonder where she wanders now, Mandalore? Why she gave you your orders, then abandoned you at the edge of the galaxy?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Kreia: «I know many things, and I can answer the question that burns within your shell, Mandalore. But there is a price - you must keep the one I travel with safe.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kreia: «Show the same loyalty you have shown in the past, Mandalore. If there is a Mandalorian crusade, let it be for something that will carry your people's memory into the future, so when there are no more Mandalorians, at least their honor will remain. The one I travel with has walked your same path - and I ask that when the end comes, that you remember that kinship, even if it seems there is nothing else left.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Davrel: «For Mandalore!» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- Kreia: «The Sith forces must be stopped. Otherwise the Mandalorian is right - Master Kavar and Queen Talia won't survive this day.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Kreia: «Those transmissions are the enemy. They are linked to the fate of Onderon. They must be stopped. Otherwise the Mandalorian is right - Master Kavar and Queen Talia won't survive this day.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Kelborn: «As you wish. Mandalore has arranged for special transportation to get to Iziz.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- Iziz Citizen: «But that's crazy, I was a member of the resistance. I fought in the Mandalorian Wars.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Twi'lek: «He is a war hero, leader of the resistance during the Mandalorian Wars. A man of vision with experience. He... may not tell all the truth, but he works for the best interest of the people. Many realize this.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Mira]**
- Mira: «There's a lot of lost people out there, scattered ever since the Mandalorian Wars. Sometimes... it's like you can almost hear them, like an echo, calling out for each other. And maybe, just maybe, by finding them, I can start putting the galaxy back together.» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- Atton: «Well, here we are... the smuggler's moon. It's the gaping maw of Nal Hutta, swallowing all the cargo and space port thugs the galaxy has to offer. Mandalorians, mercenaries, war veterans, and pilots from the Mandalorian Wars and the Jedi Civil War ended up on Nar Shaddaa, from all sides of the conflict. When the last war ended, there was no place left for them to go.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Onderon]**
- Atton: «This is where the Mandalorian Wars started? This doesn't look much like a battlefield.» [K2/Onderon]
- Kreia: «This is where the Mandalorian Wars began. She fought here once, and there are things here she must see.» [K2/Onderon] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- Atris: «You presume much. The Force does not guide your movements, not any more. Its connection was lost when you fell. Perhaps you feel that the Force also led you to the Mandalorian Wars, as well. But in truth it only led to your exile.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «The Jedi Order asked only for time to examine the Mandalorian threat. They urged caution, patience. And you defied them. So when you returned, you were brought before us. You were a Jedi no longer. And so you were exiled.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «I have not known you since you left to serve Revan in the Mandalorian Wars. The only question that remains is how far you have fallen.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]**
- Zuka: «Mandalore, we have breached the hull of the Sith vessel and are securing the area. The vessel has suffered extensive structural damage, but its particle fields still maintain a minimal atmosphere within the ship.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Zuka: «No, Mandalore, the proton cores do not emit a signature the Republic ships can detect. If they do pick up the signal, they will assume it to be emanating from the ship's missile bays.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Zuka: «You were right about the vessel, Mandalore. It is of Malachor... it still bears the wounds of Mandalorian guns.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Zuka: «You've reached one of the target sites. Place the proton core, prime it, then give the signal, Mandalore.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Zuka: «Arming signal received, Mandalore. The proton core is active.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Zuka: «We still need to set three more charges. Mandalore, we are holding our position, but the Sith appear to be offering little resistance.... it's like they don't even realize we're here.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Zuka: «Mandalore... we have made contact with the enemy... and one of the cores has detonated prematurely.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Zuka: «No, Mandalore. We... have failed you. I offer you my...» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Zuka: «No, Mandalore. Even with the other two cores set in the ship, it will not be sufficient to destroy the vessel.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Zuka: «That's the last of them, Mandalore. We can destroy the ship on your command.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- Master Zez-Kai Ell: «And that is why the Mandalorian Wars echo within you still.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «We did not cut you off from the Force. You were merely deafened to it, because of that last battle of the Mandalorian Wars.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Kavar: «I thought you had died in the Mandalorian Wars...» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «Do you wish to feel the teachings born of the Mandalorian Wars? Of all wars, of all tragedies that scream across the galaxy? Let me show you - you, who have forever seen the galaxy through the Force. See it through the eyes of the exile.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Refugee: «This sector's filled with refugees from the Jedi Civil War, and even as far back as the Mandalorian Wars. Refugees and war veterans both... and anyone else who was rendered homeless by the war. The Jedi destroyed planets across the galaxy, and here's where the wreckage ended up.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «It's the Ebon Hawk isn't it? She was stolen from me during a routine run in the Mid-Rim, near the close of the Mandalorian Wars.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- Odis: «Because if you need a crew, I'm your man. I flew during the Mandalorian Wars, and again during the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Rescue_T3-M4]**
- HK Protocol Droid: «Surprised Answer: Why, I told them, master. You are the exiled Jedi who served with Revan in the Mandalorian Wars, are you not? I hope all that has happened has not been the result of a miscommunication. If so, then the problem lies with the coreward databases, which are notoriously spotty.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Maintenance Officer: «'The droid told me that his master is the only Jedi he knows of in all the galaxy and that the Jedi had served in the Mandalorian Wars, almost ten years ago.'» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- Master Kavar: «I think we all know that Vrook isn't easily swayed from his convictions, and his views on anyone who went to the Mandalorian Wars are low.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «All those lives during the Mandalorian Wars - and all those you served beside. Too much death leaves echoes in the Force; it is the price for having such connections. I suspect that is why you chose to accept the Council's judgment, to wander beyond the Rim. And why you traveled with no one, and did not stay in any place too long. I have thought of you since your trial, and there are times when I wonder if being connected to the Force is always the gift it is believed to be.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «What do you expect me to say. You defied the Council. You followed Revan to war. I know why you did it, but in so doing, much more harm was done. All those lives during the Mandalorian Wars - and all those you served beside. Too much death leaves echoes in the Force; it is the price for having such connections. Apparently, your self-inflicted wounds would not heal. And now that they've festered, you come looking to the Jedi for vengeance, as if we were somehow responsible for the results of your actions.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris speaks of you, but "Jedi" is not the term she uses. She says you followed Revan to war, the same Revan who took the Mandalorian flame and brought it to the Jedi.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- Republic Captain: «Comm says we've lost another heavy droid transport. How can we break through the Mandalorian lines without support? The path is mined and the place is crawling with enemies. I know we've got our orders to press forward, but we're at quarter strength. We can't, General. It's impossible. We need to retreat.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sky_Ramp]**
- Zuka: «And here it is, a salvaged Basilisk War Droid. Mandalore said that he would make it available to you for insertion into the city of Iziz. I assure you, they won't know what hit them.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- Zuka: «Yes, just like a new Mandalorian recruit proving themselves. Into the heart of a war, as well. Mandalore has given you a great honor.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]

**[K2/Telos]**
- Atris: «It is no crime to kill the Sith or any that threaten the Republic... as you proved in the Mandalorian Wars. This is now my battle, and you are now my enemy.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «You always knew where they were striking from. You always knew. These Sith are spawned of you, spawned by the Mandalorian Wars... all those deaths, all those Jedi. Their power is to feed on life, until nothing is left except a hollow galaxy, echoing with the screams of the Jedi lost to us.» [K2/Telos]

**[K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]**
- Darth Sion: «It is ancient, a relic that survived the destruction of Malachor. It was always here, far before the Mandalorian Wars. It draws death and hate to it, channels it. Atrocities feed its power, and with its power, it creates hunger. Many Jedi have been consumed by it.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]

**[K2/Trayus_Core]**
- Kreia: «Many battles does that one have left in him... as Revan intended. A general needs an army, as he needs those he trusts. And Canderous is a loyal beast, no matter how much he is broken upon Revan's will. But you know this.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Vaklu shall have a short reign, but Onderon's independence shall persist. As he fought the Mandalorians, his triumph over the Republic shall serve to preserve Onderon against the rest of the galaxy, and Onderon shall maintain its customs, its lore, its history. Its identity. And most of all, its victory shall give Onderon strength, so that the horrors of the Sith War and the Mandalorian Wars will not soon come to its surface again.» [K2/Trayus_Core]

**[K2/Visas]**
- Visas: «I have heard tales of Malachor. It is said that many of my people felt the end of the Mandalorian Wars from across the galaxy. But do not mistake me, I did not mean to draw comparisons between Katarr and Malachor... my homeworld still exists. It is... intact.» [K2/Visas]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**

**[K2/Enclave_Courtyard]**
- Dopak: «You've wandered into the wrong camp. You got the look of a warrior, and we got all the blasters we need.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Dopak: «If you're looking for work, too, just take a trip spaceward. We don't need any more competition, got it?» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**

- Colonel Tobin: «You must make a choice - will you support us or the Queen?» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Onderon Soldier: «Listen, if you really were in a fight you should report it to the soldiers. The west square is a dangerous place these days.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Onderon Soldier: «Your visa's been scanned. You're cleared to leave. I'd leave right away. Things are going crazy in this quarter!» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Checkpoint Guard: «The checkpoint up ahead has been closed. Some sort of malfunction with the turrets or something. You can't go through.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Atton: «The Ebon Hawk is patched up and ready to go. When you're ready to depart we can ask the Guide here to take us back to the ship.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Bao-Dur: «You realized that unless action was taken, the fleet would be destroyed, and the Republic would fall. None of us realized the magnitude of what we unleashed.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Bao-Dur: «I could do without your Mandalorian honor. I saw the results of your "honor" - the absolute destruction your warriors brought. And look at them now. Mandalorians are little better than mercenary thugs. And what's honor to someone like that? All they care about are credits.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Bao-Dur: «You did nothing but murder innocents.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Bao-Dur: «That's exactly what I'm talking about. If you ask me, you Mandalorians just got what you deserved at Malachor.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Bao-Dur: «Doesn't it even bother you that your people were almost destroyed? Or do lives have no meaning to you?» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Bao-Dur: «Well, I'm glad to have you guarding my back.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- Handmaiden: «Although using your power in such a way is tempting, I would caution against it... small surrenders lead to greater ones.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- Kelborn: «One last thing about the battle circle... your actions have proven you worthy of facing our best in the circle. Perhaps we can face each other there. See you at camp.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- Atton: «Huh. I could have sworn it was this way. This forest plays tricks on you.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]**
- Kumus: «You told Xarga what happened. You have shamed me in front of my commander and my fellow warriors. If you enter the jungle, may you never return.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «All right, if you say so. Again, you have my gratitude.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kreia: «My only concerns are for the one you escort to Onderon, Mandalorian. Would you do any less for one of your clan?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kreia: «If by 'apart' you mean scattered, broken, and lost, then yes - you are correct.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kreia: «Ah, yes. The great crusade - after the first one was ended by Revan and the Jedi. Such a defeat was merciful, an echo of the end, when your ships were in flames, crushed in the grip of Malachor V. But I do not need to remind you of such things.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- Kreia: «And this is why a common soldier will never triumph against a Jedi. Your military 'tactics' are nothing compared to the Force. It is essential, and inevitable, that we face both enemies at the same time.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- Onderon Soldier: «You would fight with them? This is madness! We need reinforcements!» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**

- Atton: «What are you doing?! That's going to trap us here.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Sky_Ramp]**

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**

## The Handmaiden (Brianna)
*Corpus coverage: 359 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 33 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 11 pronoun references resolved from context · 69 lines addressed to them · 34 narration lines · 41 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Brianna is the daughter of Arren Kae and the last of Atris's Handmaidens [K2/Handmaiden].
### Personality and motivations
Disciplined Echani; curious about the Exile.
### Arc (scene by scene)
- **Telos:** joins as a stowaway (male Exile).
- Sparring.
- Learns the Jedi way and defects from Atris.
- After the Enclave, Kreia's lie leads her to Telos [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave].
- Atris lightning on Telos.
### Relationships
- Atris.
- Exile.
- Sisters.
- Visas.
### Abilities
- Echani martial arts. Potential in the Force through her mother.
### Feats
- Duels with the Exile.
### Power-scaling statements
- On Revan's tactics (first pass): «Only Revan ever demonstrated such skill in war».
### Duels and fights
- Spars with the Exile.
### Verdict implications
- Elite unarmed; low Force.
### Open questions
- None.

### Abilities: game mechanics (FLAGGED: gameplay stats, not lore)
- [K2/Handmaiden] Handmaiden: Class Soldier, Level 6, Alignment 62 (light), Vitality 78, Defense 15. Feats: Echani Strike I, Improved Power Attack, Improved Toughness, Unarmed Specialist II.
- [K2/Party] Handmaiden: Class Soldier, Level 6. Feats: DC=12, Echani Strike I, Improved Power Attack, Improved Toughness, Improved Two-Weapon Fighting, Unarmed Specialist II.
