### Quote bank A: The Handmaiden (Brianna)'s own lines, by scene (page)

**[K2/Crystal_Cave]**
- «This place affects me strangely. There is a tingling in my forehead.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- «I hope you aren't intending to support that monster. They will slaughter the citizens of Khoonda!» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Then I am glad, but I still disapprove of your actions. Using the tools of deception and manipulation will lead to you the dark side.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «I applaud your conviction! A lesser person might have deceived these brutes to avoid battle.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Docks]**
- «His stance tells of such things.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Enclave_Courtyard]**
- «I'd be surprised if the ruins have any holocrons left. They are rare - and others have no doubt been thorough in searching the ruins.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- «What are you doing? This man calls for our aid!» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «This man has done nothing to warrant such retribution.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «I think you underestimate the ingenuity of salvagers. Even the Jedi cannot protect their secrets from those clever or persistent enough. Besides, most of the Jedi who knew the Enclave have perished, and the ones who survived would not steal from this place.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- «I have not trained in the dance, but the Echani fighting arts are very dance-like in their movements. I do not think it would be a stretch for me to do this.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «If you are sure. Really, though, I would be more than happy to do it.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «If you want me to, yes I will do this for you. I would have no problem with it - truly, it is not much more than the fighting I am used to.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «[[../Entertainment Promenade#Someone else» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «"Put this on?"» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «In that case, give it to me and I will prepare myself.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- «This place resembles some of the Republic monitoring stations on Citadel Station - but much more complex.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/HK-47]**
- «That wasn't said to them directly, was it?» [K2/HK-47]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- «Those are Atris' records you have stolen.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I have come to join you. I can help you against this threat.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is just me. And I am doing this because Atris believes you will need help.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I think the strength of the enemy is unknown, but it is greater than five can hope to defeat without aid.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «We cannot go back. The defense screens around the Telos Academy may have allowed your shuttle through once, but they will not let another vessel through again. I possess the codes for deactivating them, but I will not give them to you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «The cargo hold is enough. I assure you, there is little I need. I will attend to myself.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is no matter. I am used to worse conditions. But... thank you for your kindness.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I will. If you need me, I shall be in the cargo hold.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Oh... welcome, exile. Is there something you need?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Yes?» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Yes, your features, your stance - there is a calm about you that I did not notice on Telos.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Yes, your features, your stance - Atris spoke of such Jedi who followed Revan, how their bodies came to mirror the dark side within them.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «There is an energy about you, a lightness in your movements. It is something I have seen in only the most disciplined and revered of the Echani weaponmasters, yet it comes to you with ease.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Because there is a chance of redemption within all of us. And there is no point at which the dark side completely claims one, no matter what their choices.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It shows in your features. It is beautiful to see.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «No, it is what I believe. Atris did not believe such things.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is not pity. It is what I believe. But if you do not believe it, then you are farther gone than I had thought.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You may ask.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «She said you betrayed the Jedi by going to war when it was forbidden to you. You turned on your masters, your teachings, and yourself.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «That is not all she says. She says you know nothing of loyalty to any cause except your own animal instincts, and she told us why you fell to the dark side.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Atris says that you fell to the dark side in the Mandalorian Wars when you gave in to your lust for battle. Once you tasted war, you could not give it up.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Atris says when the dark lord Revan returned to the Republic, you did not march with them because you had fallen so far you could no longer feel the Force.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «So it was a matter of choice, then. If Atris has erred in her evaluation of your motivations, it might be best to inform her.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I believe that is the extent of her expressed feelings toward you. There are variations at times, but all rise from the same foundation.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Yes. It is difficult sometimes for others to truly speak their heart or listen to it. The words often prove difficult, or they do not come at all.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Without having seen you and Atris fight, I cannot say. Battle is a pure form of expression. It is heart and discipline, reduced to movement and motion.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Then her expressed feelings will have to suffice.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Perhaps it may prove truer than conversing with words.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I do not think the battle would go as you describe, but in essence, yes.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «In battle, the words are swept away, giving way to actions - mercy, sacrifice, anger, fear. These are pure moments of expression.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «She speaks of you often, in anger. But her movements, the motion of her hand, her eyes, do not share the anger of her words. There are only the signs of loss. It has been almost the count of ten years, yet the thought of you burns within her still.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Influence: Failure] I do not wish to speak of her to you. Her actions are her own, and to reveal to you if she sees fit.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I believe that your leaving the Jedi Order may have hurt her more than she will ever admit. It is a difficult thing to speak of, to see Atris unable to confront such strong emotion within herself. You... you did not care for her, did you? Atris is beautiful. And wise.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I have heard that Jedi sometimes renounce the code by loving another, and fall from the Order. And there are others who keep such unions secret.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I see. So there are such unions?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Pulling a... Bindo?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Very... well. Are there such unions?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «That is what I have heard. I was not sure if it was something you had seen - or experienced.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I see. I have asked you many questions - I did not mean to. They are questions that have gathered over years, and I did not wish to ask Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Thank you, then. I hope this creates some measure of trust between us.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I honor the face of my mother. It is not something usually spoken of in the company of others.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «There is no need to apologize. You were merely remarking on something that you saw - there is no wrong in that.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It is not a sensitive subject, but a subject that requires trust. There is no such trust between you and I, and such trust takes time.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I do not wish to discuss it. If there is something else you wished to ask, you may do so.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Yes, that is correct. I feel that I may trust you with such things, so I shall speak of it, if you wish to hear it.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Though my father's blood I share with my sisters, I wear the face of my mother. My father was Yusanis, an Echani general.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «He left our family to serve in the Mandalorian Wars. But his choice was not because of battle. He went to join my mother, one whose movements and spirit matched his. His only desire was that they fight together, side by side, for as long as there were enemies amassed against them.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I never saw her face, and she did not return from the final battle of the war. She died in the battle that shattered Malachor V, and her body was never recovered. My father returned from the Mandalorian Wars and did not enter battle again. He entered politics, a caste where one's battles are fought through words rather than action.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «He was slain by Revan in the Jedi Civil War when Revan sought to destabilize the Echani worlds. Revan succeeded. The fact that our father chose battle is not shameful, but that is not the reason he went to war. He went to war to be with the one he loved, but not the one he had pledged himself to. He was disloyal. I am the mark of that disloyalty. It is said that such things run in the blood, and I have fought long to prove that this is not so. That is why I am different from my sisters. Yet I am pledged to them and Atris, and I would die before betraying them. I tell you this in trust, and ask that you not speak of it to others. I only wish you to know.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Because when my father returned from the Mandalorian Wars, he walked as you do now. There was something wounded inside him. He did not speak of what had happened there. And with us, he was silent. Changed. When I look upon you, I see in you an answer to a question I have searched for all my life. And that is why I tell you this now. I do not believe you to be the monster Atris made you out to be. I believe your choice was my father's choice, and it was just as difficult.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You have earned my trust. I wished to honor the trust you have shown me, and I wished to explain what you meant to me.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Perhaps not. But I do not think the reasons are so far removed. And you yourself may not realize the truth of what you represent.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Malachor V is the place where I lost my mother and my father. But it was their choice to fight the Mandalorians - and to die there, if Malachor was to be their grave.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I do not claim to understand you. There are times when your actions are a mystery to me - but I do not wish my actions to be a mystery to you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I know it is difficult for others to see why I am here, but it was important that you know one of the reasons, and know that it is not simply duty that I am here, but because I want to be here. I want to fight with you side by side, for as long as there are enemies that threaten you. You are a leader - your stance, your every action proves it.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You honor me, exile. Together, then.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «We attend Atris. It is the duty of all of us, from the first of my sisters to me, the last of the Handmaidens.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I am the last of the Handmaidens, this is correct. I train so that one day that will no longer be true.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It dishonors me that they would say such a thing to an outsider. But I cannot deny the truth in what they say. My thoughts are not always focused on training.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «That is not entirely correct. There are times I am... distracted.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Perhaps once having known the ways of the Jedi, you may understand what occupies my thoughts.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «There is much knowledge on Telos, and only one of the Jedi remain. There is so much about their ways of battle, their forms, their stances, that may be lost forever if the last of the Jedi is taken from the galaxy.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Atris has already preserved it. What I wonder is if the Jedi teachings should not simply be stored, but brought to others. There is much in their approach to battle that is worthy to be shared.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I know your meaning, but I have not been clear on mine.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «To the Echani, battle is a means of communication - it is an art, in the truest sense of the word.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Stance, form, discipline are a means of expression and communication. They speak one's heart and one's devotion to their cause.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Yes, the methods you use to meet your opponents speak truer than any words can express. When you risk pain or death, there is no truer sacrifice or strength.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «If that is where one's devotion lies. When you risk pain or death for another, there is no truer test of your beliefs and strength.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It was to the Jedi traitor Malak. It was to the Jedi traitor Revan. When Taris was destroyed, it showed Malak's heart through its execution and intent. It was brutal, without finesse... but showed his commitment to defeat the Jedi. Yet with Revan, there was the same commitment, but it was a subtle thing, like weaving threads in a tapestry, or strokes upon a canvas.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «She spoke through battles and tactics, in a way one could never do in words. She showed her heart at Malachor V... and finally, at the end of the Jedi Civil War. I believe she was speaking to Malak in that final battle, though few knew it.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «He spoke through battle and tactics, in a way one could never do in words. He showed his heart at Malachor V... and finally, at the end of the Jedi Civil War. I believe he was speaking to Malak in that final battle, though few knew it.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Through battle, Revan was meeting betrayal with betrayal - and showing Malak the pain he had inflicted on his master.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Poetry is wordplay that cloaks feelings in riddles. Battle speaks, and its feeling is true. Revan knew this when facing Malak... and slaying him.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «What stronger display than death for conveying one's sense of being betrayed by one's own student? Revan's anger must have been great indeed. I would have wished to have been there for that final exchange and seen the truth of their conflict with each other.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «But to say that seems an untruth, based on what I know of the Jedi. The Force can drive others, but there is still choice, is there not? If there is no choice in the Force, then our teachings and our actions are for nothing. And I refuse to believe that is true.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Before entering Atris' service, yes, I carried a name, as all the children of the Echani do.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is not important. My title and rank is of consequence, not my name. I take value in Atris' service, not in myself.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «We all have value in our oaths to others, and the promises we make. When we make that pledge, we are pledging ourselves to something greater. When importance is placed on the self, then by such acts the galaxy is unmade.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «If reasons of the self is why you turned away, then yes, perhaps there was a judgment there, but it was not intended as an attack.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Nothing except that which you have interpreted.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I do not know. That is a question you must ask yourself.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I meant no offense, but the question remains. If the question angers you, oftentimes there is a reason for such anger.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is not possible to dock at the Academy without the proper codes.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Yes, but I could not give them to you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I am training. So that if danger should strike, my body and my reflexes will be prepared. That, and I had forgotten how long hyperspace travel can be. If I do not have something to focus my attention on, I fear my sanity will erode as well.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «No, I do not trust him.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «That, too.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «That is untrue. You and the Iridonian trust each other... or at least, the Iridonian trusts you. We heard much of the Iridonian when we served Atris. Atris believed that the Iridonian held the knowledge to restoring Telos.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «The reasons for such siphoning of power are complicated, and I do not know all the answers. But there is something greater being achieved. The teachings at the academy must be preserved, even if it draws strength from Telos.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Yes. His skill with machines is something beyond which most can aspire to. His shield technology surpasses the designs of even the most skilled of Echani power architects.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I will respect your wishes. And his. Forgive me.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Very well. I did not mean to bring up painful memories, forgive me.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I see. Forgive my words. They were poorly chosen.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It does not sound like it is in the past. But I shall respect your wishes.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «The Mandalorian Wars were like that for many. Many returned who could not tell of their experiences in a way that could bring meaning to them.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Very well. But if you choose to speak of it, I am here to listen to whatever you wish to say.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Training is something reserved for certain caste members of the Echani... but I do not see the harm in instructing you in some basic principles. I do not understand how you and Atris fight, but I will instruct you on how Echani children are raised on warfare.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «All Echani fighting principles rely on foundations. If one does not understand the most basic of fighting moves, it is not possible to understand the higher tiers. It is similar to learning the alphabet of a language before being able to use words, then sentences.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You are correct. Words are merely words, and they fail to communicate what I intend to teach you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «As a foundation, I will instruct you in our elementary movements. The body itself is the first weapon you must master. It is not something that can be described - let us duel, you and I, and that shall teach you more than my words can. Use only your hands and feet to strike at me - nothing else, or our combat shall be over. Do not resort to using any items, or any Force techniques you may possess - such things will obstruct learning.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Duels among the Echani are rituals, and do not allow for armor or anything that restricts movement.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[[../Handmaiden#Handmaiden's Robe» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Your modesty has no place in combat. Even the youngest of the Echani understand this - you should as well.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Now - are you ready?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Very well. I shall be here.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Very well. I shall match my movements to resist your efforts. And do not hold back, or I will hurt you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You fought well, but you announce your motions before attacking, sometimes quite loudly. Take as long as you need to recover, and if you wish, we shall fight again.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Of course. I do not wish to be hurt. If you wish to hurt me, then you must try harder. It is not a contest of raw strength, but of action and reaction.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Very well. Let us duel again.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «You know the first tier of styles, but you hold back because I am unarmed. Tell me where you were trained.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You lie. Few know the Echani styles by reflex.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Fool.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It does not seem to bother you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I fail to understand the problem. I had heard the coreward systems had customs concerning... modesty, but when training, such customs are not practical or efficient.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I can see no fault in your reasoning. I do have bulkier clothes. Will this do?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «They suffice for training purposes. They belonged to my mother.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «They are. These robes belonged to my mother.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Influence: Failure] That is not something I wish to discuss. Is there something else you needed?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is a matter personal to me, and I do not feel comfortable discussing it with others. I have donned the robes to test my skills, that is all.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Influence: Success] She was the one my father followed to war - she was a Jedi.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «These are her robes. I have not worn them since they came into my possession. They are the only thing of her that I possess.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I think you misunderstand - I have no feelings concerning her at all. There is only curiosity.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I never knew her. There is no absence of presence when there was nothing there to begin with. I miss only that I never knew her at all. And what it was about her that caused my father to follow her to war.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You have violated the etiquette of our duel. We will leave the instruction for another time when you are willing to listen.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I have heard enough - your stance was unable to be read, because you chose to defy our rules.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «That is not something I believe. Without ritual and oath, without etiquette, there is no truth in battle or combat.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Such lack of discipline is a weakness. It is another form of not knowing yourself, of not knowing your limits and strengths. If we defy such conventions, then no Echani would ever know another.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You will take back that challenge, exile. If you do not, then I shall teach you not to make such accusations lightly.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «There is truth in what you say. But the manner in which it was delivered is not welcome.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Success] There is truth in what you say. But the manner in which it was delivered is not welcome.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I have heard what you have said, through pain and word, and I understand. You have taught me something, about myself and yourself, and we are both greater through the experience.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «If there is nothing more, then I wish to be left alone to consider this.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I understand what you meant by the lesson. Please leave me.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Success] You fought well indeed, you have caught the principles of the style by watching and anticipating my movements.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I now have a favor to ask you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «The weapon the Jedi use, the Force... I would like to know more about it.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is not something you can see, or fight, yet the Jedi use it... and struggle with it their entire lives.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Please, I wish to know.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Then tell me of its absence.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I see. Thank you, exile. I appreciate you sharing your knowledge with me.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «As it was mine to hear your words. I thank you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I will do this. Thank you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Very well - forgive me, exile, I did not mean to speak of things painful to you.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I do not think you are ready for further instruction. Each movement of our arts demands some time to use the art in battle, or to examine how it may be best used in battle.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is the way of the Echani to be able to read their opponents - to know where an opponent is going to strike before it connects, anticipate it, and then strike against them. Echani battles are fought several minutes in advance - in many ways, it is much like the game of dejarik played in the core systems. The most advanced among the Echani are able to predict the course of battles by months, and the most revered are said to be able to predict the path of wars.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Only Revan ever demonstrated such skill in war. And even as she slaughtered us, the Echani still respected her.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «You are already doing it.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «If you do not know you are doing it, then training will make you a dangerous opponent indeed. Come - as we fight more, I will teach you. Do not think about predicting my movements - react instinctively. You are doing better. At first, I was afraid that your awareness of your own ability might ruin it, but that is not the case. You learn quickly. Perhaps it is your connection to the Force that allows such things, but I do not think so. Now you fight as an Echani warrior fights - always in the future.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Very well. I think you are ready for the next tier of instruction. For this, we will keep the etiquette of the last duel, but we shall add any melee weapon you choose to the ritual, with the exception of the Jedi weapon, the saber. Vibroblade, Bothan stun stick, force pike - whatever you wish. But no items, no Force techniques.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Then let us begin - and again, do not hold back. I will not.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Before you go, there is something I must know. Why did you go back, face trial?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I see. It was always something I was curious about - to walk to one's own sentence willingly. It is a brave thing.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I do not understand it myself. It was always something I was curious about - to walk to one's own sentence willingly. It is a brave thing.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I... see. But I feel I understand what you felt. To go to war... and then return to be judged by those who had not.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I think you are ready for the final tier I wish to teach. You have made much progress. For this, you may use any melee weapon and any shields you possess. But nothing else, no Jedi saber and no other items.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «We shall begin this final lesson, then.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Atris was correct about you. You know war, its motions and currents.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «There is nothing more I can teach you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I have already learned much of your styles and combat. There is no need to know more.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Combat among the Echani is... a personal thing. Repeated duels are not what they are in other cultures, and I would rather that this not become more than it is. And I have taken an oath to Atris against studying from a Jedi, or anything of the Jedi teachings.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «What I desire is of no importance.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Influence: Failure] Other Echani have broken oaths in the past, and I will not. This is not something I wish to discuss.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Influence: Success] My father broke his oaths. He shamed us all. I do not wish to follow his path... I swore not to follow his path. If I were to follow a Jedi against Atris' wishes, then I would be betraying her. For you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «This is a difficult thing for me to say, but I ask that you be silent as I tell you this. It is my desire to learn from you what you can teach me of battle. I have already learned much in our duels, but with every battle, I wish to know more of you. Your stance, your movements, I can sense shades of meaning, and an echo of something I have yet to experience. Atris said that you were the only Jedi to have survived the Mandalorian Wars. That you had stared into the heart of war, and only turned away because you were forced to. I do not believe her. I believe that you made a choice... as my father did. And that is important to me, more than you know. And you are important to me, more than you know. I will accept whatever you wish to teach me, though it breaks my oath to Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I shall await that time. But until then, I will need time to reflect on this - it is different to voice something you feel, and I must grow accustomed to the weight.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[[../Handmaiden#Ends» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «What of her? As I said, I knew little about her.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Influence: Failure] This is not something I wish to discuss. If there is something else you wish to ask, you may do so.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Influence: Success] This is already known to me. Why are you telling me this?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Influence: Failure] Even if I believed you, to even discuss this is a betrayal of my oath to Atris. We have spoken long enough - now I wish to be left alone.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Failure] I... I do not know enough of you yet to speak of such things. I do not know your stance, your heart. Perhaps if we were to train together in battle, my thoughts on this would be clearer. But not now.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[Influence: Success] I... think I have always known this. It has always been on my thoughts.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «As I followed Atris, perhaps what I wished to follow was the call of my bloodline, of my mother. I have felt incomplete, hollow, since her loss at Malachor V... and with your help, perhaps this wound might be healed.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I want you to teach me to wield the Force as my mother did, to fight those that would threaten us - and end them.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I ask you to reconsider - I will not fail you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Very well. But know that I am ready and certain, more than I have been in my entire life. This is my purpose, what I was meant to do.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Very well. But I will be ready if you change your mind.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I did not ask you to train me in the ways of the Jedi. I have seen that path - in Atris, and that is not the path I wish.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I wish to follow you, not Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is you I wish to serve. Teach me. I shall not fail you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I want you to teach me the ways of the Force. To become a Jedi Knight like my mother.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Listen to me. I am already committed.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «There is no one else I would want to train me. I have seen you in battle, I have seen your heart, and you are what I want to be. It is like a hollow place inside me, but when I am with you, the echo dies.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Then that is your choice. But I had to ask before the moment escaped me forever.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «[[../Handmaiden#First steps» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «There is the betrayal of Atris, and there is the betrayal of my mother and father. After seeing you in battle, I know you more than I have ever known Atris. And I am not convinced that serving her is the greater good.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «That is all I wish.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I want to feel what my mother felt for my father, what ran through my mother's veins when she was one with the Force. I wish to hear what my mother heard as she fought the Mandalorians... until the moment she died on Malachor V.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I will not let you down, exile. I will honor you, as I honor the face of my mother.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I won't let you harm him. I have heard of your kind... you worship strength, when all it does is rot you from within.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I will not reveal my mistress... and I will not allow you to harm the one I travel with.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Protect him? I do not believe you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Be silent! You know nothing of what you speak.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Like a man. But it is more than that, and I do not have the words.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «He is selfish, at times lacking discipline.... my mistress was right about him, the galaxy has poisoned his heart. But... I have hope that he can be redeemed. It is a faint hope... And I find that I cannot help but follow him.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «His path is difficult to detect, sometimes he chooses the shadowed path - but at other times, he demonstrates a strange kindness.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «He shows kindnesses, both small and great, to strangers. He does this without hesitation. He has accepted me, even though he and my mistress are not allies.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «All I know is he makes me doubt the words of my mistress, one whom I have pledged to serve.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «And I fear such doubts. Could it be my mistress was wrong about him? That perhaps he did not seek to betray the Order.  That perhaps my mistress felt as I did, and when he left the Order, she found that she doubted as well.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «There is a feeling that emanates from him, like... like a sound, from far away, that has traveled a vast distance to reach here.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «That sounds more like hunting.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Leave me be.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «There is still hope.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I do not believe you. And I do not trust you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Why are you here?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «If you have come to ask me questions, try to find out more of Atris or the academy, then I have nothing to say to you. I see where your loyalty lies. And if you stand with her, then you cannot stand with me. She is of the Sith. She has faced battle, and been found wanting.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Why is it you trust her and not I? I would follow you wherever you led. I believe in what you seek. Yet you listen to her counsel - she who would lead you to the Sith Lords themselves, she who would sacrifice you to darkness.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Is that something that she told you? If that is what you believe, then what I say does not matter.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Stay with her, then.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I do not believe you. Every action you have taken with her runs counter to your words.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You answer her deception with deception of your own?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «This is the end between us.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I will aid you against the enemies we face, but when it is settled, you shall not see me again.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «There is nothing for us to discuss. Leave me alone.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Personal shields, I love you so... crafting you and fighting with you... exhilarates me.'» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «"You are the MAIN focus of my life... (except for my melee weapons and my armor)... I love you so."» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Please do not joke about such things. You speak lightly of serious matters to me.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Influence]**
- «That was generous of you. I do not know if Atris would have been so kind - she has taught that you do not help others by giving them what they can achieve themselves.» [K2/Influence]
- «I will be mindful of what you have said - it is not a lesson we were exposed to on Telos.» [K2/Influence]
- «Atris may seem harsh, but you dishonor her with such comments.» [K2/Influence]
- «Perhaps the stories Atris told of you were untrue.» [K2/Influence]
- «After all this time, you still follow the Jedi Code.» [K2/Influence]
- «I had not expected such an act from an exile.» [K2/Influence]
- «Your acts are inspiring - there are ways to do good beyond battle and victories.» [K2/Influence]
- «The stance of our enemy was unthreatening, more surprised than anything, and then we... then I...» [K2/Influence]
- «Their stance was unthreatening - that one meant us no harm. I do not understand why we attacked. I have fought in self-defense, in battle, but never like this.» [K2/Influence]
- «I do not know. As soon as your stance changed, so did mine... it was a strange thing.» [K2/Influence]
- «Echani are taught to be prepared for any battle, at any time. We are trained to predict each battle several movements in advance - and any great battle several hours in advance.» [K2/Influence]
- «The true Echani, the greatest of our teachers, are said to be able to predict the path of wars, of battles, years in advance.» [K2/Influence]
- «Yet this attack I was unprepared for. I did not anticipate it, yet as you acted, I acted as well. I am not used to such things.» [K2/Influence]
- «I do not understand why we attacked.» [K2/Influence]
- «I do not understand why we executed that one.» [K2/Influence]
- «Such acts do much to condemn the Jedi name.» [K2/Influence]
- «That was unnecessarily cruel.» [K2/Influence]
- «I see why the Jedi exiled you.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- «I've heard of those. Old stories from an earlier age. They come from a dead world, the crystals caught the last rays of a dying sun. Rare, indeed, and powerful.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «His mission sounds important to the Republic. You acted admirably by aiding him in his endeavor.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «That was a noble thing you did. I hope she and her family prosper elsewhere.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «We must prevent the civilians here from coming to harm by our battle.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- «Although using your power in such a way is tempting, I would caution against it... small surrenders lead to greater ones.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- «Keep calm. Their weapons are not raised, I do not think they wish to fight us.» [K2/Jungle]
- «Even though Mandalorians were your sworn enemy, you would still save one in need? You are a perplexing man, Exile.» [K2/Jungle]
- «I will have to think on what you've said.» [K2/Jungle]
- «The jungle is a living thing. That detonation will have repercussions.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- «No, we can't turn our backs on the mission.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]
- «Some sort of... sensor is up ahead. Beyond the mines.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- «The halls of the Enclave are meant for Jedi. The artifacts left behind could prove dangerous in the wrong hands. We should investigate.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- «Atris said that the Enclave on Dantooine was one of the great storehouses of Jedi knowledge - many Jedi came here to study the ancient holocrons and relics.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- «Are you finished scaring this poor man? You should conduct yourself in a manner more appropriate for a Jedi.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- «...from the first movement flows the second... strike, repeat, circle one's opponent... repeat, faster, quicker... if Father had been faster... if only Father had been faster...» [K2/Kreia]
- «...if I was faster, I would no longer be the last of my sisters... but does Atris love him? Jedi do not love. Does Atris hate him? Jedi do not hate. This journey is harder than any she has sent me on.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- «What are you talking about? It's obviously a trap.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «What are you talking about? What is wrong?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «If they are coming after us, then they will be after him as well. We need to go rescue him.» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- «I will do what you ask of me.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- «Your actions have averted disaster here. That was an admirable use of the Jedi teachings.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Let's just hope we can contain this before it gets out of hand.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- «It is not your choice, Atton, it is his. What is it you wish to do?» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «This bounty poses a threat to him. We do not need two beasts at our back when the Sith are enough.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «I do not wish your support, Dark Jedi - your words only undermine my arguments.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «You will not come near the Jedi - I will not allow it.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «We do not need your assistance, Dark Jedi.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- «Lay down your weapons and you shall not be harmed.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «I will not warn you again. Drop your weapons, or we shall take them from you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «We shall remove her, mistress.» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Come with us.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Are you all right, mistress?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Forgive me, mistress... but I must ask. The exile... I have never seen another effect you so strongly.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Was she important to you, once?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Did you care for him once?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Of course, mistress. I will tell the others you are not to be disturbed. And... please, do not exhaust yourself. We can attend to matters here.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- «Do not let them hurt him...» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- «Are you sure you wish to do this? If so, we will be forced to remain on this moon until we can hire transport out.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Atton, restrain yourself. Besides, a blaster would be unnecessary when a nerve strike to his neck would incapacitate him painfully without inflicting any lasting harm.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- «That was noble of you to help that sickly man. You put your own life at risk to save others.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «I admire your bravery.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «I was speaking the truth, but in the future, I will keep such comments to myself.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «Oh.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- «Welcome back from Onderon. How did you fare?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «That is good news. The universe tends toward unity and balance, and you served as its catalyst in Onderon.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Such vanity does not become a Jedi. The Force works its will through you, as its catalyst for change.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «I respect the need to keep some secrets, but your refusal to speak of Onderon will only foment mistrust among your companions.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- «You are the exile. The one Atris warned us about.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «It dishonors me they would say such a thing to an outsider. But I cannot deny the truth in what they say. My thoughts are not always focused on training. Perhaps once having known the ways of the Jedi, you may understand what occupies my thoughts.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «There is much knowledge here, and only one of the Jedi remain. There is so much about their ways of battle, their forms, their stances, that may be lost forever if the last of the Jedi is taken from the galaxy.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Even if I possessed the senses that the Jedi were said to have, such teachings would be forbidden. Atris has set us upon the road we are to travel until the end of our days. We are to serve the Jedi by watching them, not learning their ways.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Yes, watch them for signs of the corruption that took place during the Jedi Civil War. Such vigilance requires those not tied to the Force or its influence.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «[Failure] I cannot disobey Atris. My loyalty is to her, and to her word.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I cannot disobey Atris. My loyalty is to her, and to her word.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «You must understand. If we do not maintain our vigilance, then we risk a second tragedy, one greater than that of the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «[Success] That is something I had not considered. You have given me something to reflect on, exile - I thank you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «What stronger display than death for conveying one's sense of being betrayed by one's own student? Revan's anger must have been great indeed.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I would have wished to have been there for that final exchange and seen the truth of their conflict with each other.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Regardless, I would have liked to have been there for that final exchange and seen the truth of their conflict with each other... as perhaps you would have.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «But to say that seems an untruth, based on what I know of the Jedi. The Force can drive others, but there is still choice, is there not?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «If there is no choice in the Force, then our teachings and our actions are for nothing. And I refuse to believe that is true.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I know a little of them, yes. Much Jedi knowledge is stored here, secrets of their teachings, combat styles, and discipline.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «The Jedi relics are kept by Atris, within the walls of her meditation chamber. Entry is forbidden.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «[Failure] I will not. Atris has made her orders on the matter clear, and she says you are no Jedi.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «[Failure] Atris has made her orders on the matter clear, and she says you are no Jedi.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Entry to her meditation chamber is forbidden. To even ask shows you doubt my loyalty to her.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I have seen a few of them, yes. They were not always in Atris' meditation chamber.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Most come from Dantooine, brought here before the academy there was destroyed by Malak. Not all relics were able to be saved, for there was not time to rescue them all.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I am not permitted to speak of such things, and it is not your concern.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I... I have seen a few of them, yes. They were not always in Atris' meditation chamber.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «There were many relics... among them small cubes, warm to the touch, each containing their own light.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Yes, and with a... light about them.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «When held, some would speak, ask strange questions.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Yes, their questions were... confusing. They seemed to think I was someone else, someone's student.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I had thought perhaps I had damaged them in some way, but I could not think of what I had done.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I honor the face of my mother. It is not something spoken of in the company of others.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I am aware of this. I do not doubt your combat prowess, as my half-sisters do. There are many who went to war against the Mandalorians, and few returned. You were one of them.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I did not mean to praise you with my words. Perhaps it is your interpretation of them that needs to be viewed in perspective.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I do not fear you. My words have been misinterpreted and need to be placed in perspective.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I will, exile. In truth, I do wish to challenge you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Again, you have twisted my words to attempt to provoke me. That shows a creative mind, which bodes well for any fight between us.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «No, Atris has no bearing on this request. In fact, I think she would disapprove, quite strongly, if I were to ask.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «The Echani believe you cannot truly know the heart of another unless you meet them in battle. I would very much like to know your nature. But now is not the time - perhaps my sisters could challenge you, but not I, and not here.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Before you go, exile... question for you, if I may ask it. You have touched the Force... what does it feel like?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I see. Thank you, both. I appreciate you sharing your knowledge with me.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- «Apathy is death.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- «A lightsaber was used to fuse this door shut.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «Another great Jedi lost.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Sith_Tomb]**
- «I can sense the power from this place. It's like the light abandoned it long ago. I fear the Sith shall be very strong here.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- «This... area is strong with the dark side. I've never felt anything like it. If the mission weren't so urgent, I'd recommend we avoid this area completely. The Sith have come here because of this. It's dangerous.» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Telos]**
- «What are you saying?» [K2/Telos]
- «Listen to me - Atris has been touched by the Sith, it is not too late for-» [K2/Telos]
- «Mistress, as you commanded, I-» [K2/Telos]
- «Mistress, I do not understand. I-» [K2/Telos]
- «You came for me. I thought I had lost you.» [K2/Telos]
- «Kreia... she said the council had ended you. And all along, she was one of those who had sought to kill us.» [K2/Telos]
- «When I heard her say that you were dead, I... I failed you. I let my emotion run through me, and I acted without thinking. I wanted to punish her... hurt her, see her answer for what she had done to the Jedi, for leading you to the council...» [K2/Telos]
- «Of course. Forgive my display. I... I am the last of the Handmaidens no longer.» [K2/Telos]
- «I am Brianna, your disciple. And the one who will stand with you against all enemies who face us.» [K2/Telos]
- «I am Brianna, disciple of the last of the Jedi. And the one who will stand with you against all enemies who face us.» [K2/Telos]
- «It is... good to hear it, after so long.» [K2/Telos]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «That cave... it almost has a presence about it. Evil, almost hungry.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «It seems unlikely the Jedi Master we are looking for is within - I see no tracks, no disturbance of recent travel.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K2/Visas]**
- «She is a threat to us.» [K2/Visas]
- «I am not asking that she be harmed or interrogated, but she is of the Sith. And she has attacked us once.» [K2/Visas]
- «*I* have not attacked you - *she* has.» [K2/Visas]
- «She should not be allowed to walk freely on the ship.» [K2/Visas]

### Quote bank B: what others say about The Handmaiden (Brianna) (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- Twi'lek Domo: «[[../Handmaiden» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Atton: «Oh, yeah, one other thing - don't think I haven't noticed the way you've been sparring with our ex-Jedi friend. This isn't some pleasure yacht, so if I did know all the Echani movements, I might know more than just the first tier, including the etiquette rituals. So keep your hands where I can see them.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «You are spending time with the servant of Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «Revan welcomed her. And she was... said to be... a skilled warrior. Beautiful. And strong in the Force. The Force flows strongly in the blood of those born from Force Sensitives. I doubt that Arren was any different. If the servant of Atris is of her blood, then the potential lies within her. If you train her, if you teach her the ways of the Jedi, you will be asking her to break her oath to Atris. It would be best not to train her, and let the bloodline die with Telos.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «But we were speaking of the servant of Atris - I would caution you to be careful of your interaction with her. She is not as... tempered as you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «Before you continue questioning me, I hope your thoughts in the matter concerning this servant of Atris are clear.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «Never have you wondered what it would mean in the Echani rituals if the two of you sparred and fought - and you won, completely and utterly? If perhaps she would give in, surrender herself to you? Few are the thoughts that can hide in the shadows of your mind, exile... and such passions are not strength, but erosion.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Visas: «You do not believe that I would follow him to death? Certainly you have read as much in my movements... as I have in yours. There is no shame in your feelings, sister of the Echani.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Visas: «You are alone and wounded in your own way. I can feel its pulse, like a heartbeat from the past. But if you will not believe that I mean him no harm, then believe this - I wish to learn from him.  I wish to learn how it is he still walks when his spirit is nothing more than a shell... when you feel as though the Force, as if life itself, has abandoned you.  I had a question for you, sister of the Echani. The exile... what does he look like?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Visas: «Like an echo. Your words are well-chosen, sister of the Echani. I will not harm him. But it is not me you should fear. There are darker things in the galaxy than a blind one such as I.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «Something troubles you, servant of Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- Kreia: «All it requires is that you immerse yourself in another lie, beast. The exile - you shall be her servant, until I call upon you.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Kreia: «Listen to my words. Hear the sound of the Handmaiden's training in the cargo hold, her hands cutting the air.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «Like the servant of Atris, this one... has other masters. Though blind, she has ties to darkness.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Mira]**
- Mira: «Heh... for a minute there, I thought you and the Handmaiden... but it's just the Force.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Look, if we start sharing a bunk, the Handmaiden would get upset, and then I'd have to kick the hell out of her just to show her who the pack leader is around here. No thanks.» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- Kreia: «If you are so certain of your path then do what you will, servant of Atris.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- Atton: «The Handmaiden took her. She thinks Kreia killed you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Atton: «The only thing that matters is the Handmaiden believes it. And she's going to react exactly how that old witch hoped she would. That's why she wanted the Handmaiden on board, you know. So she could use her to reach the Telos Academy whenever she wished - without needing the access codes.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Atton: «Yes. The Handmaiden knows who she is, now.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Visas: «I want to look upon you, where no one else can see, where it is just us. I want to see your face, the color of your skin. I wish to see what the Handmaiden sees when she looks at you that causes her heart to race, and her tone to change. What causes her to forsake her heritage, her oaths... as you made me forsake mine. I... need to know if perhaps I am wrong, if the universe is not as my Master once showed me.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Kreia: «They are Echani. It is not unusual for their children to share similar features from the same parents.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «He has had some Echani training. He masks it well, but when you were in danger, his mask dropped into a stance we know well.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «I do not know. The Echani forms are known to be taught to military special forces throughout the galaxy. If the source is a mystery to you, perhaps you should ask him. It would be wise to know those you travel with.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «We share the same father, and we all bear the face of our mother. It is not unusual for the Echani of the same parents to be born so as to be indistinguishable to outsiders.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «As I have said, it is not unusual for the Echani of the same parents to be born so as to be indistinguishable to outsiders.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «To claim to know anything of Revan's choices or what lay in his heart when Malak fell is conceit, servant of Atris. And whether Revan had any choice in the matter at all is something else you should consider. The Force is a powerful thing to wield... or deny.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «Ah, but at what point does the power the Force exerts submerge any attempt at choice, or free will? You have taken a complicated question, servant of Atris, and you have trivialized it with your answer... and lack of experience.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Before we begin... are you familiar with Echani traditions?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Our next battle will be using the higher forms among the Echani. You may choose hands and feet - or whatever melee weapon you wish, but use no other items or Jedi techniques.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «I admit nothing. The Echani stand together, to defeat one or two of us says nothing of how a true battle would go.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «Echani training?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Yes, mistress: the Iridonian, the Echani-trained pilot, the exile, and the old woman.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to The Handmaiden (Brianna) (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K2/Crystal_Cave]**
- Kreia: «:: Your thoughts are disturbed. I can feel them, like a shiver running through you. ::» [K2/Crystal_Cave]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- Mira: «That man is an ice cold killer. I wouldn't trust him if I were you.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «Are you ready? I won't wait long for you.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- HK-47: «Statement: I approve of your style, Master.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Docks]**
- Kreia: «Then your allies are your weakness, and if they die, you die with them. And where once the Sith had but one target, now there are many, and you frustrate my attempts to protect you.» [K2/Docks]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- Disciple: «That man needs our help! Why are you ignoring him?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «I don't know what you seek to gain by killing him. You act as if you are touched by the dark side!» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Kreia: «This is not wise. Indiscriminate violence will gain you little.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- Twi'lek Domo: «I would have to see you attempt a dance to be sure, but otherwise, I believe Vogga would be pleased with you.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Excellent! I am glad we have reached an agreement. Now, if you'll just put this on and dance for me - just a bit.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Very good. Here you are, and let's see how you look.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Atton: «What the hell are you doing on our ship?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Atton: «Well, we don't want your help. Or any of your sisters.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Atton: «Don't you ever give it a rest?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Atton: «Oh, yeah? Guess you saw a lot of the world while you were freezing your cargo hold off in the ice academy, didn't you, sister?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Visas: «That is how flesh and blood fights. Let me show you how one fights when one's life is the Force.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Visas: «You echo another's words, and as an echo, it lacks strength. Who is it who has taught you of the Sith?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Mira: «But it's obvious you two need some advice, so let me lay out the attack pattern for you. It's simple - when you want a man, you jab him with a Bothan Stunner, then while he's screaming in pain, slap some stun cuffs on him. Then starve him for two or three days until he becomes open to suggestion, then double-check his bounty and see if he's worth anything.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Mira: «Call it what you want. Me, I love my targets.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «I have seen what you have seen - the slow seduction of the Sith. Perhaps we are more allies than you know. I fear the exile has let his feelings for the Miraluka affect his judgment - and it will doom him.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «I do not believe so. And neither do you - you have seen his stance, his movements. They mirror hers, not yours. But do not mistake my intent, we are together in this. And if we stand together, we may yet prevent a greater tragedy.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «What you think is of no concern - what the exile thinks is what should concern us.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Influence]**
- Mira: «You didn't have to do that, but I guess maybe that's the point.» [K2/Influence]
- Disciple: «Maybe fewer people would distrust Jedi if all of them behaved as you did.» [K2/Influence]
- Mira: «That was a pretty nice thing you did - I'm impressed.» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «I am not speaking to you, alien. Be silent!» [K2/Influence]
- Atton: «Next time, warn me - if you're out to kill someone, don't surprise me like that.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- Disciple: «You showed her a great kindness... I hope that she and her family can use it to build a new life.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Visas: «You are still unready to face my Master. Any flaw, he will sense. As he is a seam in the fabric of the Force, he senses fractures and weaknesses around him. By extending such kindnesses to others, you leave yourself vulnerable.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- Mandalore: «While you're at it, tell him to forget he ever saw us. Could be useful.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- Mandalorian Sentry: «I'm surprised you got this far - the jungle doesn't usually let its prey go that easily. What are you doing here?» [K2/Jungle]
- Disciple: «Even though Mandalorians were your sworn enemy, you would still save one in need? You're a woman of many levels.» [K2/Jungle]
- Disciple: «I will have to think on what you've said.» [K2/Jungle]
- Bao-Dur: «I have no love for Mandalorians, but just letting him starve up there... I think you did the right thing.» [K2/Jungle]
- Mira: «I don't like the sound of that. I think you've just stirred up a mynock nest.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- Xarga: «So you aren't completely ignorant in the ways of battle. Good. But all of you must pass through its perimeter, and undetected would be better.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]
- Xarga: «Do you see the sensor?» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- Tarn: «I'm sorry, Master Jedi! I won't tell nobody if you spare me. I promise!» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- Atton: «Could you please lighten up for one second?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Disciple: «What are you talking about? What is wrong?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Visas: «What do you mean?» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- Disciple: «Your actions have averted disaster here. That was an admirable use of the Jedi teachings.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Bao-Dur: «You continually amaze me. A lot of people would've been hurt or worse.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- Atton: «What is it you wish to do?» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Kreia: «If you are so certain of your path then do what you will; I care not.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Disciple: «If you come within striking distance of the Jedi, then the search may be over before it's begun.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Visas: «You mistake my intentions. I care nothing for this Jedi. But if finding him will speed our journey, then I will aid you in your search.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Disciple: «It is not my place to say whether you should aid us in our search. If you do, however, you may cause the Jedi we seek to mistake our intentions.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- Atris: «Then I shall send you on your way... it is now time for you to depart.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Disciple: «This is one of those times when I'm convinced you're thinking three steps ahead of me, and I cannot see the destination.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Mandalore: «That's ship's had some proud owners. You're disgracing it by giving it away like a durasteel chip.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- Geriel: «Thank you for trying...» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- Disciple: «It's good to see you've returned safely. What happened on Onderon?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Mira: «About time you showed up. We finished our part and have been sitting on our hands waiting for you. So, what happened?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Disciple: «Is that so? Well, I hope your actions contributed to Onderon's continued cooperation with the Republic.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Mira: «Hm. We'll see if Onderon's newfound 'peace' lasts as long as your tremendous ego.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Disciple: «Very well. I'm sure you are equally disinterested in talking about what happened within the Temple of Freedon Nadd.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Kreia: «Jedi holocrons. Yet warm to the touch, you said?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «It is standing atop the summit of a great mountain, the winds tearing about you, then finding yourself buried alive - trapped, helpless, and alone. It is like having the energy of youth... then feeling the cloak of years fall upon you, and knowing you are weak, fragile, and a thing easily discarded. It is knowing what you want to say and never finding the words. It is a chorus, replaced with silence. Hearing teachings without meaning. It is like a beloved pupil to whom you have shared everything, sacrificed everything, and then having them turn from you... and forget all you were.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Sith_Tomb]**
- Dark Side Energy: «You feel the distinct presence of the dark side here. A sickly fascinating feeling tugs at your belly. Waves of anger ripple through the air.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Dark Side Energy: «You feel the distinct presence of the dark side here. Waves of fear and distress ripple through the air.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]

**[K2/Telos]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «It is good that you have returned. You have much to answer for.» [K2/Telos]
- Handmaiden Sister: «You have betrayed us. You have betrayed Atris.» [K2/Telos]
- Handmaiden Sister: «You are no longer one of us. You followed the Jedi, betrayed your oath.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Where have you been? You have been absent so long. I feared for your safety. Were you with the exile all this time?» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Commanded? Did I command you to consort with him? To follow his teachings? To betray your oath?» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Of course you do not. But you will learn.» [K2/Telos]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- G0-T0: «I just realized there was a business transaction I needed to make, back at the Ebon Hawk. Surely, you can do without me here.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K2/Visas]**
- Disciple: «You spared her.» [K2/Visas]

## The Disciple (Mical)
*Corpus coverage: 277 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 2 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 1 pronoun references resolved from context · 54 lines addressed to them · 18 narration lines · 29 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Mical is a former Jedi student whose master left for the war [K2/Disciple].
### Personality and motivations
A scholar; admires the Exile.
### Arc (scene by scene)
- Found at the Enclave sublevel (female Exile).
- Teaches history.
- Can be trained.
### Relationships
- Exile.
- Atton (rivalry).

- Minimal.
