### Quote bank A: The Disciple (Mical)'s own lines, by scene (page)

**[K2/Crystal_Cave]**
- «This cave is Force Sensitive. I've read of places such as this.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]

**[K2/Disciple]**
- «There is something we must discuss. Surely you have noticed that you have been changing - as your powers return, your body suffers. It is a sign of the dark side - all your choices, they affect the flesh, rotting it, as the dark side rots you from within.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Very well - but heed my words. Unless you turn back, the dark side will claim you completely, utterly.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Fear you? No - but I am concerned for you, yes.» [K2/Disciple]
- «It is never too late to choose your path - be mindful of that. There is always hope for you - and redemption.» [K2/Disciple]
- «There is something I have observed, and now I feel I must say it. I have found your presence to be... inspiring. With your growth in the Force, you seem to have found your center, and throughout the dangers we face, you remain calm and focused.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I understand now why others followed you to war. Perhaps that is what leadership is - and it is something I have seen in only a few during my travels. In any event, it has been some time since I traveled with a Jedi, nor one so firmly upon the path. I wanted to thank you. I fear the stories that were spoken of you have misrepresented you. And if I have the opportunity, I shall reverse them whenever they arise.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Very well - then I shall keep my favorable opinions to myself.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Very well - my apologies if I have offended you. I shall remain silent.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Yes? Is something wrong?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Forgive me, but there is something I must ask. In my study of the Jedi histories and the more... contemporary records, I have heard tales of a Jedi who was exiled. You are that Jedi, but the records are somewhat evasive on why this was done. I wanted to discuss why you chose to leave the Jedi Order and accept exile.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Perspective, nothing more.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I see. And because you went to war they cast you out?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Do you have a record of this trial?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Perhaps I shall examine it when I get the chance - with your permission.» [K2/Disciple]
- «That is a shame - I feel nuances are often found when one pays careful attention to historical records.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Well, it is not solely professional interest - I confess I have a passion for Jedi records and lore. And I rarely have seen them pass judgment on anyone.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I shall respect your wishes. My apologies if I caused any offense.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Perhaps not. But one never knows.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Very well. Forgive my question, I meant no offense.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I appreciate your trust - thank you.» [K2/Disciple]
- «The record of the exile's trial - I wish to see it.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I have studied the holorecord of your trial. I am unsure what to make of it. I must confess that I was searching for some meaning beyond the records - a reason for why one would leave the order.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Yes, but exile is rare, and I have found it is not really something that the order can enforce. Believe it or not, it was really your choice.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Did you know that exile is a rare sentence? It is not really something that the order can enforce. Believe it or not, it was really your choice.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I don't think so - perhaps some of them felt strongly about your sentence, but I think something else concerned them. Again, the choice to turn away was yours, not theirs.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I do not know - that is a question best answered by yourself. But it is you who made the choice to turn away, not them.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Because I, too, turned away from the Order. Under less extreme circumstances, perhaps, but stemming from the same reasons.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I am not certain I do, either. But it is something worth considering.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I am not certain of it, but I believe it to be worth considering.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Then I shall speak no more of it.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Very well. Let us see what we can make from the materials at hand.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Certainly. Let me see how many I can make.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Not at the moment. I think we have enough to heal an army - hopefully, we won't need that many.» [K2/Disciple]
- «At times, I meditate, simply close my eyes, and listen. It is quite calming.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I try to treasure these moments before the next crisis begins.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Of course. It would be my pleasure.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Do you need a hand?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Is there some problem? I was only offering to help.» [K2/Disciple]
- «For as long as she needs me, of course.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Actually, there are times when it seems you could use some h-» [K2/Disciple]
- «No, no, I don't. I simply admire her. She has many qualities worthy of respect, and a strength that matches her beauty. Surely you've noticed.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I thought she was the hero.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Atton... I feel that perhaps there has been some miscommunication between us concerning the exile.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Atton, perhaps I should explain - I see relationships with others in a different light, not as possessive, or carnal. Relationships are something shared between two people and give both strength, whether through struggle or whether through affection. I admire her, perhaps even feel for her. It is difficult to be around her and not have such feelings.» [K2/Disciple]
- «But she walks a far more difficult path than the rest of us. I care what happens to her, and if I can help her, I will. Perhaps if there is anything we can agree on, it is that we will be there for her when no one else is. And perhaps save her so that she may continue down the road that she must walk.» [K2/Disciple]
- «The more worlds we travel to, the more questions I have. It is not just the hardships of the people, but something more.» [K2/Disciple]
- «You are right... but there is something more at work here.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Sometimes I wonder if you are right... but at other times, I feel there is something more at work here.» [K2/Disciple]
- «The attacks on Katarr, Telos itself, the decay on Dantooine - something is wrong, but I cannot pinpoint what. It's like their connections have become sickened... damaged. Sometimes I feel like I almost understand, and then it just slips away.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Yes... these dead worlds, well, they have a pattern to them. They were all touched by the Mandalorian Wars and the Jedi Civil War... but sometimes, well, I feel as if they are all connected in some other way. The attacks on Katarr, Telos itself, the decay on Dantooine - something is wrong with life. The connections have been damaged, sickened. Sometimes I feel like I almost understand, and then it just slips away.» [K2/Disciple]
- «No, I do not. But I do think there is some greater plan at work here.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Perhaps... Dantooine is only one of the worlds, and it was only the start of the journey. I would like to study the other locations. It is curious that the Jedi Masters chose those worlds to travel to - I wonder if the two are linked. But I know not how.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Is there... ? Must be hearing things. But for a moment...» [K2/Disciple]
- «You - who are you? What are you doing on this sh-?» [K2/Disciple]
- «I see the death of the galaxy. Of life. At first... I thought it was just conquest, but it's more terrible than that. It's an echo, spreading outwards, killing everything. It's not possible.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I know you - not even the markings of the dark side can hide it. Why have you done this?» [K2/Disciple]
- «How long have you been here, among us?» [K2/Disciple]
- «I will not let you hurt her.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Very well. What is it you wished to know?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Of course. What did you wish to know?» [K2/Disciple]
- «That is hardly surprising. What do you wish to know?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Like you, I was looking for some trace of the Jedi. I had heard mention that one of the Jedi Masters had gone there, but I found no trace of them.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «The other reason was the ruins of the Jedi Enclave... and once I arrived, I felt it was necessary to stay, to protect what was left.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Much had been taken from the Enclave, both by raiders... and others. I wished to preserve what I could.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Many artifacts were taken from the Enclave, but these thefts... they had to have been done by someone who knew the Enclave well. I suspect the Jedi themselves took the holocrons and records, but I do not know why.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Someone has been taking holocrons from sites across the galaxy. It is almost as if someone does not want their knowledge used to find the Jedi. The situation on Dantooine is echoed in other places in the galaxy. Raiders, smugglers, all seek to plunder what remains of the Jedi... and even the Sith. More so than the Jedi themselves, I fear the loss of their history.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Much has been forgotten in recent wars, and I fear that greater troubles shall stem from that loss of knowledge in the future. The destruction of the Academy on Ossus near the Cron Drift in the Sith War. ...the teachings of Master Arca, the adventures of Jolee Bindo on the Rimward Missions. All of these things are in danger of being lost, forever.» [K2/Disciple]
- «A force bond? What do you mean?» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I'm not sure I understand.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «:: Thinks for a moment. ::» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «No, I thought I had heard mention of such connections in some of the holocrons - but I do not possess them, they are part of the holocrons that were taken from the Enclave.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I do not know - I do not know who has taken them. If we were to find them, perhaps I could help you find the answers you need.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I know some of what you speak. It is said that when a Jedi and Padawan establish a close connection, that they can feel each other across distances and coordinate their movements in battle. The intensity of the connection varies... that bonding is said to also be something that manifests itself in such techniques as Bastilla's battle meditation, the ability to touch the minds of others, to demoralize or inspire them. It is also said that moments of death, or near-death, may also cause such bonds - the stronger one is in the Force, the stronger the connection is.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I have never heard of a bond being lethal. I suppose such a thing is possible - I had not truly believed Bastilla's battle meditation until I had seen it in action.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Thoughts - images, perhaps, but not actual communication and words. A bond often causes a sensation to be passed along it, such as extreme fear and pain. Still, I have seen Jedi who have the ability to communicate with aliens and beasts - it is a rare thing, perhaps telepathy is one such talent.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I am an historian and scientist working for the Republic, although I am certain my contemporaries would judge me more a historian than scientist. I have some training in the medical arts, if you are ever need in healing as well. But my chief passion is history. I came to Dantooine in the hopes of finding some trace of the Jedi, but you know how my explorations turned out - instead, it seems a Jedi came to me, and just in time.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Yes, I have been trained by some of the best researchers in the Republic - which is a humbling experience, let me tell you. But if you ever need some medical items broken down or constructed, let me know.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Very well. Let me see if I can treat your wounds. There - that should do it. Do you need anything else?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Are you sure you need healing? You look good to me.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Yes, your muscle tone looks good, you response time seems excellent, and I see nothing that would otherwise impair your health. You have the same vitality about you that you seem to possess, no matter what the circumstances.» [K2/Disciple]
- «It is the truth. You are more fortunate than you know.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I am surprised no one has told you the same before. It is the truth, and evident to anyone with eyes.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Very well - I hope I did not offend you in some way.» [K2/Disciple]
- «You have no wounds that I can see - although the matter of your spirit is something you should address.» [K2/Disciple]
- «It is illusionary, the strength you feel. But I will not debate it with you - your choices are your own.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I do not know if you are aware of how fragile the Republic is at the moment. Its influence is stretched thin, and it grows weaker with time. Still, there is hope - and I must remind myself of it, even when times grow dark. As long as we hold Onderon and Telos, then perhaps we have a chance.» [K2/Disciple]
- «There are many factors - aside from protecting Telos and Onderon, we need a rallying force. We need the Jedi, even if they are just figureheads. That is why I traveled to Dantooine - to find some trace of them.» [K2/Disciple]
- «The Jedi are a symbol. As much damage as their reputation took during the Sith War and the Jedi Civil War, there are still many who they serve as an example. Plus... there have been times in the past where a single Jedi has been enough to change the face of a world... or a galaxy. I suppose I still believe that might be possible. Despite the betrayal of many of the Jedi against the Republic, I must concede that as figureheads, they do serve a vital role.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Dantooine was one of the few Republic worlds on the Outer Rim - it is why the Republic is attempting to get the settlement up and running. But now that you have protected the settlement on Dantooine, it will serve to stabilize the Republic presence on the Rim.» [K2/Disciple]
- «The Republic is fragile right now - Telos is important because its success will determine whether or not the other dead worlds receive the same reconstruction efforts. If Telos is rebuilt and made habitable again, it will affect a string of worlds along the Rim.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Onderon, strangely enough, was unaffected by the Jedi Civil War. It's almost as if Revan didn't want to attack it. Its resources and position on the Rim make it a vital supply line and a guard post against Outer Rim attacks. Also, it's the only world in the Republic still capable of seeding ecosystems into other dead worlds - Onderon's wildlife is some of the most aggressive in the known galaxy. Merely placing some of those beasts on target worlds will guarantee their habitation for years to come.» [K2/Disciple]
- «The Jedi Civil War brought much suffering to the galaxy, and the forces that Malak and Revan amassed against us seemed limitless. Many worlds were destroyed, trade routes disrupted, and the Republic fleet was almost decimated.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It was almost the end. But at that last year, the year before Revan vanished, Revan's assault on the Republic stopped. No one knows why, but it was all that saved the Republic. The fleet the Sith had amassed was no more, and the remaining Sith turned on each other, destroying the academy on Korriban. It did not matter - Revan had already won.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «While it is said that Revan and several Republic heroes and Jedi defeated Malak, in many ways Malak had already won.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «The war was costly, and it shattered the Republic. In time, the Republic might recover. But if a threat strikes now, if certain key worlds are not held, then the Republic will collapse.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Telos, Onderon, Dantooine... without them, the Republic will either die slowly... or quickly, by fire.» [K2/Disciple]
- «That is my thought. The Republic is not without its flaws, but it is preferable to anarchy. Without supply lines and a standing military, the chaos that would result would be greater than the Mandalorian Wars.» [K2/Disciple]
- «It is strange that someone who went to war against the Mandalorians would disregard the damage the fall of the Republic could cause. Of course, if you sought battle for its own sake, then perhaps I could understand your disregard.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Does that mean that all who would die if the Republic collapsed also would not deserve to survive?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Yes, to some, perhaps. Yet the Sith have already experienced their own collapse, and I suspect their new tactics are proof of that. There was no Sith leader since Revan who has survived long enough to rule. Even Malak turned on Revan, and caused his own downfall. The Sith respect order and control, that is true. But few of them seem ever able to hold that power for long - to see their philosophy at work is like watching a continual collapse, just like watching their academy fall in on itself.» [K2/Disciple]
- «It would not be freedom for many. There would be economic collapse, followed by starvation and war. Perhaps it would eventually stabilize after many years, but many would suffer and die unnecessarily.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I do not claim that I am correct, only that I do not wish to see others suffer needlessly when the freedoms could be achieved another way. The Republic is in need of change, I do not deny it. But I do not believe it deserves death... or worse.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I see. Have you ever been in a situation where you are dependent on another? You could not have won the Mandalorian Wars alone. In fact, I doubt you would have survived if you had not others to stand with you. Where are they now, now that you stand here, and they are gone? Did they deserve to die? Did you deserve to live, dependent on them as you were?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Then it only becomes a question of how much blood is worth the price, how many dead are worth such an ideal. And if that same independence could be achieved without battle.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I see the Mandalorian Wars left their mark. Perhaps the Mandalorians won after all... just as they defeated Revan with their philosophy of slaughter.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Of course you do not. But perhaps one day, you will, and I hope for that day.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I think we are done speaking for the time being - this discussion has been too enlightening, I feel.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Not much remains of the Jedi - I am trying to find what little does remain. There are so many histories, relics, holocrons that speak of their teachings and their code - I fear that unless someone makes an effort to find and keep them, they will be lost forever.» [K2/Disciple]
- «[Influence: Success] Because I fear that unless the Jedi teachings are preserved, we will lose the galaxy.» [K2/Disciple]
- «[Influence: Failure] It is a private reason, but it is important. I do not like to see knowledge lost - and unless steps are taken, the Jedi teachings will suffer this fate.» [K2/Disciple]
- «And because, whatever my feelings for the Order, they do not extend to preserving their knowledge.» [K2/Disciple]
- «No, not their lore, their histories - but their masters, their teachers, perhaps. I have tried to keep an open mind about their practices, but I have seen the Republic scarred too many times by their students.» [K2/Disciple]
- «That is a truth I cannot deny - it is sometimes difficult to see that side as I walked upon Dantooine. But I will consider what you have said. There is another side to their efforts and their teaching that I sometimes forget.» [K2/Disciple]
- «That is my feeling as well - and one can see it when one walks on Dantooine. Thank you - it is good to hear a perspective of one who has walked in their halls, and spoken to their masters. I thought I was the only one who held such doubts.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I'm trying to save the Republic - Dantooine and the Jedi Order are instrumental to that effort. Despite the troubles of the Jedi Civil War, there are those among the Republic who still favor the Jedi and wish them to return. And there are Admirals within the fleet, who recognize that the Jedi must be found if the Republic is to hold together. Yet as long as Onderon remains within the Republic, and the efforts on Telos succeed, that is all that matters.» [K2/Disciple]
- «[Influence: Success] You are correct. I am afraid I have not been entirely open with you concerning my past.» [K2/Disciple]
- «[Influence: Failure] I imagine in your travels of the galaxy, you have seen many people. Faces tend to blur together after a time.» [K2/Disciple]
- «If I look familiar, it is because we have met before, at the Enclave on Dantooine, many years ago. As on Coruscant, Force Sensitive children are taken to Dantooine as well, though it is done rarely, and only with those they believe are destined to become Jedi Knights - it is the secret nature of the place. If you are not chosen by a master when you have come of age, however... then the path of the Jedi is denied you. I met you on Dantooine, long ago, briefly.» [K2/Disciple]
- «You taught us the ways of combat, how to hear music within the movements of a lightsaber blade.» [K2/Disciple]
- «You taught us how to move within the Force, and see it flow within others. How to see it in the behaviors of others, and use that sight to achieve truth.» [K2/Disciple]
- «You taught us the ways of the Force, how to hear it sing within others, within the life around Dantooine.» [K2/Disciple]
- «It is difficult to explain the difference between you and Master Vrook, but I think it is because he was knowledgeable, but not a leader, not a mentor. You were different - we could all feel it. And I knew that if I were to have a Master, I would want it to be you. And then you went to war. Many Jedi went to war, and the Jedi Masters proclaimed that you were Jedi no longer. Atris, the mistress of the archives, was first among them. I knew at that moment, that if you would no longer be a Jedi, then you must be correct. I realized I did not want to be a Jedi - instead, I wished to follow your path. And in any event, there was no one to train me, even if I wished it. They all went to war, as I grew past the age of acceptance.» [K2/Disciple]
- «It is possible to forget the Force, you know - if you not have felt it strongly enough, then there is little to miss. But I never felt the Force as strongly as I did when I was with you. And so I decided to serve the Republic, study the Jedi teachings, gather them, perhaps. It was important to me to understand the Jedi now that they were gone. I felt some part of you should be preserved, so that your lessons would not be lost.» [K2/Disciple]
- «There is nothing to be done - and the past is the past.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Perhaps. I still harbor doubts about the path I walked.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Many Jedi went to go fight in the Mandalorian Wars, and few returned. To their disciples left behind on Dantooine on Coruscant, there were few masters to train them. I was one such student. Perhaps not as strong in the Force as the others, but it really did not matter. The teachers left behind were few - only a few students were accepted as the Mandalorian Wars raged on. And when I came of age, there were no masters left to train me. So I was forced to leave the Order, to seek my path elsewhere. It was for the best, I think. I had begun to have... doubts. And I think the masters were aware of them.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Never. I have seen you, traveled with you. My life was mine, not chained to yours.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Your apologies are not needed, or warranted. I chose not to follow the Jedi, and the choice was my own.» [K2/Disciple]
- «That may be so. I had already failed the first test. But enough of the past - it is the present that should concern us.» [K2/Disciple]
- «[Influence: Failure] It is not time, if there ever is a time.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I am on a diplomatic mission - I am one of several tasked with attempting to contact any remaining Jedi and convince them to return to the Republic.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I know something of the Jedi. I have studied them for a good portion of my life... and now that they are gone, such studies are even more important.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I do not think it is a matter of luck, or chance - only my own inability to find them. While the Enclave trained me for many things, I am afraid that the life of a bounty hunter was not among them.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I suspect my turning away from the Force is also a hindrance in such cases. I imagine the Jedi would be easier to find if I still maintained my connections to the Force.» [K2/Disciple]
- «You have? Where have they gone - and why?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Like you? Curious. It is unlike the Jedi to vanish when the Republic is in need of them - and the Republic is more in need of them now than ever.» [K2/Disciple]
- «And what was that - do you know?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Discerning the reasons for why the Jedi do as they do is indeed... confusing. But there must be some reason.» [K2/Disciple]
- «What happened? Was the Jedi killed?» [K2/Disciple]
- «Perhaps the Sith have learned from Malak's defeat and have chosen a different tactic. It is like them to strike from the shadows.» [K2/Disciple]
- «This has Revan's mark in things. Though gone from the galaxy, perhaps something of Revan's teachings remain.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Yes. I do not know if you heard of the civil war on Korriban, but it was caused by Revan's absence. From what I have been able to determine, a year after the battle with the Republic fleet that almost wiped the Republic out, Revan simply... left. When Revan did not return, the factions among the Sith turned on each other. As all those that follow the Sith do, in time.» [K2/Disciple]
- «That is a question I have found no answer to.» [K2/Disciple]
- «But it must have been important for Revan to leave as he did. He did nothing lightly, and his plans often could not been felt for months, or even years after she did them.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «This has Revan's mark in things.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Yes, but there is much of Revan's legacy that was not undone in the Jedi Civil War. I suspect these assassins are one of them. Master Vandar spoke of these assassins in the council chambers. They were used before large assaults on planets and Jedi, to weaken them.» [K2/Disciple]
- «It was as I feared... they have given up the galaxy. Without them...» [K2/Disciple]
- «New strength?» [K2/Disciple]
- «[Influence: Success] I fear you are right. Their hesitation almost destroyed the Republic - and there is some evidence to suggest that it gave birth to the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Disciple]
- «[Influence: Failure] I refuse to believe it - there must be some other reason, something else that we cannot see.» [K2/Disciple]
- «No. It is something of a mystery why they would exile themselves as they have. It is not the way of the Jedi to vanish in such a way... especially when the Republic is in need of them. I fear that there is something else at work, something that we cannot see. Then again, perhaps the Jedi are hiding simply because so many people hate them these days.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It is difficult sometimes for the Jedi to see such things, since much of it is rooted in human nature, and the Jedi are often removed from events of daily life, insulated. But the reason the Jedi Civil War was named such was because few in the galaxy can recognize the difference between the Sith and the Jedi. To them, they are both Jedi - with different philosophies.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Not always. Jedi often fall. They caused much harm on Onderon, for example, in the name of peace and protection. Ulic Qel-Droma and Exar Kun, the two dark lords during the Sith War many decades ago, were once Jedi Knights, as were Revan and Malak. It is perhaps more amazing that some still trust Jedi after many have fallen and endangered the galaxy. It is also proof that a single Force wielder can change the face of the galaxy, and that is a frightening thing indeed.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Hate? No, I do not hate them. They only raise questions without answers. Jedi are not supposed to be like the rest of us. They are supposed to see a higher purpose in all things. And they are supposed to train students responsibly and well, so mistakes of the past are not repeated. Yet all I saw was ignorance and arrogance, and what those seeds created in the Republic. It is difficult to follow the Jedi Code, when so few others have. But you know this.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «[Influence: Failure] The problems with the Jedi are my own - I do not wish to burden you with my troubles.» [K2/Disciple]
- «[Influence: Success] I think you are right. It is time.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I have watched you. You have become strong in the Force again - but that is not all. You have achieved a center in the chaos around us, and I have felt it. My master - the one intended for me - left to fight in the Mandalorian Wars. Now she has returned, and I ask her now if she will train me in the ways of the Force.» [K2/Disciple]
- «They are.» [K2/Disciple]
- «The one who was to be my Master was lost at Malachor V.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I want you to teach me the ways of the Force. To become a Jedi Knight, what I meant to be.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I have thought about what you have said about the Jedi Order. You were correct - it was a truth I did not want to face.» [K2/Disciple]
- «While I do not believe the Jedi to be evil,  I feel perhaps that they are misguided - it is hard to protect the galaxy when you are removed from it and its people. The Mandalorian Wars - I understand why the Jedi hesitated, but at the same time, I wonder if they had joined Revan, united, if perhaps the Jedi Civil War would never have happened. It is very easy to  blame Revan and Malak - yet the Jedi Council was equally to blame. I believe that there is very little truth that can be found within the conference room on Coruscant.» [K2/Disciple]
- «The galaxy needs strength now. If the Jedi will not come forth and fight this threat, then we must. And if that strength comes from the dark side, then that is what we must accept. I ask that you train me in the ways of the Force, to teach me the ways of power so that a greater stability may be achieved. I do not believe in the Jedi and the Sith. I believe in strength, and that is what the galaxy needs now.» [K2/Disciple]
- «:: Silence ::» [K2/Disciple]

**[K2/Docks]**
- «Her nature tells of such things to any who care to listen.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Enclave_Courtyard]**
- «I'm surprised if the ruins have any holocrons left. They are heavily sought by Jedi Masters.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- «That man needs our help! Why are you ignoring him?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «I don't know what you seek to gain by killing him. You act as if you are touched by the dark side!» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «I am an historian and scientist working for the Republic, although I am certain my contemporaries would judge me more a historian than scientist.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «I was looking for some trace of the Jedi, as you were. I had heard mention that one of the Jedi Masters, Master Vrook, had been sighted there. I wished to speak to him.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «It was my mistake to use the term "thefts". I apologize. In any case, if Jedi did take things, for the sake of preservation, of course, then they left no clue as to where they may have taken them.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Much has been forgotten in recent wars, and I fear that greater troubles shall stem from that loss of knowledge in the future. The destruction of the Academy on Ossus near the Cron Drift in the Sith War.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «...the teachings of Master Arca, the adventures of Jolee Bindo on the Rimward Missions. All of these things are in danger of being lost, forever.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «What else would you like to know?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Many Jedi defied the Order during the Mandalorian Wars - and it paved the way for the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «There is no blame - all must accept. But at its core, one must wonder if it was the failure of the Jedi teachings... or the teachers themselves. Many of the Jedi Council trained Exar Kun, Ulic... Revan and Malak. How could they not see the danger they posed? And if they could not...» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «...perhaps there was some essential part of their teachings that was flawed. Something beyond the Jedi Code that they were missing.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «I am not certain you understand what happened with Revan. Do you wish me to explain?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Revan was captured in the middle of the Jedi Civil War, due in no small part to his apprentice, Malak, who turned on him.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Revan was brought before the Jedi Council and put on trial. You must understand that the situation was desperate... and the Jedi needed to know how Revan had amassed the forces arrayed against them. As I understand it, they were able to convince Revan to have a change of heart.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «But it was only for a time. Revan helped the Republic defeat Malak. I suspect it was out of revenge.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Revan helped the Republic defeat Malak. Perhaps in his conversation with the Jedi Masters, they were able to convince him of the rightfulness of their actions.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Revan had many Masters. Zhar, Dorak, Master Kae before Kae left for the Wars. Towards the end of his training, he sought out many to learn techniques. It is said that he returned to his first master at the end of his training, in order to learn how he might best leave the order.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I'm trying to save the Republic - Dantooine and the Jedi Order are instrumental to that effort.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Despite the troubles of the Jedi Civil War, there are those among the Republic who still favor the Jedi and wish them to return. And there are Admirals within the fleet, who recognize that the Jedi must be found if the Republic is to hold together.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «(Yet) as long as Onderon remains within the Republic, and the efforts on Telos succeed, that is all that matters.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «The Republic is fragile right now - Telos is important because its success will determine whether or not other dead worlds receive the same reconstruction efforts. If Telos is rebuilt and made habitable again, it will affect a string of worlds along the Rim.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Onderon, strangely enough, was unaffected by the Jedi Civil War. It's almost as if Revan didn't want to attack it. Its position and resources on the Rim make it a vital supply line and a guardpost against Outer Rim attacks. Also, it's the only world in the Republic still capable of seeding ecosystems into other dead worlds - Onderon's wildlife is some of the most aggressive in the known galaxy. Merely placing some of those beasts on target worlds will guarantee their habitation for years to come.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «The Jedi are a symbol. As much damage as their reputation took during the Sith War and the Jedi Civil War, there are still many to whom they serve as an example. Plus... there have been times in the past where a single Jedi has been enough to change the face of a world... or a galaxy. I suppose I still believe that might be possible. Despite the betrayal of many of the Jedi against the Republic, I must concede that as figureheads, they serve a vital role.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Dantooine was one of the few Republic worlds on the Outer Rim - it is why the Republic is attempting to get the settlement up and running. Dantooine is within reach of several other Rim worlds, and the Republic will need it as a resupply post if they are to keep a presence on the Rim.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «I do not know if you are aware of how fragile the Republic is at the moment. Its influence is stretched thin, and it grows weaker with time.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «The war was costly, and it shattered the Republic. In time, the Republic might recover. [[../Enclave Sublevel#Know» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «[[../Enclave Sublevel#Know» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Before you go, I had a question for you - you came to Dantooine in search of Jedi. Why?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «I know some of what you speak. It is said that when a Jedi and Padawan establish a close connection, that they can feel each other across distances and coordinate their movements in battle. I know some of what you speak. It is said that when a Jedi and Padawan establish a close connection, that they can feel each other across distances and coordinate their movements in battle. It is also said that moments of death, or near-death, may also cause such bonds - the stronger one is in the Force, the stronger the connection is.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «[[../Enclave Sublevel#Have me» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «I do not know - I do not know who has taken them. I'm sorry. I wish I could tell you more.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «What answers do you seek? It has been my experience that Jedi rarely answer such questions - or instead, indulge in half-truths.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Exiled you? It is strange they would sentence you and not speak honestly of your crime.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «It is not the Jedi way to lie... well, no, that is not true. There are times when truth is concealed, if they think a greater good can be achieved. But when such things are done, it is always done with the intention that the person they withheld truth from cannot be trusted with that knowledge. That it will bring harm upon themselves and others.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Perhaps there was some greater danger in you knowing the reason than in the reason itself. Strange. [[../Enclave Sublevel#End» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «If the Sith are rising in the galaxy again, then it is strange the Jedi would not be there to meet them... and that we have not seen more evidence of the Sith.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «No, I believe you. I merely find such subtleties among the Sith to be strange. They have been known to practice deception...» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «[[../Enclave Sublevel#End» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «I will return to Khoonda now and await the next transport. You may find me there if you have more questions.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «In any case, it seems to me like our goals are compatible.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «If you would have me, I can apply my knowledge and skills to helping you find the answers you seek.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Admiral, this is Mical. I have found the exile.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Welcome back. Is there anything else I can do for you?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «All faces tend to blur together after a time. It is not an uncommon thing.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

- «Me?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- «This looks like some kind of monitoring station... but it's tracking incredible amounts of data.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/HK-47]**
- «If that was spoken aloud, then it would explain much - is that what happened?» [K2/HK-47]

**[K2/Influence]**
- «So it seems one does not need to be in touch with the Force to act with compassion.» [K2/Influence]
- «It may seem like a small act you did, but some things travel, and more good may come from it. I am honored to have seen a Jedi behave as a Jedi - it was not something I still expected to see in this galaxy.» [K2/Influence]
- «As you wish. Let us continue on, then.» [K2/Influence]
- «Through such acts, the galaxy may heal.» [K2/Influence]
- «Maybe fewer people would distrust Jedi if all of them behaved as you did.» [K2/Influence]
- «Every good deed helps make up for the past.» [K2/Influence]
- «Such actions have their own rewards.» [K2/Influence]
- «It is good to see that the Jedi still live, even if the Force forsakes them.» [K2/Influence]
- «What happened? I.... Why did we just do that? That innocent! They meant us no harm.» [K2/Influence]
- «That one meant us no harm. Yet we attacked - I attacked. I do not understand why.» [K2/Influence]
- «I must reflect on this - perhaps I am changing somehow, or simply do not know myself.» [K2/Influence]
- «That one meant us no harm.» [K2/Influence]
- «When you act... so do I.» [K2/Influence]
- «I do not know what compelled me to attack.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- «I don't particularly like the idea that he's working for them now.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «You showed her a great kindness... I hope that she and her family can use it to build a new life.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «For the sake of the civilians here, we should exercise restraint in this battle.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- «Use caution when affecting the minds of others. Some have been scarred by its use - both the user and the recipient.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- «Even though Mandalorians were your sworn enemy, you would still save one in need? You're a woman of many levels.» [K2/Jungle]
- «I will have to think on what you've said.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- «The others are depending on us to see this through.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- «...how could the Jedi leave the Republic? Was it because of the Civil War? Is it possible that they...» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- «No, Atton, I think it may be a trap.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «What are you talking about? What is wrong?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «If they are coming after us, then they will be after her as well. We need to go and rescue her.» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- «I shall do my best.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- «Your actions have averted disaster here. That was an admirable use of the Jedi teachings.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Let's just hope we can contain this before it gets out of hand.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- «It is not your choice, Atton, it is hers. What is it you wish to do?» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Forgive me, but these bounty hunters pose a threat to her. Their persistence is unusual - and if they are not stopped, one of them may prove lucky.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «If you come within striking distance of the Jedi, then the search may be over before it's begun.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «It is not my place to say whether you should aid us in our search. If you do, however, you may cause the Jedi we seek to mistake our intentions.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- «This is the Jedi Council? But they seem... seem weakened, somehow.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Is something wrong? You look troubled - I can feel it.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «That is an odd answer. What did you mean by it?» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I do not understand what you mean.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Never.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I follow you, feel as I do, because it is my choice.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I believe in what we are doing - what you are doing. I am here because I choose to be.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I simply do. There is nothing I can show you as proof, except give you my word. Something happened within the Enclave. What is it?» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Then they do not understand you. That is the danger of being a Jedi. When one seperates themselves from others, chooses to lead a life of isolation, denying what makes them a feeling being - it is easy to make such judgments. And such judgments, I believe, are made in ignorance. There is no danger in what you represent - other than your humanity. You change others, but I do not believe it is due to the Force. I believe it is because you are a natural leader - and because you feel connected to the people around you. Where they look at you and see the death of the Force, I look at you and see hope for all life. And that perhaps a life lived without the Force is not the punishment it is believed to be.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I will understand if you feel you must go alone, but I ask that you do not. Instead, take strength from your connections to others. Do not forsake them, as you did in exile.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- «This is one of those times when I'm convinced you're thinking three steps ahead of me, and I cannot see the destination.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- «How could you break them like this?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «I know that you are right. But it is difficult for me to accept. I am still learning from your wisdom.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «I understand.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «As you command...» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- «It's good to see you've returned safely. What happened on Onderon?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «I hope that Onderon's new unity of leadership will ensure their continued cooperation with the Republic.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Is that so? Well, I hope your actions contributed to Onderon's continued cooperation with the Republic.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Very well. I'm sure you are equally disinterested in talking about what happened within the Temple of Freedon Nadd.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- «Apathy is death.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- «A lightsaber was used to fuse this door shut.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «Lonna Vash... I heard stories of her.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Sith_Tomb]**
- «This entire area is highly attuned to the dark side. I've heard of locations like this, but never seen one. The Sith must have come here because of it. We should avoid that area entirely.» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «The valley we just came from was full of dark energy, but this cave is darker still.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «These Tuk'ata were once trained to serve the Sith here.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K2/Visas]**
- «You spared her.» [K2/Visas]
- «I did not believe you would. I am more concerned about what she will do when she recovers.» [K2/Visas]
- «That is not what I asked - although I thought perhaps we had *some* measure of trust between us.» [K2/Visas]
- «I am not advocating harming her at all. I think she may be a means by which we can track down the Sith - in time.» [K2/Visas]
- «Just... be careful. I am concerned that you may feel a certain kinship for her, and that she may exploit that weakness.» [K2/Visas]
- «Just... be careful. Not everyone can be saved.» [K2/Visas]

### Quote bank B2: pronoun references resolved from context (context-resolved, hand-reviewed scene-wide (each line read by hand against the whole conversation; anchor = the line that names the character))
- [context-resolved] Kreia: «If he leaves this place, he will leave all that is Jedi behind him at last. A heart can only handle so many betrayals before turning away entirely. He will become a Senator on one of the Mid-Rim worlds, and devote himself to the people there. He will be a wise, steady ruler - and he will not call upon the Force again. But his rule will grow to be a cold one, and he will find that the code of the galaxy pales in comparison to the failures of the code of the Jedi.» [K2/Trayus_Core] (anchor: And the Disciple?...)

**[K2/Disciple]**

- Atton: «Problem? No, no problem. Just wondering how long you're planning to stick around.» [K2/Disciple]
- Atton: «How heroic of you. Well, she doesn't need you. In fact, we were doing just fine until you showed up.» [K2/Disciple]
- Atton: «Always with the details, aren't you? You can't fool me. You have some agenda, spying on her, always keeping your eye on her.» [K2/Disciple]
- Atton: «And another thing - stop being all noble around her, in your big hero way. She sees right through your little act. She likes honest guys, not guys who run around being unselfish and heroic all the time.» [K2/Disciple]
- Atton: «Oh, great, what do you want now? To give me a hand? I told you, I don't need any help, especially from you.» [K2/Disciple]
- Atton: «No, I understand you just fine. Look, I know what's going on in your want-to-be Jedi mind. Thought you Jedi or ex-Jedi or whatever you are were supposed to be above all that.» [K2/Disciple]

- Kreia: «Enough. What did you see in the web of worlds that have died? What did you see when you saw it through the Force?» [K2/Disciple]
- Kreia: «You are a wasted pawn of the Republic, young one. You could have been so much more, even with your wide-eyed innocence, your naive love for others. Now you understand the magnitude of what is being done.» [K2/Disciple]

- Kreia: «You know the truth. I have always been here, watching and listening to the echo you have found. You know its source and what must be done.» [K2/Disciple]

## Mira
*Corpus coverage: 289 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 17 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 13 pronoun references resolved from context · 63 lines addressed to them · 34 narration lines · 14 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Mira is a Mandalorian War refugee raised among Mandalorians who became a bounty hunter [K2/Mira].
### Personality and motivations
Rescues people rather than killing them.
### Arc (scene by scene)
- **Nar Shaddaa (light path):** joins.
- Hanharr's life-debt chase.
- **Malachor Depths:** fight with Hanharr.
### Relationships
- Hanharr.
- Exile.
- Atton.
### Abilities
- Rockets and traps; latent Force.
### Feats
- Survives Hanharr.
### Power-scaling statements
- None.
### Duels and fights
- vs Hanharr.
### Verdict implications
- Street level.
### Open questions
- None.

- [K2/Party] Mira: Class Scout, Level 6. Feats: DC=6, DC=8, Precise Shot I, Targeting II, Uncanny Dodge I.

**[K2/Depths]**

**[K2/Disciple]**
- «You can say that again.» [K2/Disciple]

**[K2/Docks]**
- «Looks more like one of the Sith to me.» [K2/Docks]
- «Sure doesn't look much like a Jedi.» [K2/Docks]
- «Well, looks like the Jedi raised their standards.» [K2/Docks]
- «And it looks like you're not alone. How many Jedi are on this moon, anyway?» [K2/Docks]

- «What, working for the Hutts suddenly make you stupider, Hanharr? You touch me, you'll break the truce, and then you won't last long enough to slip off this moon.» [K2/Docks]

- «I don't want your life debt, Hanharr - I told you!» [K2/Docks]
