**[K2/Droid_Warehouse]**
- «Uh, you're running a little late - your friend already walked into a trap in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «We took out Visquis, but Goto has her... and that means no bounty for me.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «There's no way to get her back.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «No one knows how to reach Goto except Visquis, and that squid-head died in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «The only way to reach Goto is if we had a Jedi, but now, he's got your friend, he doesn't have anybody else he wants captured...» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «He's got a cloaking device. He's the one that arranges the meetings on his ship, and until then, he can't be found. Trust me, if anyone knew how to track his ship, he'd have every bounty hunter and criminal on Nar Shaddaa gunning for it.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «Yeah, you're right - but his warehouse is shut down because of it, he's not sending any more flights out. Even if you signed on with Vogga, that slug wouldn't let you out of the system anyway.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «If you were hunting for Goto's yacht, your freighter would be flying blind... well, unless it was one of Vogga the Hutt's cargo ships, then it would be snapped up by Goto pretty quick.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «Goto's been preying on Vogga's freighters for a while now - it's the reason Vogga's had to haul his bulk up here to Nar Shaddaa from Nal Hutta. Even with all the traffic around Nar Shaddaa, Goto seems to always know which ones are Vogga's, and his ship just snaps them up.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «Probably does it by tracking their transponder codes, but no one knows how he's getting them.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «I guess. You'd need to get the codes first, then retrofit your ship so it had the right transponder signal.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «Well, it's not just getting any code - it's the one for Vogga's freighters, and those are stored in his droid warehouse.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «Problem is, Vogga's shut down the droid warehouse until he can find out who's been leaking the codes. You'd have to be a droid to get in there.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «Count me in.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- «Yeah, and I don't like being cheated. Trust me, Goto's yacht is going to have some pretty heavy defenses. You're going to need all the help you can get.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- «Are you sure you want to take this guy with us? I mean, he's easy on the eyes, but he talks like a roomful of Jedi.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «Great. I hope he doesn't snore like Atton.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- «Wait one second! I refuse to participate in this. I'm not dancing for some slobbering, overgrown slug.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «You need me to do this? Why don't you do it?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «You need me to do this? Can't we find another way?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «Well, all right... I guess I'll do it.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «Oh so it's going to be like that? Fine! I will do it. I'm not going to step down from a challenge like that.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «I don't know why I do these things for you, honestly. Vogga had better watch himself though, or he'll be the biggest lump of dead slug on Nar Shaddaa.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «Now what?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «All right, just give it to me and let's get this over with.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- «I don't think I've ever seen so many consoles - wha- what is this place?» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «I can't believe we just blew up Goto's yacht. That's going to destabilize crime throughout this whole sector.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «You don't understand. Crime in the Y'Toub System - it's like the economy. Plus the power vaccuum? Even if Vogga gets up and running again, the system is going to be feeling the effects for years to come.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «Zez-Kai Ell? Um... well that's not true. I already found him. Actually he found me first. He hired me to watch out for you, keep Goto off your back... until he could meet with you. We'll need to head back to Nar Shaddaa, to that safe house off the docks. I said I'd meet up with him there if we ran into any trouble.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «What do you want now?» [K2/Goto's_Yacht] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- «I don't know what the two of you are arguing about. I caught him, he's mine.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «But it's obvious you two need some advice, so let me lay out the attack pattern for you. It's simple - when you want a man, you jab him with a Bothan Stunner, then while he's screaming in pain, slap some stun cuffs on him. Then starve him for two or three days until he becomes open to suggestion, then double-check his bounty and see if he's worth anything.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Call it what you want. Me, I love my targets.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Influence]**
- «Uh... hey, thanks.» [K2/Influence]
- «Well, that's not something you see everyday. Guess they can't take the Jedi out of the exile, can they?» [K2/Influence]
- «Well, whatever. We better keep moving - didn't mean to waste your time talking.» [K2/Influence]
- «You didn't have to do that, but I guess maybe that's the point.» [K2/Influence]
- «Not what I'd do, but I'm sure they're happy just the same.» [K2/Influence]
- «That was a pretty nice thing you did - I'm impressed.» [K2/Influence]
- «Guess the Jedi Order isn't dead.» [K2/Influence]
- «Why... what did we just do? We didn't need t-» [K2/Influence]
- «Why did we... why did I just do that? There was no reason to, but suddenly... suddenly this urge came over me.» [K2/Influence]
- «I... didn't like it. It felt wrong, but I couldn't help myself.» [K2/Influence]
- «I... can't believe we just attacked like that.» [K2/Influence]
- «What did we gain by that?» [K2/Influence]
- «There's no credits in acting like Hanharr.» [K2/Influence]
- «Being around you is starting to affect me.» [K2/Influence]
- «You're acting like Hanharr.» [K2/Influence]
- «I can't believe I just helped you do that.» [K2/Influence]
- «What is your problem?» [K2/Influence]
- «Why did we just do that?» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- «Hey, we should check our lines of fire around all these bystanders. No grenades.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- «You better not try any of those Jedi mind tricks on me.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]**
- «Good eyes, Hanharr. No wonder you're still number two on Nar Shaddaa.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- «Yeah, and maybe you'd like to explain why you've decided to backstab Goto and claim the Jedi for yourself.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- «You've signed on with Vogga the Hutt? Visquis, you're dumber than I thought. There's no way Goto won't find out.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- «Yeah, right. I'm not telling you where the Jedi is - it's my bounty, and that means she's under my protection.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- «That does it, Hanharr. I don't want to kill you, but I will if you don't get out of my way.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «Great. I need to get out of here.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- «Watch it, Jedi. There's a body over there. A lone Mandalorian. Fresh kill, too. I can't figure the percentage in them still being on this rock.» [K2/Jungle]
- «Most Mandalorians I know are mercenaries. And not the cheap kind.» [K2/Jungle]
- «This used to be their headquarters during the wars. Could be all sorts of reasons. He's probably employed by someone. All Mandalorians are these days.» [K2/Jungle]
- «Less than a standard week. Someone must have paid a lot of money to hire him. Mandalorian mercenaries don't come cheap.» [K2/Jungle]
- «Whoever hired him had a good reason to send him to this jungle. There may be more of them around. Keep your weapon charged.» [K2/Jungle]
- «Great, just what we needed. Mandalorians.» [K2/Jungle]
- «I don't like the sound of that. I think you've just stirred up a mynock nest.» [K2/Jungle]
- «Whoever these visitors are, they don't know the terrain very well.» [K2/Jungle]
- «If we spot any scouts, we'll need to take them out fast before they can warn their companions.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Jungle_Landing]**
- «You thinking what I'm thinking? These jungles are filled with long narrow paths. Dumb beasts that just keep charging at you. This place is perfect for mines. If their carcasses were worth anything you could make a fortune.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]
- «Well, if it isn't the Zhug brothers. Sorry, but this one's my bounty, so keep those weapon barrels pointed down.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]
- «And very dead, if you keep pointing those guns at us.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]
- «Sweet talk isn't going to make me change my mind about you, Jedi.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]
- «Watch it, cargo. Otherwise I might decide to split the bounty with the Zhugs.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- «Wait, this is the wrong way. We need to turn around.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]
- «A type 2 perimeter motion relay? A little out-dated, but it'll do the job. If anything moves within 6 meters of that, then they know they've got visitors. Someone skilled with a stealth field generator can get by it, but that's about it. But if they set off any of those mines, it'll still send the signal.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- «So you used to be a farmer?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- «...this place is so quiet. Nothing like Nar Shaddaa... I don't even know why I'm here...mm - wonder how much the bounty on the Jedi is now.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Don't understand how that Jedi keeps in shape like she does; she's barely changed from the holorecords. She just seems to be naturally beautiful.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Don't understand how that Jedi keeps in shape like he does; he's barely changed from the holorecords. He just seems to be naturally young.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- «So you're the big Jedi that everyone's been talking about. You don't look so tough to me. I thought you Jedi were supposed to be smart, and here you are, running around Nar Shaddaa sticking your lightsaber into everyone's business.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «What, were you planning to save everyone on this moon? You're attracting more attention than a fleet of Sith warships.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «You're making some powerful enemies and attracting more attention than a fleet of Sith warships.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Yeah? Well, maybe you'll have time for this - I'm here to warn you.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «I'm Mira. I'm the best bounty hunter in this system, and that's not me bragging, that's fact. I had you in my sights ever since you landed. I've been watching you run all over the Refugee Sector like a bantha, and for someone with a price on their head as high as yours, you sure don't know how to keep a low profile. Look - I know that squid-head Visquis sent you a message to meet him in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. He works for Goto, and it's a trap. I'm betting he's going to lure you in there, start a fight, and then he's going to wrap you up and deliver you to Goto, dead, claiming you attacked him.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Just this - I know you're meeting with Visquis.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Well, whatever your reasons are, the fact you're meeting with Visquis is what I want to talk about.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «If I know about it, that means everybody else on this moon knows about it - or will soon enough. And when that happens, the bounty hunter truce is off. That means things are going to get real ugly, real quick.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «No - I don't think you get it. They're not going to come after you.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «I think your friends are the ones in trouble.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «This is one of my safe houses. It's... it's not too pretty to look at, but keeps away prying eyes.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Yeah, well, it's one of those tradeoffs. All the freighter exhaust from the docks, you know. Some aliens actually like breathing it, if you can believe it.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «You could say I've gotten used to it. This is actually perfume compared to how the rest of the docks smell.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «You know Visquis is arranging a trap for you in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. No surprise there. Thing is, he intends to cut the bounty hunters out of the loop and deliver you to Goto personally. Hm. Not smart from where I'm standing.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Well, for one thing, you're going to stay here, and I'm going to meet with Visquis instead.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Oh, you will. Just not right now. In fact, you're going to stay here until I meet with Visquis.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «An ex-Jedi with a death wish doesn't concern me - unless credits are involved. That's why you're going to stay here until I meet with Visquis.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Well, that smell you noticed when you came in. It's probably filtering through your lungs right now. I upped the dosage in case you had some Jedi training to resist poisons.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Actually, you really don't have much choice. That smell you noticed when you came in... yeah, it's filtering through your lungs right now.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Anybody without olfactory blockers like I have is going to start feeling dizzy... and eventually fall unconscious. Good night, Jedi. Let me just take that environment suit, and I'll be back soon.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «No, you don't, Hanharr.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «I was saving this dart for you, Hanharr, but right now, I need one less Jedi running around. Good thing I upped the dosage enough to take down a ronto.» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- «I'll do it, but you're still my bounty. You won't lose me this easily.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- «I agree, there's no percentage in getting involved with this.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «We're going to go after these captains, right? As long as we're not killing them, I'm on board. This could be quite lucrative for us.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «You're wrong, everything is about money. It's just a question of how much.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Together this mission should be as easy as hunting kath hounds in a kennel.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Be that way. But if I didn't have to keep track of you I'd finish this job.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Mira]**
- «Oh... hey.» [K2/Mira]
- «I was going to ask you that... it's just that your face, it's like you haven't been getting much sleep. What's wrong with you?» [K2/Mira]
- «I never saw a Jedi who had that look before. It's not natural.» [K2/Mira]
- «No, it's just business. Look, forget I said anything.» [K2/Mira]
- «Oh, hey. Uh... heh... can I ask you something?» [K2/Mira]
- «Your face... you, well, you have this glow. I mean, not a real glow, but... It's like you're calm, at peace? But it's more than that. You haven't been chewing on spice, have you?» [K2/Mira]
- «Okay, maybe not spice. Maybe someone just jammed a power coupling up your ion engine and switched it on full.» [K2/Mira]
- «Oh. Well, it shows. It's like you're hooked up to a power coupling, it's weird. I mean, not bad weird, just weird.» [K2/Mira]
- «Heh... for a minute there, I thought you and the Disciple... but it's just the Force.» [K2/Mira]
- «Heh... for a minute there, I thought you and Atton... but it's just the Force.» [K2/Mira]
- «Heh... for a minute there, I thought you and Visas... but it's just the Force.» [K2/Mira]
- «Heh... for a minute there, I thought you and the Handmaiden... but it's just the Force.» [K2/Mira]
- «You know. Hooked up a power coupling, you know?» [K2/Mira]
- «Did you get out much as a Jedi? I was asking if you two had been... you know, intimate.» [K2/Mira]
- «Got it. Just checking.» [K2/Mira]
- «Yeah? Something wrong?» [K2/Mira]
- «Grenades? I didn't think Jedi used grenades.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well, I don't like to use them unless they're C-5 concussion spheres or the Merr-Sonn cryoban pellets. Sonic screamers aren't too bad either, just don't use them on a Bith.» [K2/Mira]
- «Makes their head explode like a melon. And don't do it to a Sullustan, either, makes their ears bleed and they gibber twice as fast.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well, keep hoping, because that's not my specialty. Trust me, mass slaughter weapons just cause trouble.» [K2/Mira]
- «Would you please shut him down? He's been like that ever since he arrived. 'Here's how to kill this, here's how to kill that...'» [K2/Mira]
- «Great.» [K2/Mira]
- «[Success] Well... all right, you've got a good point. But look, be careful with them. All it takes is one blaster bolt to your bandoleer strap and suddenly you're Peragus. Get it?» [K2/Mira]
- «[Failure] Well, look somewhere else, because I'm not in the habit of arming others with anything that could kill me, got it?» [K2/Mira]
- «All right, all right. Keep your robes on. Here you go.» [K2/Mira]
- «More grenades? All right.» [K2/Mira]
- «Not right now - you've got enough. I don't want you to go off like a thermal detonator if you trip around me.» [K2/Mira]
- «Go ahead and ask.» [K2/Mira]
- «It's all this traveling. Being trapped in this ship. Everything's... everything's just too quiet.» [K2/Mira]
- «I'd rather be doing something. Somewhere with people, activity... some life.» [K2/Mira]
- «Yeah. It's been a while since I've been off-planet, I guess I got used to it.» [K2/Mira]
- «Feels better to be back here, not cooped up on that ship.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well... Nar Shaddaa may be one of the biggest cesspits in the galaxy, but... It's got a life to it, activity. Aliens, people, refugees... it's like noise, but relaxing. Like the hum of a hyperdrive.» [K2/Mira]
- «No arguments here. Still, as long as you accept it, the place isn't so bad. Better than sitting here... in silence.» [K2/Mira]
- «No arguments here. Still, once you get used to it, the place isn't so bad.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well, it's not how most people see it. You kind of have to let the place grow on you... like a fungus.» [K2/Mira]
- «Yeah? Hm. Didn't think I'd hear a Jedi ever refer to Nar Shaddaa like that.» [K2/Mira]
- «Yeah, right. Save the philosophy for some Padawan.» [K2/Mira]
- «Yeah, right. Save the philosophy for the Disciple - he'd eat that stuff up.» [K2/Mira]
- «Yeah, well, I wouldn't go that far. I'll believe it when I see it.» [K2/Mira]
- «Yeah, more or less. I wasn't born there, just ended up there.» [K2/Mira]
- «[Influence: Failure] Look, before we get into a game of guess-the-pazaak-card, pull back on the throttle. I don't know you that well to start sharing our life stories.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well, the war happened, the first one, against the Mandalorians. Had family right up until the end. It's not really a new story - you hear it all over the galaxy. It's what happens after the wars are over that you don't hear much about.» [K2/Mira]
- «I think so. After Revan crushed the Mandalorians, planets throughout the Republic were flooded with refugees. I was just one of the others. Me, I got passage to Nar Shaddaa. From there... not much you can do, so I became a bounty hunter.» [K2/Mira]
- «Take a guess, "Jedi." Only two groups of people would have lost family at Malachor - and Jedi don't have families.» [K2/Mira]
- «As much as any slave becomes a Mandalorian. They took prisoners on every world they conquered to bolster their ranks - and they took a lot of worlds.» [K2/Mira]
- «I don't know, it didn't matter. I was too young to remember, really. Besides, Nar Shaddaa's kind of grown on me. It's as much my home as any place else.» [K2/Mira]
- «When I was young, yeah. They mostly used me to carry ammo packs and munitions. Toward the end of the war, they needed everyone they could get. They taught me to fight, to hunt, to survive. I was part of their squad, even when I was young. Everyone served as part of the unit, and I felt like I had a place there. After Malachor, it didn't really matter any more - the Mandalorians lost. Bad. But you know that.» [K2/Mira]
- «Yeah, I know. I saw the worlds they left behind them during the war. That kind of stays with you - I haven't forgotten it. What happened at Malachor - they, they probably deserved it.» [K2/Mira]
- «Should I be? Maybe I should ask you if you're happy about all the Jedi who died on Malachor V. Maybe it felt like you lost family there. But I doubt it.» [K2/Mira]
- «I'm good at finding people. So I used it to make credits.» [K2/Mira]
- «Not if you know your target. Usually you get a holo or rough description, and then you just sort of listen to it, get a feel for him or her. Then start walking.» [K2/Mira]
- «This is a little hard to explain, but Nar Shaddaa's got a flow, a life to it. You see all kinds of aliens and life there, and it's got currents.» [K2/Mira]
- «If you know your target, you can feel them, know where they're going to go. And sometimes, you know where they're going to be before they do. I'm good at finding people. Because when they're lost or out-of-place... it's like something's wrong, inside them. And that's why I hunt.» [K2/Mira]
- «Not unless I have to. And bounty hunters... the good ones... don't ever need to kill a target.» [K2/Mira]
- «Oh, really, is that it? How you could ever possibly hope to understand is beyond me. Jedi don't have family. I know what happened at Malachor V, and I know the Jedi didn't care about life there.» [K2/Mira]
- «Get away from me. The next time you come and ask me a question, I swear I'll shoot you in the head and dump you out the airlock.» [K2/Mira]
- «Whatever, don't worry about it - it's just a sore subject with me.» [K2/Mira]
- «Whatever - but you were the one asking all the questions. You deserved it.» [K2/Mira]
- «Yeah, well, they're dead. That's how that story ends.» [K2/Mira]
- «But not everybody's story has to end with losing their family or their loved ones. And not all the bounty hunting I do is for criminals or killers.» [K2/Mira]
- «There's a lot of lost people out there, scattered ever since the Mandalorian Wars. Sometimes... it's like you can almost hear them, like an echo, calling out for each other. And maybe, just maybe, by finding them, I can start putting the galaxy back together.» [K2/Mira]
- «That's what I think sometimes. It all seems so hopeless - there's too much damage, inside and out. It's all you can do just to survive.» [K2/Mira]
- «That's what I had wondered. Maybe surviving was what was important. Maybe I would have been someone different... someone weaker.» [K2/Mira]
- «Hanharr kept calling me prey. Maybe surviving isn't enough... you need to be strong, or you become one of those people who lose themselves.» [K2/Mira]
- «Maybe. We'll see. I don't even know why I'm telling you this. But you're not getting anything else out of me.» [K2/Mira]
- «Hanharr's only a bounty hunter because that's the closest word for what he does - he's not out for credits. It's more vicious than that, and it runs a lot deeper. It's like he's out to make the whole galaxy suffer - every living thing in it. He wants to break them, ruin them, and when they can't suffer any more, he wants them dead.» [K2/Mira]
- «I didn't kill him once. Biggest mistake ever.» [K2/Mira]
- «[Influence: Success] Do you really want to hear this?» [K2/Mira]
- «Are you sure you what [sic] to hear this again?» [K2/Mira]
- «[Influence: Failure] It's a long story, and I don't want to get into it. Maybe some other time.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well, Hanharr and me go way back - in the worst possible way. He's from some forest planet on the Outer Rim where Czerka had set up one of their slaving operations.» [K2/Mira]
- «I don't remember the name - something with too many 'k's and 'y's. Sounds like you're gargling ronto spit when you say it.» [K2/Mira]
- «No idea - he's just 'Hanharr.' I hope there aren't any more like him. I get the impression he's not a good representative of his people, though - he's like a mad kath hound among rontos. Some of Vogga the Hutt's men said Hanharr killed his own tribe, but those two-cred thugs lie every time they open their mouths, so who knows.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well, not for long. Once off-planet, Hanharr escaped from the Czerka slavers, then killed them all.» [K2/Mira]
- «I don't know. I always thought he just liked using them as weapons.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well, before you get too proud of him, Hanharr figured Czerka had the right idea.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well, before you get too teary-eyed, Hanharr figured Czerka had the right idea.» [K2/Mira]
- «I don't think he understood the concept of slavery before, at least on the scale that Czerka practiced it. But now he did. You ever hear of Dersonn III? Or the Iti Cluster colonies?» [K2/Mira]
- «Right, that's because Hanharr happened. He makes what happened to his homeworld look like an exercise in community building. He's not a bounty hunter. He's a slaver... a predator. It's like he's out to enslave or kill every human in the galaxy, like he's trying to settle some huge score or debt. I don't get it. But he's dangerous.» [K2/Mira]
- «Anyone who paid credits - and sometimes he just hunted humans for sport. The ones who survived he sold to the Exchange, to the Hutts, to anyone who'd buy bodies... living or dead. He and Vogga used to do big credit transactions - that Hutt really liked the look of unwrinkled humans for some reason. Didn't make him too popular with the other Hutts, let me tell you.» [K2/Mira]
- «I was prey. And not only did I escape, but I saved his life while doing it. He's been hunting me ever since.» [K2/Mira]
- «I don't pretend to understand it, but among his people, they have these codes of honor. But somewhere along the line, Hanharr's got twisted. His people form these things called 'life debts.' If you save the life of one of them, they pledge themselves to you.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well, with Hanharr... he can't escape that life debt, it's bred into him. But he hates every other living thing in the galaxy... so pledging himself to someone else, especially a human, was unbearable. So when I saved his life, it was the worst thing I could do - it was like slavery all over again, but it was in his head. It was like it pushed him over the edge.» [K2/Mira]
- «A life debt to Hanharr is a death sentence; he'll hunt you until you're dead. When I saved his life, it meant he had to kill me. And so he kept chasing me in hopes I would die. I think the fact I showed him mercy after hating humans for so long, that was something he couldn't stand.» [K2/Mira]
- «Tell me about it. Like I said, I get the impression a life debt's supposed to be a gift, but to Hanharr it's more like a curse, to both people involved.» [K2/Mira]
- «Probably. But if he had multiple life debts, especially to humans, Hanharr would probably go mad. He was angry before, sure, but he'd be ten times worse if that happened.» [K2/Mira]
- «Hanharr's tough. Really tough. And when he loses it, it's like nothing can stop him. I've seen him shrug off blaster bolts, Bothan stunners, and even survive a freighter crash on Dersonn III. He keeps coming.» [K2/Mira]
- «Oh, I'm glad he's gone... it's like a weight off my shoulders. I don't have to keep watching my back every minute, wondering when he's going to show up. And he always did. It's like he always knew where I was.» [K2/Mira]
- «Trust me, if he was still alive, he'd be chasing us even now, waiting to ambush us when we least expect it. And he always shows up at the worst possible time - he was one of the best bounty hunters on Nar Shaddaa. Hanharr never gives up on his prey... or his life debts. He's a hunter, he's a natural predator.» [K2/Mira]
- «[Influence: Success] Well, as happens on Nar Shaddaa, I made someone mad. Mad enough for them to send Hanharr after me.» [K2/Mira]
- «[Influence: Failure] You know, it's a long story, and I'm kind of tired of talking about it right now - maybe some other time. It's not much to hear anyway.» [K2/Mira]
- «Turns out, they were even able to get him cheap - he'd heard about me, and wanted to hunt me down. For sport. He didn't think I'd be much of a challenge.» [K2/Mira]
- «Well, he tried to box me down in vents beneath the Nar Shaddaa docks, and he'd set one too many proximity mines to cover the escape routes.» [K2/Mira]
- «I think he'd hoped to drive me into the mines and let them do the work - or that I'd be too scared to try and walk through them. Thing is, I knew Hanharr's supplier - and the trigger signatures for the mines. It was pretty easy to broadcast a signal to blind their sensor receptors for a minute or two. I figured that would buy me enough time to move through them and get away.» [K2/Mira]
- «No, it isn't. I spent most of my childhood hauling mines and munitions. I got to know my way around them; if I hadn't, I wouldn't be here right now.» [K2/Mira]
- «Like I said, I disarmed the trigger fuses for enough of the mines to get by - temporarily. Hanharr was pretty fast on my trail. I'd just made it to safety when he hit the first one. The blast leveled the entire ventilation section... and Hanharr was caught right in the middle. And he survived. Barely.» [K2/Mira]
- «He was crawling around, blinded from the flash and the plasma burns - it had happened so fast, all the blood had been scabbed and crusted from the flash. I had the drop on him, and even blind, he knew it. He could still hear me. My ears were ringing from the blast, but I could hear him... I think he was begging me to let him live. His voice... it- it wasn't a roar, more like an echo of it. I suppose I should have killed him, but I couldn't do it... he was in pain, and he was helpless. So I dragged him out of there... enough to get him to safety. And he kept hunting me ever since. He said he'd pursue me to the edge of the galaxy, no matter where I ran, he would find me - and break me. That I would always be prey.» [K2/Mira]
- «Maybe... I've thought about that moment. A lot. And wondered if I would do things differently if I had another chance.» [K2/Mira]
- «I've killed people before. But not if I don't have to.» [K2/Mira]
- «I know. It's different... I don't know why.» [K2/Mira]
- «No, I'm fine. Look, I don't really want to talk about it, okay? The sooner this trip is over, the better.» [K2/Mira]
- «I don't know. I... I haven't killed anyone for a long time. But when I'm around you, suddenly it's like I've always been doing it. It's like a reflex. I don't like it. And I don't know when - or why - it became so easy.» [K2/Mira]
- «[Influence: Success] Maybe one day I'll let you.» [K2/Mira]
- «[Influence: Success] All right, prove it. You can show me now, if you want.» [K2/Mira]
- «[Influence: Failure] No thanks... you can keep your Jedi training to yourself. I already know Nar Shaddaa better than you ever will.» [K2/Mira]
- «All right. But I doubt you're going to show me anything I don't already know. And when you show me, don't act like a tourist. It attracts predators.» [K2/Mira]
- «What's wrong? Why are you stopping?» [K2/Mira]
- «I've been by here hundreds of times. There's nothing special about it.» [K2/Mira]
- «I don't believe in the Force. It's Jedi tricks, sleight-of-hand.» [K2/Mira]
- «This I'd like to see. It's not going to hurt, is it?» [K2/Mira]
- «All right. It's not going to hurt, is it?» [K2/Mira]
- «::Mira closes her eyes::» [K2/Mira]
- «I can feel this... planet... I can't shut it out. It's louder now... it hurts. All these people...» [K2/Mira]
- «That's what I want. I'm sure of it, more than anything.» [K2/Mira]
- «But if... if I become a Jedi, I'll have to turn myself in for the credits.» [K2/Mira]
- «Are you going to train me?» [K2/Mira]
- «I've endured worse. I can deal with this. Can you train me to do what you do, with the Force?» [K2/Mira]
- «I want to become like you. I want to be strong, and I don't want to be afraid or alone any more. I don't want to keep running, and looking, and never feel like I'm finding what I'm looking for. I'm tired of being hunted.» [K2/Mira]
- «I don't want to be lost any more. When the galaxy or anyone takes something from me, I want the power to take it back... and hurt them.» [K2/Mira]
- «I am ready. I shall not fail you.» [K2/Mira]
- «I have to tell you - what you showed me on Nar Shaddaa... it's changed my view of things, on the galaxy. For the first time, I feel in control. I feel... powerful.» [K2/Mira]
- «It's strange, but it feels right somehow, like a piece inside me just clicked into place.» [K2/Mira] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I want to become like you. I want to be strong! I don't want to be afraid or alone any more. I... I... I don't want to keep running, and looking, and never feel like I'm finding what I'm looking for. I'm tired of being hunted.» [K2/Mira]
- «When the galaxy takes something from me, I want the power to let go... and I want the power to heal the echo when it's gone.» [K2/Mira]
- «That sounds all right from where I'm standing.» [K2/Mira]
- «All right - I think I'm ready to try, then.» [K2/Mira]
- «I... wanted to say thanks. For what you showed me on Nar Shaddaa. It's going to take some time to let it sink in, but... But thanks. I feel... alive.» [K2/Mira]
- «Talk? About what?» [K2/Mira]
- «Sure, they're easy. That's why I dress like this. When they're looking down to check you out, you can usually smash them on the base of the skull, or deliver an uppercut that knocks them flat.» [K2/Mira]
- «It's simple - when you want a man, you jab him with a Bothan Stunner, then while he's screaming in pain, slap some stun cuffs on him. Then starve him for two or three days until he becomes open to suggestion, then double-check his bounty and see if he's worth anything.» [K2/Mira]
- «Why, you trying to be my mother? No thanks, already had one. Somewhere.» [K2/Mira]
- «No thanks - I didn't need friends on Nar Shaddaa, and I don't need them now.» [K2/Mira]
- «Why, you trying to be my father? No thanks, already had one. Somewhere.» [K2/Mira]
- «Are you sure you're a Jedi? That exile of yours must have gone on longer than I thought. Look, you're way too old for me. Even if I were interested, you couldn't handle me anyway. I mean, you're really good in a fight. And you've got those intense eyes, and it's obvious you take care of your body, but I really don't have the luxury of getting attached to you.» [K2/Mira]
- «Besides, you already have your little admirer. I don't want to be number two, you know?» [K2/Mira]
- «Besides, you already have your little entourage. I don't want to be a part of the pack, you know?» [K2/Mira]
- «Look, if we start sharing a bunk, the Handmaiden would get upset, and then I'd have to kick the hell out of her just to show her who the pack leader is around here. No thanks.» [K2/Mira]
- «Look, if we start sharing a bunk, the other girls would get upset, and then I'd have to kick the hell out of them to show them who's the pack leader around here. No thanks.» [K2/Mira]
- «You're sweet, old guy. But let's keep it professional.» [K2/Mira]
- «It's more than age. I thought I'd seen a lot, but you look like you're a hundred inside.» [K2/Mira]
- «Run away? You're the one who couldn't handle me - I'd kill you.» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Onderon]**
- «If they decide to do a cargo search, we're going to be in trouble.» [K2/Onderon]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- «And what did she want?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It's okay - I already know she's a Jedi.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Really? Interesting.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Mm - trust me, Nar Shaddaa just got complicated.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- «Would you like a hand with that?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «You got it. It should be all set.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «All right. Just trying to help.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «Hey, watch the language. I was just trying to help.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- «About time you showed up. We finished our part and have been sitting on our hands waiting for you. So, what happened?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Huh. We'll see how long that lasts.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Hm. We'll see if Onderon's newfound 'peace' lasts as long as your tremendous ego.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Ugh. Jedi and their blasted secrets.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- «Apathy is death.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- «When someone goes through this much trouble to seal a door, it usually means there's something important on the other side.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «I think the body was left here as a message to us.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Sith_Tomb]**
- «I've got a bad feeling about this place. Sith and ancient tombs are never a good combination.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- «Something feels... wrong here. I don't think you should get any closer to it. I'm not a Jedi, but even I can sense the dark side here. This... thing... has to be part of the reason why the Sith are so interested in this place.» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I won't tell you how to do your job. But I know that I wouldn't get near it.» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Uh... right. Go ahead then.» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «That's the first smart thing I've heard today. Let's leave it alone then, before you change your mind.» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I think all the soldiers and beasts are trying to keep us out of that door. Must be something important. I don't suppose I could talk you out of getting in there? I thought not. The door is shut tight. Somewhere down here there's got to be a way to open it.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]

**[K2/Surface]**
- «Well, here's a surprise; yet another slice of galactic paradise. Why we can't ever go to Alderaan or Ithor or some place without metal, jagged rocks, or packs of bloodthirsty beasts?» [K2/Surface]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «Please tell me we aren't going in there.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «The hairs on the back of my neck are standing up. Are you sure we need to go in there?» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K2/Western_Square]**
- «Back off, wormhead, this bounty's mine. Don't do something you might regret. There's soldiers all over this place.» [K2/Western_Square]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Mira (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Bao-Dur]**
- Bao-Dur: «Claws out today, aren't they? You've been spending too much time with Mira.» [K2/Bao-Dur]

**[K2/Depths]**
- Hanharr: «The gray-maned female brought me here to hunt. To hunt you. You have always been prey, Mira. Weak, scared, always running. Hesitant to kill.» [K2/Depths]
- Hanharr: «You are weak, a child of the Hutt's moon, frightened, forever fearing the dark. You shall always be prey, Mira. If you do not kill me now, I shall hunt you through the paths of your mind until you die.» [K2/Depths]

**[K2/Docks]**
- Vogga: «Yes, yes... and that is why Czerka found you such easy prey. I care nothing for the accomplishments of primates on some backwater world, Hanharr - I care only for your accomplishments here, upon the smuggler's moon. But if you cannot perform the task, then perhaps the human female will serve - Mira, is it? Even nauseatingly thin and shapely and free of wrinkles, she is said to have a dancer's grace - and to be a better hunter than you. She refuses to kill, but I imagine with enough credits, I could persuade her otherwise, especially considering what a disappointment you have proven to be.» [K2/Docks]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- Twi'lek Domo: «[[../Mira» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Twi'lek Domo: «Mira? Ah, so that is her name. Absolutely - this vision of beauty before me would be more than acceptable for my Master.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Hanharr]**
- Hanharr: «No, one day you will die as Mira did. It is different. I shall be there at the moment when your life dies. I will watch it go from your body - fleeing, for somewhere else.» [K2/Hanharr]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]**
- Visquis: «Trapped? No, Hanharr... as you know, many vents and shafts run beneath the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. You chased Mira through them once, if I recall.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- Visquis: «Ah, Mira. I am pleased that my traps did not cause any permanent scarring.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Visquis: «But in the meantime, as boss of this sector, I must pronounce judgments on those who have crossed me - and reward those who serve me. Mira, I believe that you know this one - as he knows you. Hanharr, I have heard tales of how you have ripped humans in half. Indulge me. I have left her armed - I do not want her to die too quickly.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «Where... where can I find Mira?» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Mira]**
- Atton: «I heard you making friends with the bounty hunter. I don't blame you. She's a scrapper. You don't survive on Nar Shaddaa for very long if you aren't.» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Vossk: «I am Vossk, once of the bounty hunter's guild, now no longer. You have nothing to fear from me.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Vossk: «Yes, there were oaths to the bounty hunter's creed, honored in blood. There were laws to hunts and bounties, once upheld, now no longer. This is not known to many, but the bounty hunter's creed has been infected. Laws are fragile, broken easily.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Vossk: «There is a series of hunter droids, that are new to the moon... and there is always Hanharr... and Mira. They will hunt each other until the sun of this system dies, and beyond.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Vossk: «Mira, once a slave captured by Hanharr, brought to the smuggler's moon, but she escaped. She is still a bounty hunter, perhaps one of the best I have ever seen.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Western_Square]**
- Twi'lek Bounty Hunter: «Ah, Mira. I thought you were still collecting two credit bounties on Nar Shaddaa. Such a shame to die here, so far from your home... and with a wanted criminal.» [K2/Western_Square]

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to Mira (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- Mandalore: «Watch out for Azkul. I've known his kind before. He will attack you without hesitation if he believes you aren't working with him.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Handmaiden: «I hope you aren't intending to support that monster. They will slaughter the citizens of Khoonda!» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Depths]**
- Hanharr: «Here, on this graveyard planet, you have nowhere left to run. The eyes of Nar Shaddaa do not look here, and what happens here shall only be between you and I. Run, prey. Or fight me, here. I can feel the rage of this planet pouring through me. Yet it is nothing compared to the hate I have for you.» [K2/Depths]

**[K2/Docks]**
- Hanharr: «You are my prey, female. Always.» [K2/Docks]
- Hanharr: «Maybe seeing your bones break, seeing you in pain will be all I need to see before I die, then the life debt over, and I can be at peace.» [K2/Docks]
- Hanharr: «Warn me? Of what? Warn me you trick me, warn me you violate life debt? Little human girl, I could crush you with one hand, end lifetime of misery.» [K2/Docks]
- Hanharr: «Then little girl should not have made mistake so long ago. On Kashyyyk, females who make mistakes die. Hanharr smells your fear, even through the stink of the moon.» [K2/Docks]
- Hanharr: «Are you are finally ready to die? I do not think so... your eyes show you are still filled with fear.» [K2/Docks]
- Hanharr: «It is not the time for you to enter the Shadowlands. When it is, I will be there, my claws at your throat, and I will stare into your eyes as you die.» [K2/Docks]

**[K2/Droid_Warehouse]**
- Hanharr: «If you are hunting Goto, you will fail - you are chasing, ghosts, sh- I... will accompany you. Help you.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Atton: «Yeah, right - you're the one who wanted to sell her to Goto in the first place.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «I will not rest until your Jeedai is found... upon this, I swear.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- Disciple: «If you would have me, I can apply my knowledge and skills to helping you find the answers you seek.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- Twi'lek Domo: «Oh, this is wonderful news. Vogga will be most pleased to see you.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Yes yes, of course. Let me just get you to put this on and dance a little, so I can be sure.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- Hanharr: «...and I will take you there.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Atton: «Yeah, well you'll understand if I hold back the tears.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- G0-T0: «If you thought to escape my notice so easily, you would be wrong. As a token of my goodwill, I present to you a gift: this droid. It will serve you well on your journey.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Visas: «Like an echo. Your words are well-chosen, sister of the Echani. I will not harm him. But it is not me you should fear. There are darker things in the galaxy than a blind one such as I.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «Something troubles you, servant of Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Influence]**
- Visas: «I fear that will be your undoing.» [K2/Influence]
- Handmaiden: «Perhaps the stories Atris told of you were untrue.» [K2/Influence]
- Handmaiden: «After all this time, you still follow the Jedi Code.» [K2/Influence]
- Handmaiden: «Your acts are inspiring - there are ways to do good beyond battle and victories.» [K2/Influence]
- Mandalore: «I wouldn't try that with a Mandalorian - they're likely to kill you for helping them.» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «I am not speaking to you, slave. Be silent!» [K2/Influence]
- Bao-Dur: «As always, General, when you lead, I shall follow. But it is only because we have been through so much together.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- G0-T0: «We must schedule a time for you to teach me how to use such a power. I would, of course, compensate you for such instruction.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]**
- Hanharr: «You!» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Visquis: «Restrain yourself, Hanharr. There is no need for violence. I gather from your conversation that this is not the Jedi I invited here - it is difficult for me to tell, you humans all look so alike to me. Perhaps you would care to explain what I owe the honor of this visit? And where I might find the Jedi?» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Visquis: «Ah, what a prime example of human arrogance. What you know is substantially less than what I know. Otherwise, you would not have come. One, everything that goes on in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr is invisible to Goto. It is something I discovered by accident, and have tested many times since to insure accuracy. Second, I am not acting on Goto's orders - not anymore. Hanharr and I reached an agreement to deal with Goto altogether... and collect a tidy sum from Vogga.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Visquis: «He might, it is possible. But it is a risk I am willing to take. You see, Vogga is very tired of having his freighters hijacked by Goto - it is causing his word to have less... weight on Nal Hutta. There is a leak in his operations here on Nar Shaddaa. Rather than simply find the leak and eliminate it, his anger has compelled him to eliminate the source of the irritation altogether. Unfortunately, Goto is very careful about showing himself to others - Vogga's employee, Hanharr, discovered this when he attended the gathering on Goto's yacht. It is always holograms with Goto – always. But I have a suspicion that Goto will reveal himself to a Jedi. When that happens, that is when I intend to strike. Unfortunately, it is clear he will not reveal himself to a two-credit bounty hunter with the audacity to try and strike a deal with me. So please - tell me where the Jedi is. I am not in the mood for negotiation.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**

## Hanharr
*Corpus coverage: 260 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 54 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 13 pronoun references resolved from context · 40 lines addressed to them · 28 narration lines · 8 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Hanharr is a Wookiee exile, enslaved by Czerka, who owes a life debt to Mira [K2/Hanharr].
### Personality and motivations
Murderous hatred: «why they must all die» [K2/Hanharr].
### Arc (scene by scene)
- Kreia empowers him.
- **Dark path:** joins.
- **Depths:** fight.
### Relationships
- Mira.
- Kreia.
### Abilities
- Brute strength.
### Feats
- Killed Goto (dark path, his claim) [K2/Goto's_Yacht].
### Power-scaling statements
- «I could crush you with one hand» [K2/Docks]
### Duels and fights
- vs Mira.
### Verdict implications
- Peak physical brute.
### Open questions
- None.

- [K2/Party] Hanharr: Class Scout, Level 4. Feats: Improved Power Attack, Precise Shot I, Targeting I, Uncanny Dodge I, Wookiee Toughness I.
