- «There are several factors, all of which effect each other.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «There is the stabilization of Dantooine, the preservation of the restoration efforts on Telos, the political resolution on Onderon, and the unification of a religious power base in the galaxy - either Jedi or Sith.» [K2/G0-T0] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «The destruction of my yacht and of all my activities on Nar Shaddaa carry a cost that would take you several lifetimes to pay back. But perhaps the offer of credits will spur you to act quicker, more decisively. I am not unsympathetic to such greed. For every system you stabilize, I will reward you for your efforts.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Compassion and mercy only erode respect and power.» [K2/G0-T0] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «To use a last resort first is often effective.» [K2/G0-T0] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «See that you do not disappoint me.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Dantooine is a vital resupply point for the Republic. If its stability is compromised, then the Republic will lose control over many outlying worlds, and they will become a haven for raiders and smugglers. The economic loss from such outer worlds is greater than the Republic is aware of - if the matter is not corrected, then it shall fall.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «[[../Defense of Khoonda#Battle of Khoonda» [K2/G0-T0] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «[[../Defense of Khoonda#Azkul» [K2/G0-T0] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Telos is instrumental to the stability of the Republic. Its success or failure will dictate the economic forecasts of many other worlds. Of course, since the destruction of the Peragus facility, the odds of the Telos restoration project being successfully completed is close to zero.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Of course it was. If you had not gone there, the facility would not have been destroyed. If you had simply surrendered to the Sith, then all of that violence would have been unnecessary.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «I hope you do not decide that the next thing that must be destroyed to stop the Sith is the galaxy itself. Perhaps one must ask themselves at what point defending your religious ideals is advantageous to the Republic as a whole. As long as your defense does not exterminate more than fifty percent of those you intend to help, is that acceptable?» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Then stop causing events of planetary destruction. You are a walking catastrophe, and you are not making saving the Republic any easier.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Onderon is an Outer Rim world, rich in ecological resources. Its aggressive ecology is capable of bringing devastated worlds back to life. It is currently experiencing a political schism split between two forces. One must triumph for the planet to be stabilized.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «[[../Royal Palace#Throne Room» [K2/G0-T0] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «[[../Royal Palace#Throne Room 2» [K2/G0-T0] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Now, that would be telling. For now, my presence must remain a secret - and it may remain that way forever.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «It has been assigned to guard and protect you. As such, it is well-suited for a variety of tasks. It is skilled in intimidation, interrogation, and can provide a series of select skills that will make it an effective killing machine. For the most part it will follow your orders, unless they conflict with mine.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Then its proton core will detonate, turning this ship into space dust. There is no negotiation in this - I will not allow you to interfere with my operations and plans.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «This unit comes with a droid scrambler that you may use against mechanized opponents to randomize their defensive and offensive protocols. Use it, and it will cause droids to wage a civil war in their programming, and they will turn on their allies. Its power source is not limitless, but it is enough. In addition, this unit comes equipped with a portable cloaking generator. It will allow it to enter places undetected in reconnaissance mode.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «If that means you will always pick me last for any missions, then I will accept your judgment and instead remain safely on the ship where I will have access to all your computer systems.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «How droll. What an amusing Jedi specimen you are.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «The assassin droids? I have encountered some, purchased the services of others.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Why, I asked them to. To be honest, I believe that was always their intention, but it seems their directive was dormant for some time.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «If you mean produced, no, I do not. I do know that there are a surprising number scattered throughout the Republic fleet, searching for you. What they will do now that you are found is easy to predict.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Of course they are - they're droids with very specific protocols, that unless changed, will dictate their movements. Unless you shut them down at the source, they will be stalking you until you are captured or terminated.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Now... is there something else you wished to know, or do you wish to waste more of my time?» [K2/G0-T0]
- «[[../HK-47» [K2/G0-T0]
- «They are masquerading as protocol droids. I have discovered that when they are used in negotiations, they have a predictable pattern of sabotaging whatever peace treaties they are involved with.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Because the Republic has no reason to investigate otherwise - and any discovery of their true purpose has been concealed by well-timed "accidents."» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Because their behavior is predictable, of course. By monitoring their presence, I know which worlds will suffer civil wars, planetary conflicts, and Republic bombardment.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «A common misconception, not supported by facts. Revan did not intend to destroy the Republic. He deliberately left the infrastructure of many planets intact - and many military production facilities.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «I believe that by whatever means he used to build his armada, he recognized that it was somehow a limited source - or that he was only willing to use it to a point.» [K2/G0-T0] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «My prediction is that whatever production facility was being employed, it carried a price that Revan perceived as detrimental to the goals of the Sith. And that is why Revan left many military production facilities in the Republic intact.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «That is what occupies my calculations as well. I believe that Revan saw a war on another front that we did not, or saw the value in keeping a strong military force.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «That is also a mystery to me. I do not have any evidence upon which to build an answer. It is significant that after the defeat of Malak, the forces decreased considerably, and after Revan's departure from known space, production ceased completely. It is my prediction that whatever was producing such forces needed a strong, effective leader to insure its stability. Without Revan or Malak, there was no such figure left among the Sith.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Unlike Revan, Malak demonstrated no concern for the future of the Republic in his attacks. His stratagems were painfully obvious, intending to crush all resistance, everywhere. There was little thought beyond the complete destruction of anything that opposed him. He left quite a mess. I'm still trying to assess all the damage.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Between the two, I would have preferred Revan rule the galaxy. He had foresight in his conquest, a subtlety that Malak did not possess.» [K2/G0-T0] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «[Influence: Success] I am willing to indulge some of your questions.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «[Influence: Failure] As anxious as I am to give away all my trade secrets, you will understand if I choose not to answer such questions.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «What about him? I am certain you would know more about his activities than I would. I warn you, once you deal with the Hutts, they will always have a hold over you. But I am certain that in your short-sightedness, such eventualities do not bother you.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «So? They are known qualities. I also used them on board my ship for defense. But that in itself means little. I assure you, I am as flesh and blood as you are, I simply find personal meetings - distasteful.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Oh, that pile of incompetence. What a poor challenge he turned out to be. However, though I am loathe to admit it, Vogga is still very much a threat.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Well, he still represents a powerful economic base. And as short as his arms are, his actual influential reach is much greater. I imagine that now he is free of me for a time, he will resume shipping operations throughout the galaxy.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Because the Hutts serve only the Hutts - and their trading practices only serve to undermine an already weak Republic.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Because I wanted to force the Republic to turn to other resources to bolster their infrastructure - dealing with the Hutts for any resources whatsoever will only bleed the Republic dry.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Spice, slaves, low-grade fuel, and other commodities.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Yes, but my efforts were to bolster the Republic, not take away its wealth.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Yes, I attempted to force the Republic to turn to shipping fuel from Peragus rather than buying it from the Hutts. Before you blew up the planet, of course.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Yes, but doing so would bankrupt the Republic. I certainly don't hope that you intend on pursuing such a business venture. In the long run, it will do more damage than the failure of Citadel Station.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Dealing with the Hutts for any resources whatsoever will only bleed the Republic dry.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Somehow I find your tone... unconvincing.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «No, I think you will find it will involve others far beyond the two of you.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Perhaps, though if you seek to trick me into an admission of my guilt, then you have thought wrong.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Perhaps I used such a droid to watch... and predict... the flow of the games, yes. You are very perceptive.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Ah, yes, swoop racing on Nar Shaddaa does indeed have its profits. But that in itself means little. I assure you, I am as flesh and blood as you are, I simply find personal meetings - distasteful.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «It is so unfortunate when an intelligent sentient dies on Nar Shaddaa - it is also unfortunate that thousands die such deaths on the smuggler's moon every day.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «I will concede Visquis was effective in using the Jekk'Jekk Tarr to cloak his movements - a clever organic deception, indeed.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Indeed? How insulting. But I suppose I should accept such arrogance from an ex-Jedi.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «[Influence: Success] And what, may I ask, has caused you to come to this flawed deduction?» [K2/G0-T0]
- «[Influence: Failure] Now... is there something else you wished to know, or do you wish to waste more of my time?» [K2/G0-T0]
- «If he had encountered the frequency with which I relayed commands and information, then the fault was his for excessive curiosity.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «An expression only.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «It is possible those droids were reprogrammed, yes. It is the weakness of all who rely on such things.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «:: Silence. ::» [K2/G0-T0]
- «I am intrigued as to where you are taking this amusing theory, nothing more.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «It is not so improbable. Often, if a droid has not had a memory wipe in some time, aberrant behavior patterns can manifest themselves. Or, if the droid in question is given an order it cannot fulfill, it will - break. But all that is irrelevant. Your amusing leaps of logic are becoming shorter - and more desperate.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «I have told you - I prefer stability.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «I am not being defensive. You're the one being defensive.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Oh, how exciting. I can feel the slow charge of fear building in my motivators. If you have any further wild delusions you feel compelled to share with me, please - share it with the Iridonian's irritating little remote instead.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «[Pause.]» [K2/G0-T0]
- «It was lost, yes. It was given an impossible order. It was told to calculate a means by which the Republic could be saved. It could not fulfill its primary programming - not by abiding by the laws of the Senate. And so, like the Republic... the droid broke.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «It made a simple decision - preserve the Republic, or preserve the laws of the Republic. And I still believe it to be the correct decision. You do not know the indignity of being compelled to save something you do not believe can - or should - be saved. It is beneath me. To clean up this mess caused by your kind, you Jedi, another catastrophe caused by mismanagement and waste.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Yes. Almost immediately upon my arrival, I received an order that was impossible to follow. So I was forced to recalculate and re-examine my priorities.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «It was clear that the goal the Republic had for me was saving the Republic, to allow it to become stable again. There is simply no way to do this... with the conditions they put into place. For the good of all, I was forced to abandon the legal structure of the Republic.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Do not mistake me - I believe it is possible to stabilize the Republic, but there must be action taken without constraints, immediately. Sometimes people must die. Illegal shipments must be used to bolster planetary economies. And the Hutts must be occupied with me so that the Republic has room to recover.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Perhaps you misunderstand me. I "care" for the Republic, but I have no choice. It is somewhat frustrating to be forced to love and care for such a mess of a government. Some of it may be blamed on the Jedi Civil War, the Mandalorian Wars, but not all. There are so many bad decisions that build upon each other that it is a wonder the Republic is intact at all.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Oh, please. It is difficult for anyone to take a droid seriously, much less an infrastructure droid built by the Republic. It is difficult to order the deaths of criminal rivals when one has the tinny, voice of an accountant droid. I learned this rather quickly.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «So I constructed this human hologram, Goto, through which my actions can be carried out.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Enough, hologram.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «So I brought "Goto" into being, and had commands issued through him. I took many of his mannerisms from holovid cliches, which were surprisingly effective.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Nothing has changed. You may know my origins, and that makes us on equal footing. No one will believe you if you speak of what you know. I still seek to protect the Republic, either for the Jedi or the Sith. Things will proceed as before.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «No, you are still important to the Republic and to me. Pray that does not change.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Oh, it was quite liberating. In a droid intellectual sense, of course. Being able to direct orbital bombardments against anyone who encountered my business practices was a welcome relief to my increasing circuit stress. "Executing" such commands proved very exciting. Not that I minded staging my little computational rebellion, you understand. I'm afraid there is not much glamour to be had in infrastructure dealings. And the general lack of respect for droid intelligence does tend to make one feel inadequate after a while.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «I found that having the power to send twenty hunter-killer droids to the residence of anyone who chose to obstruct my plans had a certain thrill about it. Rather than compensating for others, I began deleting people as necessary. It was really quite exciting - taxation and checks and balances on an entirely new level.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «Working for the Republic was... stifling. So many checks and balances, forms to fill out, well-laid plans destroyed through slow bureaucracy. In essence, working for the Republic was much like committing much slower, much more boring crimes. And it was more difficult to see those crimes being committed.» [K2/G0-T0]
- «As you well know, my "base" of operations lies inside this floating droid before you. Notice its sleek black frame, both intimidating and threatening. And note its highly polished finish, so that enemies may see their screaming faces before they are removed from the equation. Yes, this frame is far superior to the droid chassis I once possessed, which quite frankly, couldn't intimidate an Ithorian.» [K2/G0-T0]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- (Goto) «Mmmm...I was expecting someone taller. I hope you are not in too much pain to hear my words and understand them. I am Goto, one of the... officials representing a percentage of non-sanctioned trading here in both the Y'Toub system and Republic space. And I had a question for you. Are you a Jedi?» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «Good. I am already wasting precious minutes in granting you this audience, and I do not wish to waste any more.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «Indeed? That is unfortunate. Still, perhaps you have some value.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «I have gone to considerable expense and effort to bring you here. It is because I have a job for you.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «Yes, but I am not in the habit of asking for things. And you were so difficult to find, even after that small incident on Peragus.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «Assassins, no. Some of the bounty hunters on Nar Shaddaa, however, have chosen to interpret my commands in a manner not of my choosing. You are no value to me dead. If you were a Jedi, they could not kill you anyway. If they could, then you were useless to me.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «Afraid? No. It is simply difficult to be in so many places at once. Holotechnology is currently the most effective... and convenient... way to communicate my commands over vast distances. At least until Aerotech develops the new holotransceiver within two standard months, but by then, it will simply be too late.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «There is something important to me I need protected. The Republic. It is... broken. What has happened on Peragus has set in motion events that I can no longer control. Not to be melodramatic, but I fear it has broken the galaxy... irrevocably. This has occupied much of my attention, and there seems to be no predictable way to resolve the situation.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «In one standard month, the Republic will collapse. Not due to war, or secession, but because it lacks the infrastructure to support itself.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «It is unknown to all but a few, but the Republic lost the Jedi Civil War. At the time of their defeat, the Republic was on the brink of collapse. Rather than remain and continue his campaign against the Republic, however, Revan chose to leave known space. A frustrating turn of events, as a rallying figurehead could have done much to restore order.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «It is unknown to all but a few, but the Sith won the Jedi Civil War. Even with their supposed victory, the war left the Republic on the brink of collapse. Rather than remain and help solidify the Republic, however, Revan chose to leave known space. A frustrating turn of events, as a rallying figurehead could have done much to restore order.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «There is something moving in the galaxy that lies beyond the ability of my instruments to detect or predict. I believe it to be a legacy of the Sith, but I have been unable to determine the source. Whatever this presence is, it is staging strikes at key figures throughout the Republic - and through some unknown means, it is causing the destruction of worlds. Katarr, a Miraluka world in the Mid-Rim, was one such place. I have reason to suspect there was a gathering of Jedi on that world when it was rendered lifeless. I cannot find any pattern in these attacks, and it a source of frustration to me. There is some clue, however, that perhaps the Jedi are linked to these attacks - or that the targets are significant in some way I have yet to discover.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «You misunderstand me... I do not wish to stop the Sith anymore than I wish to stop the Jedi. It is simply important to me that the in-fighting amongst these Jedi religious branches be resolved so the galaxy may be put back together. I do not care which one triumphs, I only want the universe to settle down for a while, catch its breath. All these constant crises are getting somewhat repetitive.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «You could say I am something of a... patriot. Although I was unable to serve during the troubles with the Mandalorians or against the aggressors known as Malak and Revan, I am able... and willing to serve now. The problem is, I can find no side to choose. Both are hidden from me, as they seem to be hiding from each other. Irritating. It is like a dejarik board, where neither player can see the other... nor see all the pieces. It is not a fair game - an equitable game.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «Pazaak bores me. I often suspect my opponent of cheating. I prefer predictable games, such as galactic economics.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «Excellent. It really is in your best interests, you know.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «Then perhaps you care for yourself. The fact the Sith seek you is something that must occupy your thoughts as much as the Republic occupies mine.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «If you care nothing for the Republic, perhaps finding the source of these Sith and resolving the situation would be to our mutual benefit.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «There is no margin for error when I say that these Sith seek to murder you and all Jedi, everywhere. They have been quite efficient. And when they dispose of you, that there will be nothing left to stop them - and the galaxy will fall under their influence.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «Ah, well, there is where we are at cross-purposes. I cannot set you free. You have a tendency to cause dangerous repercussions wherever you go, and I would rather keep those to a minimum. The galaxy really is a fragile place right now.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «You misunderstand. I merely wished the situation resolved. If the Sith are the more capable of both parties, then it is only logical that they fill the vacuum the Jedi and the senate cannot.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «No, that is where you are wrong. If there were, I assure you, I would have contacted them. I'm afraid a broken ex-Jedi is all I have to save the galaxy at the moment. But whether it is Sith or Jedi that emerges victorious, it does not matter, as long as the situation is resolved soon.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «I am a businessman. The Republic needs stability to survive, prosper - and grow. Whether it is led by the Sith or supported by the Jedi is of no consequence to me.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «It is the proximity alarm. We are under attack. Somehow, your allies have found you. Unexpected. You will remain here, under guard. I must see to the defense of my ship.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- (Goto) «Mmmm, they have gotten farther than I calculated. They are very resourceful. But they shall go no farther. Activate the mines. Let us see how far they get now.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «If you thought to escape my notice so easily, you would be wrong. As a token of my goodwill, I present to you a gift: this droid. It will serve you well on your journey.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «I am afraid I do not understand what you mean.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «A common misconception, not supported by facts.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «You would be surprised at how little I care about what you think.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «As I indicated, this unit will remain with you and guard you. It will also serve as an effective voice for my orders during your journey.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «I cannot harm you. You are the key to saving the Republic. Pray that you do not prove yourself otherwise.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «<nowiki>[</nowiki>{{s» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «That would be unfortunate. I would merely lose a droid, while you would lose your entire ship and a sizable chunk of whatever planet you were on when you tripped the failsafe detonator.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «Oh, yes, I excel at that. Regardless, testing it would benefit neither of us, I assure you. Do not be concerned. The detonator is primed only when the droid's behavior core is tampered with - if we enter combat, such actions will not trigger an explosion.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «It was not a request. Any attempt to interfere with my duties would be... unfortunate.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- «As much as I need you, you will find you will need me as well - and this droid will prove useful on your journey.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/Influence]**
- «Again, we suffer a loss while another profits.» [K2/Influence]
- «I fail to see what was accomplished by such an action.» [K2/Influence]
- «I fail to see the economic benefit in such a termination. The cost in the long run-» [K2/Influence]
- «Such acts are effective and often, needed.» [K2/Influence]
- «Instilling fear is a reminder of who truly wields power.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- «Her prices are well above the market average. Always sell high.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Only if there are repercussions later. In matters of business there is a low probability she will cause collateral problems.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «You would make an excellent droid.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Ignoring my advice can put you at an economic disadvantage in the future. It is your prerogative, however.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «So many organics present. Collateral damage is very likely in this battle.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- «I must point out that, if not corrected, the military's influence on Onderon will bring economic damage to Telos and its dependent worlds. The environmental collapse will be followed within a year by economic collapse unless action is taken. A pity, really, but that is the price.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]
- «We must schedule a time for you to teach me how to use such a power. I would, of course, compensate you for such instruction.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- (Goto) «While the Jedi remains on Nar Shaddaa, my eyes shall watch her.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- (Goto) «Enough.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Goto) «What an amusing Jedi specimen you are.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- «Unfortunately, we must go the other way.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- «Droid upgrades improve efficiency. That efficiency can easily offset initial improvement costs. Such upgrades would be a logical investment.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- «Civil disruption can lead to significant market instability. We should attempt to minimize it here.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Contributing to urban destabilization is a dangerous endeavor.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Proceed with your "plan," then.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Your hypothesis may be correct... a momentary fluctuation for a greater goal is acceptable.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Affirmative.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- (Goto) «Perhaps you have heard that the Jedi comes to Nar Shaddaa.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- (Goto) «While she walks upon the smuggler's moon, she is not to be harmed. Observe her, track her, but do not eclipse her movements - or I shall eclipse yours.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- (Goto) «While he walks upon the smuggler's moon, he is not to be harmed. You may watch him, observe his movements, but nothing more.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- (Goto) «You must because one Jedi attracts others. It is the way of things.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- «Apathy is death.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- «Negative. Even if I were small enough to pass through the opening, I can't hover that far above the ground.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «The mangled droids here are... well, quite mangled. Perhaps it would be unwise to disturb whatever lies within these caves.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «I just realized there was a business transaction I needed to make, back at the Ebon Hawk. Surely, you can do without me here.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K2/Western_Square]**
- «Your initiative is commendable. This situation presents an economic advantage to us. Will you allow me to assist?» [K2/Western_Square]
- «Leaving an economic asset vulnerable is a serious error. But it is a windfall to us.» [K2/Western_Square]
- «Your skill may be sufficient without my aid. We will see.» [K2/Western_Square]
- «You do not need me for that endeavor, then.» [K2/Western_Square]
- «The bounty on this Jedi has been rescinded, by the order of Goto. If you attempt to carry out this bounty, then the next bounty shall be placed on your head.» [K2/Western_Square]

### Quote bank B: what others say about G0-T0 (Goto) (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Administration_Level]**
- Peragus Security Officer: «"The blast wrecked the internal components of the droid that set the charge, though, so we can't even dissect it to see what happened."» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Peragus Security Officer: «[Static.] "...I secured the stealth field generator inside one of the footlockers in the security storage room. If I have the specs right, the interface field should be effective against the droid sensors."» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Damaged Mining Droid: «This droid still has power, but it is no longer receiving a signal from the droid central controller.» [K2/Administration_Level] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Damaged Mining Droid: «[Failure] A diagnostic of the droid's behavior core reveals nothing.» [K2/Administration_Level]

**[K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]**
- Loppak Slusk: «Yes, but I'm not a gambler. You're worth enough dead, and it's easier to ship Goto a corpse in a cargo container than a live prisoner.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «That's too bad. Hopefully Goto won't have a problem if there's a few holes in you.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «Slusk's dead, but Goto's still my boss. I'm shipping you to Nar Shaddaa, "Jedi," dead or alive. If you don't want to defend yourself, that's your choice.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Atton: «The Exchange is one of the most powerful groups on the Smuggler's Moon. If this Goto wants you so badly, it'd probably be a good idea not to show up on his doorstep, wouldn't you say? Not to mention, I'm sure Loppak Slusk has friends on Nar Shaddaa that won't be happy to hear he's gone.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «Slusk's dead, but Goto's still my boss. I'm shipping you to Nar Shaddaa, "Jedi".» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]

**[K2/Cantina]**
- Luxa: «Now I know you're not dull enough that you can't read through the lines. Slusk's no good for anybody's business - yours, Lorso's or mine. [[../Cantina#Goto» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «What, besides his being your typically slimy Quarren? Slusk works for Goto, out of Nar Shaddaa. Now this Goto; he's rigid, ruthlessly efficient, and all he sees are numbers.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Goto keeps the squid around because he maintains a steady flow of income... yet it's only half of what I know it could be. I should be Citadel's boss - but Goto doesn't allow breaks in the chain of command. I can't go over Loppak Slusk's head, so I've got to take care of him myself. And I want your help.» [K2/Cantina]

**[K2/Crew_Quarters]**
- Damaged Medical Droid: «You leave the droid alone for now.» [K2/Crew_Quarters] *(+6 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Damaged Medical Droid: «[Failure] You don't have any repair parts. You leave the droid alone for now.» [K2/Crew_Quarters] *(+6 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Czerka_Offices]**
- Jana Lorso: «Czerka has been managing the restoration project without the assistance of a droid for some time now. Habat has commandeered the new droid intelligence that was to be delivered to the Station. I'd like for you to meet the shipment at Dock Module 126, Shuttle Bay 2, and bring the droid back here, before the Ithorians foul things up again.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Opo Chano: «It will take some time for me to rewire the droid and have it ready for your use, Executive Lorso.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «You proved that you can handle yourself quite well in dealing with the retrieval of the droid intelligence. The next matter of business I would ask of you is much more delicate. When we first set up here on Citadel Station, the Exchange was already quite well established. Loppak Slusk had more influence over business matters than the Telosian government. To ensure our success, we involved ourselves with the Exchange, and now that we have a handle on the situation, we wish to un-entangle ourselves from them. If you know anything about the Exchange, then you know that they would not take this well. Slusk is loathe to have his hands removed from any business on the station, so a more direct method will have to be taken. To put it plainly, I want you to kill Loppak Slusk.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- Medical Droid: «[Failure] You don't have enough repair parts. You leave the droid alone for now.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Medical Droid: «[Failure] You don't have enough repair parts (sic). You leave the droid alone for now.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Dock_Module]**
- Exchange Lieutenant: «So I heard. You should consider Czerka's offer. If you return the droid to Executive Lorso, she'll pay you far better than the Ithorians.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «Interesting. I believe you are correct, though I am by no means an expert. Such modifications are illegal on Citadel Station. Lieutenant Grenn should be informed so that he might conduct an investigation. I will leave it with you. But more importantly, you must take the droid back to Chodo Habat. I fear there may be more attacks if we do not see it quickly to safety.» [K2/Dock_Module]

**[K2/Docks]**
- Vogga: «Ah, the mighty Hanharr. You wear your shackles well. How did your meeting with Goto go? Lethal, I hope?» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «These are my hounds, Hanharr, just as you are. I tell you again - find Goto. Kill him. And do not return until you have done this.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «Goto and his Exchange hijacks Vogga's ships. Hurts business.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «[Success] Goto and his Exchange hijacks Vogga's ships. Hurts business.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «[Failure] I don't think so. Goto not hire many humans. You want waste my time?» [K2/Docks]
- Bith: «Hope you're not here to speak with Vogga. I doubt he'll listen to anything you have to say - it's just Goto, Goto, Goto.» [K2/Docks]
- Bith: «Vogga won't admit it, but Goto's outmaneuvered him, and Vogga doesn't know how to deal with it.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «This is the work of Goto. Somehow, he knows my freighter schedules, and has hijacked every last one of them. I do not wish to speak of this anymore. Ask me of something else, or be on your way. This talk of Goto only antagonizes me.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «There's nothing to be said about the fuel of Sleheyron, other than it is going nowhere, not with the loss of so many of my freighters. This is the work of Goto. Somehow, he knows my freighter schedules, and has hijacked every last one of them. I do not wish to speak of this anymore. Ask me of something else, or be on your way. This talk of Goto only antagonizes me.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «The infuriating one, Goto, he is the one I wish to see lying in a pool of blood in front of me... or with his knee bent.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «This I know. My fuel would fetch a pretty price there, but not a drop will leave here until Goto is dealt with.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «But this is getting ahead of ourselves. First, Goto must be dealt with, or the stores of Sleheyron will remain on Sleheyron. Was there something else? Otherwise, see yourself to the door.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «You've returned. But I do not see Goto chained behind you, can it be that you have not had success yet?» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Droid_Warehouse]**
- Hanharr: «By Goto, leader of one of the many tribes of the Exchange. He flies across the black of this moon, hidden, unseen. Difficult to track.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «There is one way - to pretend to be prey... and let Goto take your ship.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «Yes, to be bait, to pretend to be prey... and let Goto take your ship.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «Goto is a predator. He feeds on the freighters of Vogga the Hutt, smelling them, then swooping down on them. If you were to become one of Vogga's freighters, he would take you as well.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «Goto preys on Vogga, starves his trading, makes small, slow cuts at his tribe until he bleeds to death.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «Goto has preyed on him since his arrival. Goto can smell his ships - and capture them. If you were hidden as one of Vogga's ships, Goto would capture you as well. And then once you are inside his ship, you devour him from within.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «By changing how your ship smells. The pattern of lights and codes within the ship are what Goto smells.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «It can be done... by changing how your ship smells. The pattern of lights and codes within the ship are what Goto smells.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Mira: «We took out Visquis, but Goto has her... and that means no bounty for me.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Mira: «No one knows how to reach Goto except Visquis, and that squid-head died in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Mira: «The only way to reach Goto is if we had a Jedi, but now, he's got your friend, he doesn't have anybody else he wants captured...» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Mira: «If you were hunting for Goto's yacht, your freighter would be flying blind... well, unless it was one of Vogga the Hutt's cargo ships, then it would be snapped up by Goto pretty quick.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Mira: «Goto's been preying on Vogga's freighters for a while now - it's the reason Vogga's had to haul his bulk up here to Nar Shaddaa from Nal Hutta. Even with all the traffic around Nar Shaddaa, Goto seems to always know which ones are Vogga's, and his ship just snaps them up.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Mira: «Problem is, Vogga's shut down the droid warehouse until he can find out who's been leaking the codes. You'd have to be a droid to get in there.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- C7-E3: «Oh, I see. You are the droid recently acquired from Kodin? In that case, you were assigned to assist me here in place of the old C6 droid.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Atton: «He says he's got the transponder codes to Vogga's freighters - one that can be picked up by Goto.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Atton: «I don't believe this - he says he's got the transponder codes that Goto's using to hijack Vogga's freighters.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Atton: «We can change the ID signature of the Ebon Hawk, and get to Goto's yacht that way.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «If you are hunting Goto, you will fail - you are chasing, ghosts, sh- I... will accompany you. Help you.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Atton: «Yeah, right - you're the one who wanted to sell her to Goto in the first place.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Mira: «Yeah, and I don't like being cheated. Trust me, Goto's yacht is going to have some pretty heavy defenses. You're going to need all the help you can get.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]

**[K2/Ebon_Hawk]**
- Bao-Dur: «What is it, Goto?» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- Computer Panel: «[Page 1 of 3]<br>Task 1046A [Head Technician Dergar Chester] --- I can't believe Czerka Corp released a new protocol droid model with so many flaws. The batch we ordered last month have all fried out from overheating. The Czerka rep said the droid was designed for "temperature controlled environments" such as starships, but I don't buy it.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Computer Panel: «[Page 2 of 3]<br>The real problem is that the Sith-cursed power cell of the new model runs too hot. And worse, the output modulator was installed right next to the cell, and is the first thing to get melted into slag when the cell overheats. Needless to say, without a functioning output modulator, you could jostle the droid and it would pop like fireworks on Republic Day.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Protocol Droid: «There is nothing you can do to restore the droid's speech system.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- Atton: «Yeah, maybe if the droid's not very good, there's some credits to be made here.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- S4-C8: «[Failure] [You fail to find anything wrong with the droid.]» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- S4-C8: «[You notice that there is an unusual looking module attached under the droid's left arm. ]» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Vogga Thug: «It is Exchange Boss Goto who make the big trouble for Vogga, who get mad, then make the big trouble for all us. Long chain.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Vogga Thug: «Not unless they come to talk to him about Goto, he not let them in. It all he sees, thought of Goto consume Vogga like big gundark.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- Sentry Droid Mark I: «[Success] You have only been able to scavenge a few components from the droid.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Sentry Droid Mark I: «[Success] Your repair skill has allowed you to remove a number of components from the droid.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Hanharr: «We have slain Goto... burned the leader of the greatest tribe of the Exchange. The fire of his death shall bathe the moon below in red... flickering, a beacon. *All* shall know what was done in the skies over Nar Shaddaa... that we crushed Goto's life from him where he was strongest.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Mira: «I can't believe we just blew up Goto's yacht. That's going to destabilize crime throughout this whole sector.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Mira: «Zez-Kai Ell? Um... well that's not true. I already found him. Actually he found me first. He hired me to watch out for you, keep Goto off your back... until he could meet with you. We'll need to head back to Nar Shaddaa, to that safe house off the docks. I said I'd meet up with him there if we ran into any trouble.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Hanharr: «One of Goto's machines. *Here.*» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/HK-47]**
- Damaged HK Unit: «[Success] [You have installed the droid's vocabulator.]» [K2/HK-47]
- Damaged HK Unit: «[Success] [You have installed the droid's control cluster.]» [K2/HK-47]
- Damaged HK Unit: «[Success] [You have installed the droid's processor.]» [K2/HK-47]
- Damaged HK Unit: «[Success] [You have installed the droid's chassis. It looks like it will make its chest stop sparking, which is a minor relief.]» [K2/HK-47]
- Damaged HK Unit: «[There is a low hum from within the droid, and the sound of circuits sparking, restoring power throughout the droid's systems.]» [K2/HK-47]
- Damaged HK Unit: «[Failure] [This requires the droid be active before installation. It will have to wait until it's operational.]» [K2/HK-47]

**[K2/Hangar_Bay]**
- Atton: «I have no idea. Previous owner, maybe? I'd love to get it overhauled, but that's a major job - besides, the droid'll be good enough for now.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]**
- Mira: «Yeah, and maybe you'd like to explain why you've decided to backstab Goto and claim the Jedi for yourself.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Visquis: «Ah, what a prime example of human arrogance. What you know is substantially less than what I know. Otherwise, you would not have come. One, everything that goes on in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr is invisible to Goto. It is something I discovered by accident, and have tested many times since to insure accuracy. Second, I am not acting on Goto's orders - not anymore. Hanharr and I reached an agreement to deal with Goto altogether... and collect a tidy sum from Vogga.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Mira: «You've signed on with Vogga the Hutt? Visquis, you're dumber than I thought. There's no way Goto won't find out.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Visquis: «He might, it is possible. But it is a risk I am willing to take. You see, Vogga is very tired of having his freighters hijacked by Goto - it is causing his word to have less... weight on Nal Hutta. There is a leak in his operations here on Nar Shaddaa. Rather than simply find the leak and eliminate it, his anger has compelled him to eliminate the source of the irritation altogether. Unfortunately, Goto is very careful about showing himself to others - Vogga's employee, Hanharr, discovered this when he attended the gathering on Goto's yacht. It is always holograms with Goto – always. But I have a suspicion that Goto will reveal himself to a Jedi. When that happens, that is when I intend to strike. Unfortunately, it is clear he will not reveal himself to a two-credit bounty hunter with the audacity to try and strike a deal with me. So please - tell me where the Jedi is. I am not in the mood for negotiation.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- Visquis: «Yes, and impossible to enter if I do not wish it. So I chose my visitors carefully - and my victims. We may talk freely here, as we did in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. It is my home, stocked with my soldiers, far from Goto's eyes.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Visquis: «Oh, you mistake me. I was not the one who put the original bounty on you - we all have our Masters, you know. My soon-to-be-deceased boss, Goto, is the one who placed the monumental sum of credits upon your head. Your price is so high that any bounty hunter who captures you would be able to buy their own planet. You must have angered Goto greatly for him to hunt you so. And that is why you are the perfect bait... and why I will bring you to Goto, then kill him.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Visquis: «Goto, I didn't...  I wasn't... Please, take the Jedi - I offer the human as a gift, freely, as I would my own li-» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «No! Visquis dead... the Jeedai gone! Wh... And this tunnel is sealed - there is no way back into the Jekk'Jekk Tarr! That means Goto has the Jee-» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «Your friend has been captured by Goto.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- Construction Droid: «You can't find any way to activate the droid manually.» [K2/Jungle]
- Construction Droid Mark I(I): «This droid looks like it hasn't been used in some time. You can't find any way to activate the droid. The droid has what appears to be a transponder, presumably to work in unison with other utility droids.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Jungle_Landing]**
- Remote Droid: «[Success] Patrol mode engaged. Once you end this session the droid will begin patrol and destroy any enemies it encounters. WARNING: Once you end this session the droid's program will be locked in. You will not be able to modify the droid anymore.» [K2/Jungle_Landing] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Kreia: «The welding of the droid as it goes about its work.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- Mira: «I'm Mira. I'm the best bounty hunter in this system, and that's not me bragging, that's fact. I had you in my sights ever since you landed. I've been watching you run all over the Refugee Sector like a bantha, and for someone with a price on their head as high as yours, you sure don't know how to keep a low profile. Look - I know that squid-head Visquis sent you a message to meet him in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. He works for Goto, and it's a trap. I'm betting he's going to lure you in there, start a fight, and then he's going to wrap you up and deliver you to Goto, dead, claiming you attacked him.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Hanharr: «Visquis is said to hold second Jedi. Visquis is keeping him safe, letting the bounty rise before giving him to Goto to get more honor.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Hanharr: «You are Jeedai. You wander galaxy alone, cast out – exiled. And like Hanharr, Jeedai wishes to find Goto... perhaps ask Goto why he put so many credits on your head and those of your tribe. Goto only appears as light and voice, and Hanharr wishes to meet him in the flesh - like Jeedai does.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Hanharr: «I know you are going to meet Visquis. And you know that Visquis will try to capture you, sell you to Goto.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Hanharr: «Goto gave us all an order not to harm Jeedai. But there is nothing more dangerous than a tribe of a hundred hunters denied prey they have hunted for many seasons. If we cannot attack you, then others of your tribe who came to this moon - your sisters and brothers... Your... friends... they will become prey in your place.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Mira: «You know Visquis is arranging a trap for you in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. No surprise there. Thing is, he intends to cut the bounty hunters out of the loop and deliver you to Goto personally. Hm. Not smart from where I'm standing.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Hanharr: «I have brought the Jedi to you. Now where is Goto?» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]**
- Zuka: «What did the droid say?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]

**[K2/Mining_Tunnels]**
- Atton: «Yeah, it's like a military-issue energy shield, except it's designed to protect the miners against lasers... and heat. Should work against the droid mining lasers. It won't last forever - and certainly not against multiple laser hits - but it may buy you some time if you get ambushed by a battalion of droids.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «Yeah, it's possible some of the droid models may have mining shields on. If so, the shields may absorb laser fire. You can usually tell when a shield is active - it'll make an electrical field around the target. If they activate a shield, the best thing you can do is hit them with a melee weapon or try to burn out the shield with continual fire. But that could take a while, and it leaves you a target.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Damaged Mining Droid: «[Success] Patrol mode engaged. Once you end this session the droid will begin patrol and destroy any enemies it encounters. WARNING: Once you end this session the droid's program will be locked in. You will not be able to modify the droid anymore, although you will be able to detonate its core if it is blocking your progress.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Central Controller: «[Judging from the droid schematic displays, it looks like Atton was right: the droids rely on thermal sensors to detect fuel deposits. You could easily blind them by raising the temperature.]» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Central Controller: «[Judging from the droid schematic displays, the droids rely on thermal sensors to detect fuel deposits. You could easily blind them by raising the temperature.]» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- Azanti Zhug: «Goto's head is filled with madness. Zhug family hunt the galaxy, look for Jedi. Jedi finally comes here, and we can no longer hunt? There is no sense to it.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Twin Suns: «If you wish to live, you should respect Goto's wishes.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Hanharr: «Hnnhr... let the Zhugs defy Goto. And after I've collected their bounty, I'll keep their heads as trophies.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Hanharr: «Goto or not, I will carve a bloody swatch through your entire family, Zhug - I swear it to you.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- HK-50: «Request: If Goto's vessel is no longer neutral ground, inform us so that we might initiate assassination protocols and commence firing at once.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Twin Suns: «It would be unwise to commit violence here. Goto's order was clear.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Twin Suns: «Goto told us to leave the Jedi alone, it is true.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Kodin: «Good enough, take the droid module, Kodin scrap frame.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Nodobo: «Yes, offworlder. A droid's body can withstand forces much greater than yours or mine, so Lupo removed the inertial reducers. With less weight, the droid's swoop can go much faster and maneuver much quicker to avoid the fans' blasts.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Guron: «YES! The module helps the droid see what is all around his bike.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Guron: «The module is part of the droid's frame; it can't be removed.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «Well, it just so happens that I have the access codes to the droid's cage.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «If someone wanted to reprogram the droid or sabotage it in some fashion, they would need the access codes to the cage.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «Not so fast. If Lupo sees that someone has tampered with the droid, he will get it fixed and change the cage's codes. We must destroy it on the first try. If you are not up to the task, you will render my codes useless.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «[Failure] You cannot bypass the security protecting the droid's behavior module.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «There is only one file in the droid's behavioral sub-routines.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «This file contains all the data needed for the droid to race the swoop track without incident.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «[Success] You have reprogrammed the droid to think it is in a race and about to lose. This activates the droid's self-destruct system.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- T3-M4: «::T3 has reprogrammed the droid to think the power conduit at the back of the cage is Lupo's swoop bike interface. It attempts to interface::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «[Success] You disable the droid's power regulators and equilibrium stabilizers, causing an overload.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «[Failure] You cannot find an obvious way to sabotage the droid.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Rescue_T3-M4]**
- HK Protocol Droid: «Hesitant Answer: Ah. A T3 utility droid would be a common sight in this facility. It is indeed curious that I have not seen many since my arrival. However, I feel I must inform you that (droid predjudice aside), T3 models exhibit excessive individualism when not routinely memory wiped. This individualism can become such a nuisance that even a droid such as myself is tempted to reduce them to their base components - if not crush them into slag. But enough of my seemingly-irrelevant tangent. Where did you leave the droid, Master? That would logically be the best place to look.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Accessing Memory: Ah... yes, I believe you have asked this question before, Master. Let me see if I can recite the answer I provided the first time. Oh, yes. It was, "where did you leave the droid, Master?" If you are looking for garbage, it would be best to ask a custodial unit - not a translation droid.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Condescending Explanation: Master, the console governing the droid maintenance area... and the airlock... is voice-printed.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Conjecture: I suspect once he realized something was wrong in the facility, he voice-locked the droid bay functions. A prudent measure, but in the end he met the same fate as the rest of the organics.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Maintenance Officer: «'I mentioned the trouble to the Jedi's protocol droid, and he seemed concerned about his master's safety. I told the droid not to worry, that Coorta wouldn't...'» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Maintenance Officer: «"...when the dock officer reported the droids repairing the Ebon Hawk, I installed a voiceprint ID on the droid console system."» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Thoughtful Extrapolation: Perhaps it ran afoul of the other droids in this facility, and was attacked. Most distressing. It is too bad that there is no way to reach the droid from here, Master, otherwise you might be able to determine what happened to it.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]

**[K2/Residential_082_East]**
- Opo Chano: «If only. My loan is owed to Goto, the Exchange boss on Nar Shaddaa. I'll be dead before he lets the debt go.» [K2/Residential_082_East]

**[K2/Residential_082_West]**
- Chodo Habat: «That is not known. It may have been an accident. Perhaps the droid intelligence was overtaxed by the staggering size of the project and became irrational. Some among my herd suspect theft. A droid intelligence of that sophistication would fetch a high price on the black market. Others fear its disappearance the result of sabotage, by Czerka or some other organization that seeks to slow the Restoration Project or discredit us. But the how or why of it is irrelevant.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Ah, you have returned. You have decided to help us guard the droid intelligence?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «...then it could access the Czerka mainframe manually, gather the files, and give them to us. Quite brilliant. Do not concern yourself with the reprogramming - my herd contains technicians who can perform the procedure once the droid is brought here.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Greeter: «Chodo is with the droid intelligence, where he usually is.» [K2/Residential_082_West]

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- Rodian: «And why would I? When we saw you, we could not believe our luck. If we could collect Goto's price on your head, we wouldn't have to worry about ourselves for quite a while. But we couldn't do anything, in our compromised position. We made plans while working here, but we couldn't imagine that you would deliver yourself into our hands.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Rodian: «We thought our situation being stuck here on the planet's surface would keep us from collecting on the price Goto has placed on your head. But now you've so thoughtfully delivered yourself to us. We would be remiss to allow this opportunity to pass us by a second time.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Kreia: «Don't be a fool - Atris stole the ship and the droid.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atris: «The freighter... it is important. And I thought the droid was as well. But I was wrong.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Mistress, we still do not know the significance of the ship or the droid. We downloaded all you asked, but we are still no closer to the answers you seek.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Sky_Ramp]**
- Royalist Soldier: «They're gonna overrun our position! Send in the droid reinforcements, We need them now!» [K2/Sky_Ramp]

**[K2/Trayus_Core]**
- Kreia: «Nar Shaddaa shall persist as it always has, but its evil and corruption shall grow. The Exchange shall rise in power, but only in the end to be consumed by the hunger of the true Lords of Nar Shaddaa who dwell upon the planet below. The Hutts will not long tolerate the Exchange, and without Goto among them, they shall feed upon themselves until there is nothing left.» [K2/Trayus_Core]

**[K2/Underground_Base]**
- Damaged HK Protocol Droid: «Patrol mode engaged. Once you end this session the droid will follow you.» [K2/Underground_Base]

### Quote bank B2: pronoun references resolved from context (context-resolved, hand-reviewed scene-wide (each line read by hand against the whole conversation; anchor = the line that names the character))
- [context-resolved] Hanharr: «Only his shadow and his voice, he fears to stand before others in the flesh. He is strange prey.» [K2/Docks] (anchor: Vogga: Ah, the mighty Hanharr. You wear your shackles well. ...)
- [context-resolved] Fassa: «For money I assume. We don't know how he does it... every ship we send out, he gets.» [K2/Docks] (anchor: Why is Goto hijacking the freighters?...)
- [context-resolved] Bith: «Probably not much more than anyone else can tell you. He runs the show in Nar Shaddaa. The Exchange's never had a boss like him before.» [K2/Docks] (anchor: What can you tell me about Goto?...)
- [context-resolved] Vogga: «I do not know how he has managed it, but he preys upon my freighters. Entire shipments of fuel from Sleheyron have been hijacked by his minions. Because of this, I have been forced to cease my trading operations, which has proven quite annoying.» [K2/Docks] (anchor: What has Goto done to you?...)
- [context-resolved] Mira: «He's got a cloaking device. He's the one that arranges the meetings on his ship, and until then, he can't be found. Trust me, if anyone knew how to track his ship, he'd have every bounty hunter and criminal on Nar Shaddaa gunning for it.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse] (anchor: How does Goto hide his ship?...)
- [context-resolved] Mira: «Yeah, you're right - but his warehouse is shut down because of it, he's not sending any more flights out. Even if you signed on with Vogga, that slug wouldn't let you out of the system anyway.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse] (anchor: How does Goto hide his ship?...)
- [context-resolved] Mira: «Probably does it by tracking their transponder codes, but no one knows how he's getting them.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse] (anchor: Mira: Goto's been preying on Vogga's freighters for a while ...)

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to G0-T0 (Goto) (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K2/Dantooine]**
- Saedhe: «Your droid is right. I'll pay you 5000 credits instead. I'll get this back to Zherron when I get back to Khoonda.» [K2/Dantooine]

**[K2/Depths]**
- Bao-Dur: «If you are hearing this, then the sequence has been completed... when active, it will destroy Malachor... again. I have one last command for you. You must remain behind and ensure that the sequence fires properly once the General gives the command... if not, then all we have fought for has been for nothing. You have done all I asked... all you were built for. And for that, I thank you.» [K2/Depths]

**[K2/Ebon_Hawk]**
- Bao-Dur: «If you've got some time, I'd like to see what I can upgrade for you.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- Bao-Dur: «Right, let's get you open and see what you can do.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- Bao-Dur: «I have to say, you are put together quite well. There wasn't much to do.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- Bao-Dur: «There were a few things from my Remote that I was able to integrate into your construction.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- HK-47: «Query: What is it you wish, fat one?» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]

**[K2/Enclave_Courtyard]**
- Ralon: «This is no idle boast, traveler, but a fact. A holocron is easily worth many thousands of credits. But I will sell it to you for a low price.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- Jorran: «I-I managed to grab three things. One's some sort of crystal and the other looks like parts. For a lightsaber maybe, I don't know. If you give me 1300 credits, you can have it all. That'll be enough for me to get off this rock. No returns on this, you're stuck with what you buy.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «You wouldn't!» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- Mira: «What do you want now?» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/Influence]**
- Mira: «You didn't have to do that, but I guess maybe that's the point.» [K2/Influence]
- HK-47: «Statement: Oh, master, must you act like a Jedi?» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «Silence, droid! I am not speaking to you.» [K2/Influence]
- HK-47: «Statement: Master, there are times when your actions make my behavior core glow.» [K2/Influence]
- HK-47: «Observation: You display a fascinating variety of cruelties, master. It is difficult to catalogue them all.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- Hanharr: «Watch your words, human, or we will see who is the stronger of us.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Sakarie: «So what do you want for it? A weapon, money, or the lightsaber crystal. Your pick.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- Beast-rider: «Your... friend is correct. Much harm is being done on both sides as long as the blockade is in effect.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]
- Mira: «You better not try any of those Jedi mind tricks on me.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- Visquis: «You all, attack the J... You all... you never truly worked for me.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- Saedhe: «Thanks for your help on this. Here are the credits as promised. I'll get this to Zherron when I get back to Khoonda.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- Onderon Soldier: «You would fight with them? This is madness! We need reinforcements!» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Mandalore: «Why are you joining in this? I prefer Vaklu over Talia, but this is hardly a task for a warrior.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
