## Bao-Dur
*Corpus coverage: 265 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 13 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 6 pronoun references resolved from context · 50 lines addressed to them · 39 narration lines · 11 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Bao-Dur is the Iridonian engineer who built the Malachor mass shadow generator [K2/Depths].
### Personality and motivations
Atonement and anger: «And above all else, myself, for Malachor.» [K2/Bao-Dur].
### Arc (scene by scene)
- **Telos:** rescue.
- Companion.
- **Malachor:** his hologram and remote fire the generator again.
### Relationships
- Exile ("General").
- His remote.
### Abilities
- Engineering; shield tech.
### Feats
- Malachor V.
### Power-scaling statements
- «When I built weapons to destroy them, the Mandalorians were destroyed.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
### Duels and fights
- Fights on Malachor (narration).
### Verdict implications
- Engineering hax.
### Open questions
- His fate on Malachor (narration).

### Abilities: game mechanics (FLAGGED: gameplay stats, not lore)
- [K2/Party] Bao-Dur: Class Tech Specialist, Level 6. Feats: DC=10, DC=8, Improved Gear Head, Unarmed Specialist II.

### Cut content (RCM)
- None found on the RCM-tagged lines for this character.

### Quote bank A: Bao-Dur's own lines, by scene (page)

**[K2/Bao-Dur]**
- «Hey, General. Are you all right?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «You look like you've been standing to close to one of my shield generators.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Whoa, claws out today, aren't they?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Claws out today, aren't they? You've been spending too much time with Mira.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Seriously, you don't look that way from spending extra time in the 'fresher. I've seen other Jedi - not many, but a few - who looked like that. And I feel a little... strange... around you. A little more in control - calm, like I usually only get when I'm repairing something.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «All right, if you don't want to talk about it, I won't press you.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «[[../Bao-Dur#Talk» [K2/Bao-Dur] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Hey, General. I want to talk to you.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «No, you're not leaving until we've talked about this.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Oh, is that how it is?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Maybe the others don't know what's happening, but I can see what's going on - and the others have felt it, too.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «You can't hide it, General. Just look at your face. I saw other Jedi who looked like you - during and after the war. It's that woman. You have to get away from her. She'll destroy you.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «She's manipulating you, maybe all of us.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I'm not saying you're not strong enough, General, but I'm not discounting her either.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Just be careful, General. You don't know what she wants, and she's got some goal. I'm sure of it.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «General. Need something?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Sorry, guess I can't get my head out of the past.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «As long as you don't mind having me around. It's a nice change from drifting.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I moved around for a couple years. Working as a starship mechanic got me from place to place. I wasn't ready to settle down after the war.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Then you understand my restlessness. Though the war had ended, I couldn't find peace in anything.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «And I don't think I'll be the last.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «As long as I kept moving, I didn't have to think about what happened. Know what I mean?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I'm sure you do.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I was, no doubt. It's hard to face up to yourself sometimes, and for a few years, I couldn't.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Maybe if I had the Force to lean on, I could have worked it out. But then the Force didn't seem to be of much help to others.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Mostly, I was angry. Angry about what I had done, about why I had done it.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I decided I'd do something constructive. I wanted to make up for the things I'd done in the war. I wanted to design planetary shields, but there weren't many systems with the credits to spare - there was more that needed to be rebuilt than protected. I found out that Telos was going to be the flagship project for the Republic, and it sounded like something good. I saw Telos before the Sith razed it. It deserved a better fate. But Czerka ruined everything. I thought I could force Czerka out on my own, but I guess I can't fix everything myself.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «All I wanted to do was send a message, but I couldn't even do that right. That's the past, though. It's good to be working with you again, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «It's good to be working with you again, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Talking to you is better than talking to a machine all day.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Yes, General?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «That old thing? I built him when I was a kid. Been following me around for years now, despite what I've done to try and chase him off.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Hey, just kidding. I'm happy to have you around.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I would, but then I wouldn't be the only one with a floating sphere following him around, and I kind of like that.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «It's not that bad. I guess you'd know - you've always got someone following you around.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «He helps me out with repairs. I outfitted him with a cutting laser and some other tools for delicate modifications. He's also good for singeing the pants of annoying techs.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I've been thinking about doing some other work on him, but I barely have time. Too busy fixing up the ship.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «If the Republic would just rein Czerka in, there'd be no problem. But as long as Czerka is allowed to undermine the Ithorians' efforts, Telos will remain dead. I can't take seeing my work being used by those bloodsuckers. But there's nothing I can do about it, so let's talk about something else.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I got tired of it - kept dropping my hydrospanner. Figured I'd get a new one.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I was only kidding. Actually, it was a souvenir from Malachor. I was lucky it was all I lost.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «If you were me, you'd probably want to joke about it, too. Actually, it was a souvenir from Malachor. I was lucky I didn't lose more.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «But at least it gave me something to do, right? Everyone always said I was probably half machine, anyway.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Just working on the ship. I'm not sure who got her up and running, but I'm amazed she's even spaceworthy. Whoever made these repairs doesn't think like most mechanics. But don't worry, I'll get everything in shape.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I didn't want to talk about the war, but can I ask you something?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Just drop it then. It's not important.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «The Force lets you look right through me, huh? But if you don't want to talk about it, that's okay.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Sure, it's nothing important, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Why did you decide to fight?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I felt the same way. I remembered when word of the Mandalorian attacks arrived on Iridonia.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «The war went poorly before Revan and the Jedi lent aid to the Republic. Many of us believed the Jedi to be cowards who were afraid to face the Mandalorian threat. I remembered when word of the Mandalorian attacks arrived on Iridonia.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «My people had colonies across the Outer Rim. Many of them were among the first systems to fall.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Revenge, and to crush the Mandalorians - to send them back to wherever it was they came from.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I did not join because I wanted to protect, though. I hated them. I wanted to destroy them - to give them the mercy they gave the people they conquered. I remember the thrill I felt when we fought them in battle. Victories were rare, but we celebrated every Mandalorian's death. Do you know how it felt?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I couldn't do that. It was almost as though the battle took control of me, drove me forward.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «It's always on my mind, now. That loss of control blinded me, turned me into a weapon. I... just needed to get that off my chest.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «You know, I'm glad I found you again, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «You're right about that.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «We were together at Malachor. I don't know if anyone else could understand.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Getting philosophical on me? I'm here because you found me on Telos and I decided to come along for the ride - not that I had much choice after we got going.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Your recollection of the incident must be clouded. But as to why I'm here, it's simple.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Tired of me already?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I was frustrated. Watching the Ithorians getting pushed around by Czerka - I thought I could make a difference, but it was taken away from me. Guess if one planet was good enough for me, why not the galaxy?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «You might not be looking, but maybe it'll happen along the way.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «You just have to know what the circuits look like.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Only if you don't have any more questions, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I'll be sure to do that, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «That's the way I see things. Traveling with you, I know there's something else in the universe, but I can't do anything about it anyways. So I'll leave it to you to take care of.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «They might not call you a Jedi anymore, but believe me, you are. It's not the sort of thing that you just stop being. You're stuck with it, just like you're stuck being the General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Having you here has an effect on me, General. I never noticed it years ago. I think my mind was too occupied then.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Yes, it is the feeling of hatred, of anger.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I feel the anger boiling to the surface, as I once felt towards the Mandalorians, but now it is towards myself alone. I cannot let go of it. It is too raw, too consuming. The last years of my life have been defined by it. The Mandalorians, Czerka and Revan. And above all else, myself, for what I did at Malachor.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Anger is the only constant in my life - perhaps it is the truth. Drawing from it, I was able to crush the Mandalorians. Once, I built shields to defend planets from their attackers. The Mandalorians crushed them. When I built weapons to destroy them, the Mandalorians were destroyed.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Yes, teach me the ways of the Force, General. Together, our enemies will fall.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «We can't all be Jedi like you, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «I feel... calm. More in control. The anger is still there, but I can feel it drifting away. The last years of my life have been defined by it - the Mandalorians, Czerka and Revan. And above all else, myself, for Malachor.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Never, General. It had to be done. My hands destroyed the Mandalorians. I cannot be forgiven for that.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Even if I did it out of hatred of the Mandalorians?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «That might be the truth, but I don't want to see it that way. I can't just ignore the blood on my hands.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «My feelings are bad enough without having to worry about Jedi problems. My anger is nothing compared to the blood on my hands.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Even if there isn't, I still feel like I need to do something, to make up for it.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Maybe that's just the shield that I need to stay sane.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- «Under your guidance, I feel like I could overcome my anger.» [K2/Bao-Dur]

**[K2/Crystal_Cave]**
- «I don't know about that.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- «They are vitally important pieces of equipment on tectonically or environmentally unstable regions. But do you really need atmospheric sensors on Dantooine?» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- «I'll have this open in no time. Child's play.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- «Nice job, General.» [K2/Crystal_Cave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Czerka_Site]**
- «There's the landing pad. There should be a computer terminal I can access from there. Looks like we're going to have to fight our way there, though.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «Look sharp.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «Hopefully, I'll be able to access the shield network from this console. Good it's functional, and my passcodes still work. Now let's find your ship.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «The TSF probably thinks the ship was put down in the wastes, but they don't know the planet as well as I do. Telos' atmosphere has been turned into acidic vapor. Landing a ship in the wastes would be like sealing it in a hangar full of hungry mynocks.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «So that means there's probably an unsanctioned landing site somewhere on the planet. Still shielded, but not a restoration zone or other listed facility. That's why I need access to the shield network. Here - a small anomaly in the shield network's power grid. I'm not surprised the TSF didn't spot this. It's subtle, more like an error or random flux than anything suspicious.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «It looks like power is being drawn to generate a shield over a small area in the polar region, but nothing should be down there. Orbital cameras show... nothing. Just an empty mesa.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «That's a little tougher. According to the computer, a shuttle is currently docked inside the research facility. At least, there was a last report, though that was months ago.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «I don't, but that's not going to stop me. I'm getting back to Citadel if I have to build a new ship myself.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «There's one other small problem. Recently, Czerka teams that were sent into the military facility have not been coming out. But it's not as though we have any choice.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- «Where are you going? We need to check out the computer console up on the landing pad, not go exploring some abandoned military facility.» [K2/Czerka_Site]

**[K2/Dantooine]**
- «But Dantooine is temperate. There is no need for atmospheric sensors except for seasonal data collection.» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Depths]**
- «If you are watching this, then what the General suspected was true... the Sith are striking from the graveyard of Malachor V. The ship the Sith Lord was on was one of the vessels damaged in that final battle. If you reach Malachor's surface, the mass shadow generator can be undone... provided there are enough vessels intact within the orbiting debris or buried in the planet's surface. Use the sequence I am unlocking in your memory banks on the engine core of any vessel you encounter on Malachor. If my calculations are correct, four vessels should be enough to power up the mass shadow generator and undo the damage that was done in Malachor V... the damage I did. Once the sequence has begun, it cannot be stopped, so make sure the General is off the planet before triggering the sequence. Otherwise, there will be no retreat. I know your weapon systems are minimal, and I don't know what awaits you on Malachor's surface. Try to avoid contact with any potential threats. Follow your programming...» [K2/Depths]
- «If you are hearing this, then the sequence has been completed... when active, it will destroy Malachor... again. I have one last command for you. You must remain behind and ensure that the sequence fires properly once the General gives the command... if not, then all we have fought for has been for nothing. You have done all I asked... all you were built for. And for that, I thank you.» [K2/Depths]

**[K2/Docks]**
- «The Mandalorian Wars proved such things. In only one Jedi did I see valor.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

- «What is it, Goto?» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]

- «If you've got some time, I'd like to see what I can upgrade for you.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- «He helps me out with repairs. That isn't a problem, is it?» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- «General.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Influence]**
- «That was a kind act. It is good to see that one can still show compassion even after the war.» [K2/Influence]
- «For some, it would have been a difficult thing to do.» [K2/Influence]
- «And that is why I follow you, General. Lead on.» [K2/Influence]
- «You did the right thing, General.» [K2/Influence]
- «I knew there was a reason I'm following you around.» [K2/Influence]
- «What happened? One moment, we were speaking, rationally, and then I felt this rage...» [K2/Influence]
- «We have seen much during the war. Is it your wish to continue causing such suffering?» [K2/Influence]
- «As always, General, when you lead, I shall follow. But it is only because we have been through so much together.» [K2/Influence]
- «That was not a battle we needed to fight, General.» [K2/Influence]
- «If you persist, then the Mandalorians truly won.» [K2/Influence]
- «That was an act of a Mandalorian.» [K2/Influence]
- «You lead, I follow.» [K2/Influence]
- «We fought the Mandalorians, now I find myself acting like them.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- «I wonder what his mission was... I think you may have helped the Republic considerably.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «It's gonna be tough to avoid hitting innocents in this crowded cantina.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «I dreamt of Malachor. I remember the ships.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Not since Malachor. It is always in my dreams. I remember the ships.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «The last stand of the Republic. The tattered remnants of our fleet, the largest we could gather, but it was damaged, weakened and vulnerable. The Mandalorians couldn't resist. They tore into us like beasts, shredding our ships to scrap as we fought back. Yet this time, there were no reinforcements for either side. Revan had been delayed out-system by Mandalorian scout ships. By the time he arrived, it was too late. And beyond Malachor, there were no more Mandalorians left to die. I remember standing on the bridge with you and watching the destruction of the Republic - watching ships full of soldiers and Jedi burn and die. I remember the look you had when you turned to me. It was the longest you'd ever looked at me. You didn't say anything - just a nod. Events move quickly then, even in my dreams. Flashes, explosions, you - falling. I could feel the pain around me. And then the memory. The drifting hulks of the Mandalorian ships, the dead - allies, friends, strangers. And then the echo. Lingering. The sound I awaken to in my nightmares.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Glorious? It was nothing more than a slaughter. A slaughter caused by one of my creations.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Does anyone deserve the Mandalorians' fate? Even some of their conquered worlds fared better.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Blame lies with me, for creating it. The situation forced your hand, anger forced mine.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «You realized that unless action was taken, the fleet would be destroyed, and the Republic would fall. None of us realized the magnitude of what we unleashed.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «I was part of the war effort, yes. I worked as a technician, though.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «I could do without your Mandalorian honor. I saw the results of your "honor" - the absolute destruction your warriors brought. And look at them now. Mandalorians are little better than mercenary thugs. And what's honor to someone like that? All they care about are credits.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «You fought for no cause other than to spread suffering and pain to the people you conquered.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «I don't need compliments from a murderer.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «You did nothing but murder innocents.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «That's exactly what I'm talking about. If you ask me, you Mandalorians just got what you deserved at Malachor.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Doesn't it even bother you that your people were almost destroyed? Or do lives have no meaning to you?» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «Well, I'm glad to have you guarding my back.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- «Telos needs new ecosystems if the reclamation efforts on the planet are to succeed. Ithor, Onderon and Dxun are rich in food chains necessary to create new life on Telos and sustain it.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]
- «That'll be another death blow to Telos if no new ecosystems can be brought to the planet. The Ithorians are only part of the equation.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- «I have no love for Mandalorians, but just letting him starve up there... I think you did the right thing.» [K2/Jungle]
- «I agree. There was enough senseless death back then to last a lifetime.» [K2/Jungle]
- «You never know.» [K2/Jungle]
- «Whatever you say.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Jungle_Tomb]**
- «A type 2 perimeter motion relay. That particular model has several security vulnerabilities and design flaws. Stealth field generators can fool them. If one of us could get close to it, I know the corporate override code for it. Amateurs shouldn't even bother building security technology.» [K2/Jungle_Tomb]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- «You used to live here?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- «I've been dealing with that problem for years. The Ithorians are justifiably renowned for their ability to stabilize ecospheres, but some things are beyond even their skills.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- «If I may. I find it curious that you are looking so carefully for atmospheric sensors. But Dantooine is too placid and temperate to need them for anything except seasonal data collection.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- «I see. They aren't that expensive.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- «Your command echoes still, General. And I obey, as I did at Malachor.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Malachor.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- «Something happen?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «I'm not leaving the General behind. We either leave here together, or not at all.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «I think he wanted us to give up the General to his poorly-trained collection of bounty hunters.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «I'll take the stupid one who decided to threaten us rather than shoot us when he had the chance.» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Lost_Jedi]**
- «A strange coincidence.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «General, is there a reason you don't carry a lightsaber anymore?» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «That's not your lightsaber anymore. That belonged to someone who served Revan in the wars, not the person you are now. You could build another one - if you wanted to. But you know that.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «I don't know, General. But whatever the reason, you should put it behind you.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «The lightsaber isn't always a weapon of the Jedi, General. You don't need me to tell you that.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «I never said you were. But whatever the reason, you should put it behind you.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «I know this - a lightsaber is part of who you are. Without it, you're not complete.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «I think I can help you out there. I happen to know the parts you need.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «I spent a lot of time around Jedi during the war. None of them would let me take their lightsaber apart, but I did learn about their construction.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «We need a power cell, emitter matrix, lens and focusing crystal, though I have to admit, the crystal is beyond my means - never did understand them. Those parts are fairly common, though a Jedi once told me that it's best if your lightsaber reflects you, and if it is constructed of things that identify it as your own. Just bring the parts to me before you get started building it, I'll make sure they're usable.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «All I'm saying is that you've gone for a long time without a memory wipe. Most droids behave erratically under those circumstances.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «I know that. But I'm fixing everything else around here, so I may as well take a look at you, too.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «What was that? That's what I'm talking about - that is not normal droid behavior.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «If it wasn't for me, we'd all be floating in space! I think I can handle a few repairs to your circuits.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «I am not pushing you around. I just wanted to see if there was anything I could do to upgrade your functionality.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «Thanks for the vote of confidence.» [K2/Lost_Jedi] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Good. Now let's get started.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «Yes, I'll be careful. Now let's get started.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «You wouldn't guess it from the outside, but it looks like you've been through a lot.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «I'll bet. I'm all done with you - if anything comes loose, let me know and I'll put it back in place.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «<nowiki>[</nowiki>{{s» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «Let me see what you have.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «No, you're still missing a lens.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «That's everything. Now all you need is a little quiet time with the workbench.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «[What kind of lightsaber do you want to create?]» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «[What color focusing crystal do you use? (The focusing crystal can later be changed).]» [K2/Lost_Jedi]

**[K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]**
- «What's the problem?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «I can take a look at it, if you will permit it.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Correction - we can take a look at it, if you will permit it.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «That part's rare enough in most spaceports. Finding one here would be difficult.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- «You continually amaze me. A lot of people would've been hurt or worse.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- «This has the makings of a military council...» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- «If we needed to, Tienn may be able to modify our ship's transponder codes.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «A memory core, especially in a used astromech droid, isn't worth more than a hundred credits.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «I'm just stating a fact. It's better you hear it from me than some stranger.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Nice. It looks like it was a high-powered transmitter of some kind. The only reason for something like this is to transfer large quantities of data to a third-party.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «I used to work on droids like this in the war. Let me take a look. Droids like this always have a self-destruct system built into them at the factory. A quick adjustment here and...» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- «After you assemble it, I'll double-check your work for you.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «Looks grim. Let me try something. That's a little better. Try it now.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- «Good to have you back, General.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Easy now. You survived one spectacular crash. Lucky I was here to pull you and your friends out of that shuttle or you'd be more than a little crispy. But it's only fair, I owe you more than one, General.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «You must be in shock from the crash. Have to expect some long term memory loss from that.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Too bad she's not a droid, huh?» [K2/Restoration_Zone] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «We can't all be that lucky.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «I'll humor you, General. I was one of the Iridonian mechanic corps that was at Malachor. Bao-Dur? I can see how you'd forget me, being that I was the only one.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «I'll agree to that. The less said the better.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Don't think too hard. I'd rather not talk about the war if we could.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «We all went through some tough times after Malachor, and maybe we all did a little forgetting. Guess that's one thing we've got better than droids - they can't forget anything. But then, you give them a memory wipe and they forget for good.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «They'll be fine. The pilot's more or less unharmed and the old lady, well, she's tougher than she looks. You know, I never thought I'd see you again, General. Galaxy's a big place, and this is the last place I thought I'd bump into you. So I have to ask, just what are you doing here?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Got some news for you, General. That shuttle of yours is done for – scrap.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Well, if your ship's in as bad shape as this one, I don't think you're gonna have much luck.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Crashed a shuttle that time, too?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «They're probably already on their way.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «I had a little run-in with Czerka that involved me ending up in a force cage. I managed to escape during an electrical malfunction. So far, I've managed to outrun them.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Something went wrong underground. The ruins used to be some sort of military research center, and something reactivated its defenses. Two salvage teams never came back, so Czerka sent in a mercenary team. Later, one merc bugged out with military droids on his heels, and they started shooting up the compound.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «I don't know - I was already locked up. They thought the salvage teams accidentally turned something on, but I don't believe it.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «The power was blown out in the firefight, and that's when I got away. They sent a pursuit team after me, but most of them were busy trying to re-secure the compound.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «That's cruel, General. And anyways, you'll need my help.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «I can help you find it - I have access to the shield network. I came hoping to repair whatever damage your shuttle took, but not even I can fix that wreck.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «We'll have to get back to the compound. It's the old Ithorian research station, turned into a salvage team staging area. It won't be an easy hike.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Czerka freights men and equipment from the landing pad there. If it's there and we can breach the perimeter, there's probably something we can use. If not, we could try our luck in the underground ruins. Either way, it's back to the compound for us.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «First, there's the mercenary pursuit team looking for me. If Czerka's secured the compound, there could be a lot of them out there. Second, there's all the cannoks.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «The vicious, wide-jawed predators the Ithorians imported from Dxun. They were meant to keep the herbivore population in check.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Right. The cannoks ate them all. Now they'll eat anything they can catch, including us. Without the Ithorians to maintain the accelerated ecosystem's balance, everything here is falling apart. Czerka's killing the Restoration Project piecemeal.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «You need my help. How else are you going to get into the shield network?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Trust me, there's no way you're going to slice into the shield network. Just leave it to me.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Why, can't handle a few mercs? Don't worry, I can do the heavy lifting.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «That's the plan.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «The mercenaries have at least two hidden caches like this. We should keep our eyes open.» [K2/Restoration_Zone] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Mercenaries, right where we need to go. That sentry droid probably spotted us already.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «They were probably looking for me when they saw your shuttle go down.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Who do you think shot you down in the first place?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Another sentry droid. The mercenaries must be using them to locate us. There's probably another patrol nearby.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Wait, bounty? Why is there a bounty on your head?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «I don't think so. You'll have to come through me if you want to take the General.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «I always feel a sense of calm when I walk the surface of Telos. The Ithorians are truly amazing in their work.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Quiet! There's a large mercenary patrol up ahead.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Look! There's a large mercenary patrol in the valley up ahead.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «If we move carefully along the perimeter, we may be able to get by without their spotting us. We could cross along the shore, or head along the cliffs to the south.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «I'll follow close.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Bold. All right, let's do it.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- «The construction of this door is like nothing I've seen on Telos... and it is not the same as the rest of the facility, here.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I... am sorry, General. I must have lost consciousness in the crash.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I am fine, General. Even power has been restored to my arm.» [K2/Secret_Academy] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I will, General. The attack was unexpected, but I will not let down my guard again.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I will not, General. The attack was unexpected, but I will not let down my guard again.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «What is this place? Where are we?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «This must be where I had detected the energy readings before - and the drain to the restoration shields. This room, this place - it looks part of a huge polar irrigation system, possibly planet-wide, like the one on Coruscant. I had been told by the Republic that it was not in use.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Of course, General. If you wish, I may travel with you or join you at the ship.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I am, General. If you wish I may travel with you, or join you at the ship.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Very well, General. I will await your arrival.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Very well, General.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «This place looks part of a huge polar irrigation system, possibly planet-wide, like the one on Coruscant. I had been told by the Republic that it was not in use.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- «Hey, what's the commotion here?» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «You're threatening Atton with a lightsaber, and I'm supposed to just stay out of it? No!» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Apathy is death.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- «Someone did quite a job on this door. Reinforced and welded shut! We're not getting through, that's for sure.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Sith_Tomb]**
- «Whoever the architect was that made that door, he or she was quite competent. Mandalorian iron, reinforced joints. Whatever is behind it isn't meant to be disturbed. Freedon Nadd's resting place must be beyond it.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]

**[K2/Sky_Ramp]**
- «The force shields won't present a problem. I can get us through them.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]

**[K2/Underground_Base]**
- «This arm of mine isn't just for show, General. Stand back.» [K2/Underground_Base]
- «The Czerka mercenaries were a little surprised when I broke my way out of my holding cell. The shields there were even weaker than these. After you.» [K2/Underground_Base]
- «[Bao-Dur has the ability to break through various shields with his repulsor fist.]» [K2/Underground_Base]
- «There she is. One orbital shuttle... looks like it's in serviceable condition. That's all a moot point, though.» [K2/Underground_Base]
- «The hangar bay doors are closed. I don't fancy flying the shuttle through solid metal, so I'd say we need to find a way to get them open. We'll also need to find the ignition codes for the shuttle, or else we'll have some trouble getting off the ground. If we get all that, I wouldn't worry about what shape this heap is in. I'll get it running.» [K2/Underground_Base]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Bao-Dur (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Depths]**
- G0-T0: «Touching. The probability of the Iridonian installing triggered commands within your core was high; I see the probabilities have played out. Of course, the probability that I would do the same is equally high. Your inability to move right now is evidence of that. If the General issues the command, only I will be here to receive it. You realize I cannot permit you... or the exile... to activate the mass shadow generator here on Malachor. In that, your programming and mine... conflict. And since you have no offensive weaponry to speak of, the probability of your programming overwriting mine is low. You must understand that the General would not wish the relics or the Sith strength here on Malachor to be compromised. Their presence is needed to stabilize the galaxy. Without them, the galaxy would be reduced to anarchy within years. And if there is anything I can't stand, it's an untidy galaxy. So, let us wait here, you and I, for the General's orders, and the fate of the galaxy shall wait with us.» [K2/Depths]

**[K2/Ebon_Hawk]**
- G0-T0: «Iridonian, if I might have a moment?» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- HK-47: «Statement: If I require a similar diagnostic in the future, I shall seek out the Iridonian.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]

**[K2/G0-T0]**
- G0-T0: «Oh, how exciting. I can feel the slow charge of fear building in my motivators. If you have any further wild delusions you feel compelled to share with me, please - share it with the Iridonian's irritating little remote instead.» [K2/G0-T0]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Handmaiden: «That is untrue. You and the Iridonian trust each other... or at least, the Iridonian trusts you. We heard much of the Iridonian when we served Atris. Atris believed that the Iridonian held the knowledge to restoring Telos.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Jungle_Landing]**
- Remote Droid: «This remote droid is damaged but the remote computer core is now active. With repairs it could be operational again.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]
- Remote Droid: «[Failure] The mapping functions of the remote droid return only errors. It cannot be put on patrol mode. It is locked in fire support mode.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]

**[K2/Residential_082_West]**
- Chodo Habat: «I have heard that the ship you arrived on is missing, hidden somewhere on Telos. I know one who could help you find it, and a means by which you could travel to him. When Citadel Station was developing the shield system it uses to protect the Restoration Zones, they worked with a Zabrak ex-military engineer named Bao-Dur. Bao-Dur designed and oversaw the installation of the shield system's planet-side components. His knowledge of Telos' surface and the shield grid is unparalleled. If there is anyone who could locate your ship on Telos' surface, it is he. He is a friend, and may be trusted.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Bao-Dur should be on Telos' surface. I believe he is at one of the currently Czerka-held Restoration Zones, RZ-0031.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «An orbital freighter is waiting for you in Bay II, Dock Module 126. Bao-Dur should be in RZ-0031. He'll help you find this ship of yours.» [K2/Residential_082_West]

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- Atton: «We could try handing the Zabrak over. You know, bargaining chip?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «Yes, mistress: the Iridonian, the Echani-trained pilot, the exile, and the old woman.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- Kreia: «Stay out of this Bao-Dur! This is a personal dispute between Atton and myself.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

### Quote bank B2: pronoun references resolved from context (context-resolved, hand-reviewed scene-wide (each line read by hand against the whole conversation; anchor = the line that names the character))
- [context-resolved] G0-T0: «I have spent some time in the presence of your remote, and the upgrades you have performed on him are quite adequate... I am impressed with your work, though less so with your remote itself.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk] (anchor: G0-T0: Iridonian, if I might have a moment?...)
- [context-resolved] G0-T0: «I find his use of resources - energy spent on frivolous things - to be an unsightly waste. But it is obvious you have some skill, however slight, in the upgrading of machines. I want you to provide me with similar upgrades.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk] (anchor: G0-T0: Iridonian, if I might have a moment?...)
- [context-resolved] Handmaiden: «Yes. His skill with machines is something beyond which most can aspire to. His shield technology surpasses the designs of even the most skilled of Echani power architects.» [K2/Handmaiden] (anchor: Atris thought Bao-Dur was the key to saving Telos?...)
- [context-resolved] Handmaiden: «I will respect your wishes. And his. Forgive me.» [K2/Handmaiden] (anchor: Atris thought Bao-Dur was the key to saving Telos?...)
- [context-resolved] Chodo Habat: «He has been our ally in our struggle against Czerka. If you tell him that I sent you, he should be willing to help.» [K2/Residential_082_West] (anchor: Chodo Habat: Bao-Dur should be on Telos' surface. I believe ...)
- [context-resolved] Chodo Habat: «He grew weary of the Telosian government's reluctance to quickly resolve our contract dispute with Czerka. Perhaps as a Zabrak, he does not have our patience. I have not seen him since.» [K2/Residential_082_West] (anchor: Chodo Habat: Bao-Dur should be on Telos' surface. I believe ...)

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to Bao-Dur (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K2/Czerka_Site]**
- Atton: «Why do you say that?» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- Mercenary Commander: «What do we have here... the 'Jedi.' Saves us the trouble of looking for ya. Corrun Falt did say you were dangerous... maybe he does know what he's talking about.» [K2/Czerka_Site]

**[K2/Dantooine]**
- Saedhe: «Hmm, that's just a great attitude. Sorry to waste your time.» [K2/Dantooine]
- Saedhe: «Ehhh... It's just important we get it back. They're expensive pieces of equipment and we can't afford to replace them. Regardless, I don't suppose you'd be willing to keep an eye out for something?» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Ebon_Hawk]**
- G0-T0: «Yes, I do have a few moments to spare for your work. I would like to know what he is doing here, though.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- G0-T0: «I suppose not. Perhaps in working on my circuitry, your assistant will learn something about how a fully-functional droid is constructed.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- G0-T0: «I would appreciate that. Our group has little in the way of time to spare, and I would not want to delay you from your other duties.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- G0-T0: «As I told you, my design is streamlined and efficient, though I am pleased that you were able to make some improvements, and this was not just a waste of my valuable time.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]
- G0-T0: «I see... Well, thank you. I'll let you get back to your work.» [K2/Ebon_Hawk]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- Dahnis: «That was... magnificent. Thank you for such a stimulating game.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Handmaiden: «The cargo hold is enough. I assure you, there is little I need. I will attend to myself.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Influence]**
- Atton: «Let's not make this a habit - I don't want your good nature rubbing off on me.» [K2/Influence]
- Atton: «I don't understand what that accomplished, but if it makes you feel better, fine.» [K2/Influence]
- Disciple: «Maybe fewer people would distrust Jedi if all of them behaved as you did.» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «I am not speaking to you, fool. Be silent!» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- Xaart: «You've done a tremendous service for the Republic. Now I have to catch the first shuttle out of here. May the Force be with you!» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Mandalore: «So, you fought against the Mandalorians in the war?» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Mandalore: «But you fought on the front lines. To a Mandalorian, there is honor in that.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Mandalore: «If I were you, I'd pick your words more carefully.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Mandalore: «Maybe that's what it looked like to you, but that isn't why we fought. We fought for honor and glory in the heat of battle.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- Beast-rider: «Although some of your terms are unknown to me, what you say is true.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- Handmaiden: «I will have to think on what you've said.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- Atton: «Did you really used to live around here?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Pato Ado: «If I understand your question correctly, then it is a difficult to answer. Ecosphere forces are hard to disrupt, but sentients have terrible power.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Pato Ado: «Your compliment is appreciated. But in order to fix these things Ithorians must learn an ecosphere's innermost workings. And that eludes me here.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Saedhe: «I do. The Kinrath are dangerous, but as long as you stay on your swoop bike there's no way they should catch you. I've carefully searched this area and haven't found anything. So maybe our man just got sloppy. We really need those atmospheric sensors.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Saedhe: «I don't suppose you've found any of those atmospheric sensors?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Saedhe: «Regardless, have you found any of 'em yet?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Kreia: «Now do you hear me? Truly hear me.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- Visas: «What do you mean?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Atton: «The truce between the bounty hunters in Nar Shaddaa is off. There's going to be war. A trap in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr is bad enough, but having a hundred bounty hunters on your back is something else.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Atton: «Yeah... you're right. But I'm guessing we're in a lot more trouble than she is.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Atton: «Ah. well that would explain it. Which one do you want?» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]**
- Zuka: «If you think you can help, there's the broken cables right over there, and the relay maintenance structure's behind me. You can find the telemetry computer in there.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- Handmaiden: «Your actions have averted disaster here. That was an admirable use of the Jedi teachings.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Onderon Soldier: «I don't know what you did, but thank you, off-worlder. That was too close for me.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Tienn Tubb: «Eh? What is this "big-ears?" Compliments will not get you a discount.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «[Success] You have bypassed the meager security lock-outs. Lupo must have considered the shield adequate protection.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «[Failure] You cannot find an obvious way to sabotage the droid.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- 3: «What are you talking about?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- 2,: «Looking for you? Maybe we shouldn't have you along.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Rodian: «What luck! We did not expect to see you again.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Rodian: «We thought our situation being stuck here on the planet's surface would keep us from collecting on the price Goto has placed on your head. But now you've so thoughtfully delivered yourself to us. We would be remiss to allow this opportunity to pass us by a second time.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Rodian: «Don't you know? The Exchange is paying handsomely for the head of a Jedi knight. Your friend here, just happens to be one. But that's of little importance. All that matters is that we take them back to Nar Shaddaa, and collect.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Atton: «Amazing at wasting the Republic's credits, maybe. But now that you mention it, I think I feel it too. Like a weight's been lifted off my shoulders.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Kreia: «I see. Even that may be a good sign, perhaps what you feel is the emptiness of the planet, its pain. We should press on.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- Atton: «No, I won't allow her to corrupt you! I will protect you, even from yourself.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Tomb]**
- Mira: «I think all the soldiers and beasts are trying to keep us out of that door. Must be something important. I don't suppose I could talk you out of getting in there? I thought not. The door is shut tight. Somewhere down here there's got to be a way to open it.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]

**[K2/Sky_Ramp]**
- Captain Bostuco: «You can? That will save us precious minutes.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]

**[K2/Underground_Base]**
- Telos Orbital Shuttle: «[This old orbital shuttle doesn't have a hyperdrive, but it can get you out of the restoration zone... once you have the ignition passcodes and open the underground hangar doors above you.]» [K2/Underground_Base]

## HK-47 (KOTOR 2)

Meatbag contempt.
